The Cuttlebot has four primary artificial muscles in its fins in addition to a tentacle-inspired soft gripper. The robot was evaluated in a series of tethered and untethered swimming tests, demonstrating a top speed of 2.5 centimeters per second translation and 10 degrees per second rotation. Furthermore, the CORE system was capable of driving specialized control signals into the artificial muscles to controllably output force and torque in six axes. This work provides a platform for developing complex, bio-inspired swimming robots for ocean exploration and monitoring, laying the foundation with our leading example: the Cuttlebot.

</details>


### [55] [Series-Parallel Integrated Nonlinear Elastic Actuator applied to the lean motion of a bicycle simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00201)
*Christina Kohler,Michiel Plooij,Nuria Peña-Perez,Arend L. Schwab,Heike Vallery*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPINEA，一种将串联弹性执行器（SEA）和并联弹性执行器（PEA）融合的创新设计，通过单个非线性弹性元件同时实现串并联功能，在触觉交互应用中实现高峰值扭矩和高精度力控制，并在自行车骑行模拟器上验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高扭矩、高保真触觉交互的机器人设计具有挑战性；现有PEA和SEA各有优势但需独立弹性元件或离合机构才能结合，缺乏紧凑高效的集成方案。

Method: SPINEA采用非线性传动结构，使电机和负载的旋转轴不对齐并通过弹性元件连接，单个弹性元件同时发挥串联和并联双重作用，实现峰值扭矩和精确扭矩跟踪的兼顾。

Result: 在触觉自行车模拟器实验中，SPINEA实现了低阻抗和精确扭矩跟踪：自行车框架固定时可达4.25 Hz，有骑行者时可达4 Hz。

Conclusion: SPINEA通过单个非线性弹性元件融合SEA和PEA，在紧凑结构中实现了高保真触觉交互所需的高性能和精度，可推广至其他需要紧凑型高性能执行器的应用场景。

Abstract: Designing robots for high-torque, high-fidelity haptic interaction is challenging. Parallel Elastic Actuators (PEAs) use elastic elements in parallel to smaller motors to complement torques, and Series Elastic Actuators (SEAs) use elastic elements in series to decouple motor impedance and improve force control. Recent work combines SEAs and PEAs to obtain both benefits but requires separate elastic elements or clutching. This paper presents the Series Parallel Integrated Nonlinear Elastic Actuator (SPINEA), which merges SEA and PEA such that a single elastic element takes on dual roles simultaneously, parallel and series. This is achieved by a nonlinear transmission in which the motor and load have misaligned rotation axes and are elastically connected. This geometry enables both high peak torque and precise torque tracking. We apply SPINEA to actuate lean of a haptic bicycle simulator, which requires high moments and precise rendering for safe and realistic rider interactions. We realized a prototype and performed experiments, both with an external excitation setup and with riders cycling. Our results confirm SPINEA's low impedance and precise torque tracking, up to 4.25 Hz with the bicycle frame fixed and up to 4 Hz with riders. The benefits may transfer to other applications requiring compact, high-performance actuation.

</details>


### [56] [Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00229)
*Yueh-Hua Wu,Tatsuya Matsushima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出连续推理（Continuous Reasoning）方法，用共享的高斯潜变量接口替代传统自然语言作为VLA模型的推理媒介，通过自验证训练目标实现可共享、可验证的连续行动推理，在LIBERO-PRO和真实机器人上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言作为推理媒介与连续控制的粒度不匹配：语言和显式子目标在任务级粒度操作，而VLA策略需要在更精细的时间尺度上选择动作，导致单个推理步跨越多个动作块且与当前动作弱耦合。因此需要探索VLA中合适的推理媒介形式。

Method: 提出Continuous Reasoning模型：(1) 预测结构化连续思维集作为连续推理；(2) 将其作为共享上下文用于块结构动作生成；(3) 实例化为共享高斯潜变量接口；(4) 使用自验证训练目标：指数移动平均教师模型必须成功消费学生的推理来预测目标动作。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO上提升鲁棒性；在真实机器人上，TX-G2变体（AgiBot G2兼容）平均子任务成功率比π0.5提升40.4%，HSR上提升26.3%。

Conclusion: VLA中的推理不在于增加额外的token，而在于构建可共享、可验证的内部动作语言。

Abstract: Natural language is a powerful reasoning medium for language and vision-language models, but it is mismatched to the granularity of continuous control. Text and explicit subgoals operate at task-level granularity, whereas vision-language-action (VLA) policies must choose actions at a much finer temporal scale; a single reasoning step can therefore span many action chunks while remaining only weakly coupled to the action needed now. This suggests a different question for VLA: what should play the role of language? We argue that a useful VLA reasoning medium must be shareable across model instances, verifiable through downstream action improvement, and aligned with temporally extended control structure.
  Based on this view, we propose Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action. Our model first predicts continuous reasoning in the form of a structured set of continuous thoughts, then reuses them as shared context for chunk-structured action generation. Better action prediction alone does not certify good reasoning: if the same internal medium cannot be shared across model instances and independently verified through improved downstream control, the added latent may simply become a model-private shortcut that helps on seen behaviors without supporting generalizable control. We therefore instantiate continuous reasoning as a shared Gaussian latent interface and train it with a self-verification objective in which an exponential-moving-average teacher must successfully consume the student's reasoning when predicting target actions.
  Empirically, Continuous Reasoning improves LIBERO-PRO robustness and performs strongly on real robots, raising mean subtask success over π0.5 by 40.4% on TX-G2, an AgiBot G2-compatible variant, and 26.3% on HSR. This suggests that reasoning in VLA is less about extra tokens than about a shareable, verifiable internal language for action.

</details>


### [57] [HOIST: Humanoid Optimization with Imitation and Sample-efficient Tuning for Manipulating Suspended Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00252)
*Songyang Liu,Shunyu Yao,Dingyuan Huang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOIST通过先微调视觉-语言-动作策略（VLA）从VR遥操作演示中学习，再由全身控制器执行命令，最后使用VLA rollout和迭代批量强化学习优化，实现人形机器人操控悬挂负载，相比纯模仿学习将平移放置误差降低19.9厘米，角度误差降低3.56度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人操控欠驱动悬挂负载具有挑战性，因为只能通过全身运动和间歇接触影响振荡负载；模仿学习提供安全初始行为但无法直接优化最终放置，而从头强化学习在真实人形机器人上不安全且样本效率低。

Method: HOIST包含三个阶段：1) 从VR遥操作演示微调高层VLA策略；2) 通过全身控制器执行VLA命令；3) 利用VLA rollout和迭代批量强化学习（RL）优化放置精度和停止行为。

Result: 在仿真和真实人形机器人实验中，HOIST优于纯模仿学习和额外演示基线；相比纯VLA rollout，平移放置误差降低19.9厘米，原始角度误差降低3.56度。

Conclusion: HOIST展示了人形机器人在欠驱动物料搬运任务中的潜力，证明了结合模仿学习和样本高效强化学习调优的有效性。

Abstract: Manipulating suspended payloads with humanoid robots is challenging because the robot can only influence an underactuated, oscillatory load through whole-body motion and intermittent contact. Imitation learning provides safe initial behavior but does not directly optimize final placement, while reinforcement learning from scratch is unsafe and sample-inefficient on real humanoids. We present HOIST-Humanoid Optimized with Imitation and Sample-efficient Tuning for manipulating suspended loads. HOIST first finetunes a high-level vision-language-action (VLA) policy from virtual-reality (VR) teleoperation demonstrations and executes its commands through a whole-body controller. It then uses VLA rollouts and iterative batched RL to improve placement accuracy and stopping behavior. Experiments in simulation and on a real humanoid show that HOIST improves over imitation-only and additional-demonstration baselines; compared with pure VLA rollouts, HOIST reduces translational placement error by 19.9 cm and raw angular error by 3.56 degrees, demonstrating the potential of humanoids for underactuated material-handling tasks.

</details>


### [58] [Per-Group Error, Not Total MSE: Fine-Tuning Vision-Language-Action Models for 11-DoF Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00253)
*Pau Montagut Bofi,Mario García Blasco,Tessa Pulli,Markus Vincze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在异构关节空间的移动操作器上微调VLA模型时，总MSE最低的检查点并不一定在真实机器人上表现最佳。原因是将不同关节组（手臂、夹爪、头部、轮式底盘）的误差合并为单一指标会掩盖易失败关节的问题。通过实验表明，按关节组分别评估的误差比总MSE更能可靠地选择检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型微调时常用总MSE作为评估指标，但在异构关节空间的机器人（如11自由度HSR）上，不同关节组预测难度差异大，总MSE可能掩盖某些关节组仍存在高误差的问题，导致选择实际表现不佳的检查点。

Method: 在11-DoF Toyota HSR上微调SmolVLA（450M，仅训练动作专家），并对比预训练更强的π_0.5（3.3B）。分别按关节组分析误差模式，并设置ExP（仅训练动作头，骨干冻结）和Full（全参数微调）两种设置。最终在60次真实机器人试验（每组20次）中评估。

Result: 1) SmolVLA中移动基座收敛最慢，限制整体性能；2) ExP微调π_0.5时总MSE低于基线，但手臂精度下降；3) 真实机器人试验中，π_0.5 80k（4.0/4）显著优于两种微调变体（ExP 3k: 3.75/4; HSR-SmolVLA: 3.5/4），尽管ExP 3k总MSE最低；4) 真实性能与离线手臂组误差最相关，而非总MSE或基座组误差。

Conclusion: 在异构动作空间的机器人上，按关节组分别评估的误差（per-group error）比总MSE更可靠，应作为检查点选择的主要信号。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for mobile manipulators with heterogeneous joint spaces can produce a counterintuitive result: the checkpoint with the lowest aggregate MSE is not the one that performs best on the real robot. We argue this is a predictable consequence of collapsing heterogeneous joint groups (arm, gripper, head, wheeled base) into a single metric, where easy-to-predict joints can mask joints that still fail.
  We fine-tune SmolVLA (450M, action-expert only) on the 11-DoF Toyota HSR and compare it against $π_{0.5}$ (3.3B), a stronger pretrained baseline. Per-group analysis exposes two patterns: in SmolVLA, the mobile base converges slowest and limits overall performance. In expert-only fine-tuning of $π_{0.5}$ (training only the action head, backbone frozen), total MSE drops below the baseline but arm accuracy degrades. On 60 real-robot trials (20 per model), $π_{0.5}$ 80k (4.0/4) significantly outperforms both fine-tuned variants (expert-only 3k: 3.75/4; HSR-SmolVLA: 3.5/4; Mann-Whitney $p \leq 0.010$), despite expert-only 3k having the lowest total MSE. This separation is most consistent with the offline arm-group error, not total MSE or base-group error. We conclude that per-group error is a more reliable signal than total MSE for checkpoint selection on robots with heterogeneous action spaces. Code: https://github.com/paumontagut/per-group-mse-vla

</details>


### [59] [ScaRF-SLAM: Scale-Consistent Reconstruction with Feed-Forward Models and Classical Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00307)
*Yuhao Zhang,Yifu Tao,Frank Dellaert,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦框架，将经典特征SLAM与几何基础模型(GFM)结合：SLAM模块负责鲁棒的低延迟跟踪，GFM仅用于建图。通过将建图锚定于SLAM位姿、跨深度尺度优化、子图尺度校正及投影点云融合等策略，避免GFM预测误差影响位姿估计，在建筑物尺度和户外数据集上均实现了更优的轨迹精度和重建精度（10-20%提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作直接将几何基础模型(GFM)的预测用于跟踪，但由于模型能力和不确定性问题，GFM的几何不准确会负面影响位姿估计。作者旨在解决这一局限性，使得系统能同时获得高质量密集重建和鲁棒的低延迟跟踪。

Method: 提出解耦框架：1) 经典视觉SLAM负责低延迟跟踪；2) GFM仅用于建图，避免预测误差传播到位姿估计；3) 建图锚定于SLAM位姿并进行跨深度尺度优化；4) 构建子图，通过轻量级帧和子图尺度优化保证尺度一致性；5) 子图内进行基于投影的点云融合，并在线更新子图以反映SLAM轨迹更新。

Result: 在建筑尺度室内数据集上，轨迹精度更优，重建精度提升10%-20%，每10米块约2 cm重建误差；在大型户外数据集上，每30米块达到10 cm误差（相对于LiDAR真值模型）。

Conclusion: 解耦SLAM与GFM的建图和跟踪角色，能够有效避免GFM不准确性对位姿估计的影响，在保持鲁棒跟踪的同时实现高质量密集重建，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Recent works have explored unifying SLAM with geometric foundation models (GFMs). However, directly using GFM predictions for tracking is highly sensitive to model capability and uncertainty, as geometric inaccuracies in the predictions can adversely affect pose estimation. To address this limitation, we present a decoupled framework that integrates classical feature-based SLAM with GFMs, which achieves higher quality and more consistent dense reconstruction. In brief, we use classical visual SLAM for robust low-latency tracking and use GFMs exclusively for mapping. By anchoring mapping to poses produced by the SLAM module and optimizing across depth scales, the proposed design avoids propagating inaccuracies from GFM predictions into pose estimation while imposing geometric constraints on the reconstruction. The system builds submaps from multiple posed keyframes and enforces scale consistency via lightweight frame and submap scale optimization. It also performs projection-based point cloud fusion within each submap, and updates submaps online to reflect trajectory updates from the feature-based SLAM. To evaluate tracking and reconstruction of our method, we introduce a loop-rich, building-scale indoor dataset with accurate sensor trajectories and LiDAR ground-truth. Experiments show that our approach achieves superior trajectory accuracy while improving reconstruction precision by 10%-20% over existing methods, with about 2 cm reconstruction error per 10 m chunk on building-scale dataset. On large-scale outdoor datasets, it attains 10 cm error per 30 m chunk (w.r.t LiDAR ground-truth models).

</details>


### [60] [DRL-Based Pose Control for Double-Ackermann Robots Under Actuation Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00313)
*Oussama Zaim,Mélodie Daniel,Aly Magassouba,Miguel Aranda,Olivier Ly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对双阿克曼转向移动机器人的位姿控制问题，本文提出一种sim-to-sim-to-real方法，通过在PyBullet训练环境中引入Gazebo观测到的驱动效应，结合多环境DRL（SAC和CrossQ），将仿真到实物的策略迁移成功率从25%提升至92%（Gazebo），并在真实机器人上成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略在真实机器人上部署时面临仿真与现实动力学差异的挑战，双阿克曼转向移动机器人的非完整约束进一步加剧了这一问题。现有工作ManeuverNet仅实现位置控制，且简化驱动模型导致策略泛化能力差（成功率从100%降至25%），亟需弥合仿真与真实之间的性能差距。

Method: 1) 将ManeuverNet的目标从位置控制扩展至完整位姿控制；2) 采用sim-to-sim-to-real范式，将Gazebo中观测到的驱动效应（如致动不确定性）融入PyBullet训练环境；3) 使用多环境深度强化学习，结合SAC和CrossQ算法训练鲁棒策略。

Result: 在Gazebo中实现92%的成功率（严苛阈值下69%），相较于直接迁移的25%有显著提升；成功迁移至真实机器人，无需额外调参。

Conclusion: 通过跨仿真器的驱动效应建模和多环境训练，可以有效缩小仿真到实物的性能差距，提升非完整约束移动机器人DRL策略的鲁棒部署能力。

Abstract: Robust deployment of deep reinforcement learning (DRL) policies on real robots remains challenging due to discrepancies between simulation and real-world dynamics. We address this issue in the context of maneuvering with double-Ackermann-steering mobile robots, which introduce additional constraints due to their non-holonomic nature. Building upon the DRL framework ManeuverNet, we extend its objective from position control to full pose control, resulting in a more challenging task. We further investigate the impact of actuation-related uncertainties on policy transfer. The use of simplified actuation models during training of the extended policy can lead to poor generalization, shown by a success rate drop from 100% in PyBullet to 25% in Gazebo under stricter evaluation conditions. To address this limitation, we adopt a sim-to-sim-to-real approach, where actuation effects observed in Gazebo are incorporated into the PyBullet training environment. Using multi-environment DRL with SAC and CrossQ, we learn policies that remain robust despite modeling inaccuracies. This approach can significantly reduce the performance gap across simulators, achieving up to 92% success rate in Gazebo and maintaining 69% under stricter thresholds, with successful transfer to a real robot without additional tuning.

</details>


### [61] [Belief Consistency Between Foundation-Model Evidence and Geometric Perception in Persistent Robotic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00318)
*Christoffer Heckman,Harel Biggie,Brendan Crowe,Nicholas Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双通道语义地图更新算子，通过逐类校准提交门和每事件冲突丢弃窗口，解决了基础模型通道与几何通道在自主机器人建图中可靠性不一致和矛盾冲突的问题。在KITTI-360和ScanNet数据集上，该方法显著提升了建图精度（KITTI上轿车提交精度从43.9%提升至99.7%，平均每类IoU从0.180提升至0.522），优于仅校准算子和组合式VLM提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主机器人建图系统将几何感知通道（具有良好表征的断言）与基础模型通道（产生语义断言但缺乏校准可靠性）融合时，通常将基础模型通道视为额外的投票者融入逐元素后验，但这一做法既未针对逐类可靠性进行校准，也缺乏在几何通道与基础模型通道产生矛盾时的检测与处理机制，导致建图精度和可靠性不足。

Method: 提出一种双机制更新算子：(1) 逐类校准提交门（per-class calibrated commit gate），对基础模型输出的每类语义断言进行可靠性校准；(2) 每事件冲突丢弃窗口（per-event conflict-drop window），当基础模型断言在当前时刻与几何通道产生矛盾时，拒绝提交该断言。在KITTI-360和ScanNet上，使用oracle几何通道（全景真值）和离线语义分割器Mask2Former进行实验验证。

Result: KITTI上轿车提交精度从43.9%（仅校准算子）提升至99.7%；平均每类IoU从0.180提升至0.522；保留更多组合式真阳例且精度高于组合式VLM提示方法。该框架在oracle和离线分割器几何通道上均达到部署质量，且对基础模型替换具有不变性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双通道语义地图更新算子有效解决了基础模型与几何通道之间的可靠性差异和矛盾冲突问题，显著提升了自主机器人建图的语义精度和可靠性，具备良好的泛化性和模型无关性。

Abstract: Persistent maps used by autonomous robots increasingly fuse a geometric perception stack whose assertions are well-characterized with a foundation-model channel that produces semantic claims without calibrated reliability about the same scene. Contemporary mapping systems integrate the two channels by treating the foundation-model channel as an additional voter into a per-element posterior, uncalibrated for its own per-class reliability and without machinery to flag when the two channels contradict each other at a given moment. We propose an update operator with two cooperating mechanisms: a per-class calibrated commit gate, and a per-event conflict-drop window that refuses to commit foundation-model claims contradicted by the geometric channel at the moment of the claim. We evaluate on KITTI-360 and ScanNet, with an oracle geometric channel (panoptic ground truth) and an off-the-shelf online semantic segmenter (Mask2Former) to demonstrate real-world performance. The operator produces substantially more accurate committed maps (KITTI is car commit precision 99.7% vs. 43.9% for the calibration-only operator; mean per-class IoU 0.522 vs. 0.180), retains more compositional true positives at higher precision than a monolithic compositional VLM prompt. The framework operates at deployment quality across both oracle and off-the-shelf-segmenter geometric channels, and is invariant under foundation-model substitution.

</details>


### [62] [FAIR^2 Drones: An AI-Ready Standard for Cross-Domain Wildlife Drone Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00355)
*Jenna Kline,Kilian Meier,Vandita Shukla,Edouard G. A. Rolland,Elena Iannino,Lucie Laporte-Devylder,Constanza Andrea Molina Catricheo,Blair Costelloe,Elizabeth Campolongo,Henrik S. Midtiby,Devis Tuia,Benjamin Risse,Ulrik P. S. Lundquist,Anders Lyhne Christensen,Fabio Remondino,Thomas Richardson,Tanya Berger-Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAIR² Drones标准，通过统一数据集格式和元数据规范，使无人机采集的动物生态数据能够同时服务于生态学、机器人学和计算机视觉三个领域，最大化数据复用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机收集的动物生态数据投入巨大，但现有数据集通常只服务于单一研究社区，限制了跨学科复用，亟需一个统一标准来桥接生态学、机器人学和计算机视觉。

Method: 在现有FAIR和AI-ready数据框架基础上，增加平台元数据和标注规范，构建统一的数据集标准FAIR² Drones，并提供开源验证工具、参考实现以及多模态扩展（链接无人机影像与相机陷阱、GPS、声学等传感器）。

Result: 该标准使数据集能同时支持生态分析、机器人算法开发和计算机视觉基准测试，提升了昂贵野外部署数据的科学回报。

Conclusion: 跨学科元数据标准化能最大化无人机生态数据的复用价值，加速环境监测领域的跨域协作。

Abstract: Animal ecology data collection using drones represents a substantial investment of time, expertise, and financial resources. Yet most existing datasets serve only a single research community, limiting interdisciplinary reuse. We propose a unified drone dataset standard, FAIR^2 Drones, that bridges ecology, robotics, and computer vision by building on existing FAIR and AI-ready data frameworks while adding essential platform metadata and annotation specifications. Our standard enables datasets to simultaneously support ecological analysis, robotics algorithm development, and computer vision benchmarking. We provide open-source validation tools, reference implementations, and multimodal extensions linking drone imagery with complementary sensors such as camera traps, GPS, and acoustics. By standardizing metadata across disciplines, this framework maximizes the scientific return on investment for costly field deployments and accelerates cross-domain collaboration in environmental monitoring.

</details>


### [63] [Constrained Whole-Body Tracking for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00374)
*Daniel Morton,Pranit Mohnot,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了ConstrainedMimic框架，结合操作空间控制和控制障碍函数，在强化学习跟踪策略中实现实时约束满足，支持运行时任意约束（碰撞避免、关节极限、质心稳定等），可部署在CPU/GPU/TPU上，频率达300-500Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在人形机器人全身敏捷控制中表现优异，但难以确保安全性及满足训练后指定的约束条件，需要一种能够在运行时动态施加约束的框架。

Method: 将操作空间控制（Operational Space Control）与控制障碍函数（CBFs）的原理整合到RL跟踪策略中，利用全身运动学和动力学信息，在保持当前接触模态和跟踪目标的前提下，实时施加约束。

Result: 在仿真Unitree G1人形机器人上通过全身运动跟踪和遥操作实验，验证了碰撞避免（机器人与外部障碍物）、关节极限和质心稳定性等约束的有效性，且约束激活时对策略能力的限制最小化。系统运行频率达300-500Hz。

Conclusion: ConstrainedMimic能够在不影响RL策略核心跟踪能力的前提下，灵活施加运行时任意约束，为安全关键的人形机器人控制提供了一种高效、可微、跨平台部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in reinforcement learning (RL) have demonstrated impressive whole-body agility for humanoid robots, yet ensuring safety and satisfying constraints -- particularly those specified after training -- remains a challenge. Towards this goal, we present ConstrainedMimic, a control framework that leverages whole-body kinematics and dynamics for real-time constraint enforcement within RL tracking policies. By integrating principles from operational space control and control barrier functions (CBFs), we enable the satisfaction of arbitrary runtime constraints on both the kinematic reference motion and the underlying dynamics. In whole-body motion-tracking and teleoperation experiments on a (simulated) Unitree G1 with a learned policy, we demonstrate collision avoidance (both with the robot body and external obstacles), joint limits, and center of mass stability constraints. By remaining consistent with the current contact mode and tracking objectives, we minimally restrict the capabilities of the policy when constraints are active. Our method is fully differentiable, runs on CPU, GPU, and TPU, and can be deployed at up to 300-500 Hz. All software will be freely available upon publication.

</details>


### [64] [Behavior Cloning of MPC for 3-DOF Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00383)
*Theo Guegan,Dexter Wen Jie Teo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究使用行为克隆（Behavior Cloning）近似模型预测控制（MPC）策略，实现对3自由度机械臂的实时控制。实验表明，静态神经网络架构（Deep MLP）优于时序模型（RNN），在推理延迟降低3倍的同时实现了84.98%的成功率，但严格容差下存在精度差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC虽然具有强稳定性和鲁棒性，但其计算负担过重，难以满足实时系统需求。本文旨在通过行为克隆学习MPC策略，从而在保持控制性能的同时大幅降低计算复杂度。

Method: 以结合逆运动学与MPC的控制器为基线，训练多种神经网络架构（包括经典回归算法、Deep MLP、RNN）作为MPC的替代策略。通过在线和离线评估，从精度、计算效率和策略保真度三个维度进行性能比较。

Result: 行为克隆成功将推理延迟降低3倍，在宽松容差下达到84.98%的成功率。静态架构（Deep MLP）优于时序架构（RNN），表明瞬时状态观测足以完成该控制任务。但在严格容差下存在精度差距。

Conclusion: 行为克隆能有效降低3自由度机械臂MPC策略的计算负担，捕获全局最优轨迹，但需要进一步研究以减小终端稳态误差，弥合严格容差下的精度差距。

Abstract: While Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides strong stability and robustness, it imposes a significant computational burden on real-time systems. This paper investigates the application of Behavior Cloning to approximate MPC policies for the real-time control of a 3-degree-of-freedom robotic manipulator. We present a baseline controller combining Inverse Kinematics with MPC and evaluate neural network architectures, ranging from classical regression algorithms to deep learning models including Deep MLPs and RNNs, to derive computationally efficient surrogate policies. We analyze generalization capabilities, stability considerations, and the trade-offs inherent in different architectural choices. Our empirical study employs both online and offline evaluations to assess performance regarding accuracy, computational efficiency, and fidelity to the original MPC policy. Our results demonstrate that Behavior Cloning can effectively reduce the computational burden of MPC policies for 3-DOF robotic manipulators, achieving a 3x reduction in inference latency with a 84.98% success rate under relaxed tolerances. Notably, we find that static architectures outperform temporal variants, confirming the sufficiency of instantaneous state observations for this task. However, we observe a precision gap under strict tolerances, which suggest that while Behavior Cloning captures the global optimal trajectory, further research is needed to minimize terminal steady-state error.

</details>


### [65] [SoFiE: Soft Finger Exoskeleton for Intelligent Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00397)
*Magnus Malthe Sigsgaard Nielsen,Nicklas Nikolaj Grønvall,Xiaofeng Xiong,Saravana Prashanth Murali Babu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SoFiE模块化软手指外骨骼，采用3D打印柔性材料、腱驱动机构、StretchSense自感知弹簧和MagSense磁触觉传感技术，实现了轻量化、无束缚的食指屈曲辅助系统，能够进行姿态估计、材料刚度区分和抓取任务识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决手部功能减退人群的辅助需求，现有软体可穿戴机器人系统在模块化、轻量化和感知能力方面仍存在不足，需要一种集驱动、传感与控制于一体的紧凑型解决方案。

Method: 采用3D打印柔性材料制造模块化外骨骼；腱驱动机构配合直流电机实现主动屈曲；导电弹簧（StretchSense）兼具被动伸展和本体感知功能；软指尖内嵌磁铁与磁力计对（MagSense）实现接触力与物体柔顺度估计；电机编码器反馈提供执行器级状态感知。

Result: 实验验证系统能够提供可靠的姿态估计，区分不同刚度的材料，并在不同抓取任务中产生独特的传感器信号特征。

Conclusion: SoFiE作为模块化、软体辅助可穿戴机器人的概念验证，展示了在轻量化、无束缚设计下集成驱动与多模态传感的可行性，为未来安全自适应控制策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Soft wearable robotic systems have emerged as a promising solution for assisting individuals with reduced hand function. This paper presents SoFiE, a modular soft finger exoskeleton designed to assist index-finger flexion during grasping tasks. The proposed system is primarily fabricated using 3D-printed flexible materials, enabling a lightweight, low-profile, and modular design. Actuation is achieved through a tendon-driven mechanism powered by a compact DC motor, while passive extension is provided by a compliant conductive spring. This element, termed StretchSense, also functions as a proprioceptive sensor by exhibiting resistance changes under deformation. Furthermore, a novel tactile sensing approach, MagSense, is introduced, using a magnet and magnetometer pair embedded in a soft fingertip structure to estimate contact force and object compliance. The system is fully untethered and controlled by an embedded microcontroller. In addition, actuator-level sensing through motor encoder feedback enables estimation of the system state, providing a foundation for safe and adaptive control strategies. Experimental validation demonstrates the capability of the system to provide reliable pose estimation, distinguish between materials with different stiffness, and generate distinct sensor signatures across different grasping tasks. This paper details the design, fabrication, and sensing concepts of the proposed exoskeleton as a proof of concept toward modular, soft, and assistive wearable robotics.

</details>


### [66] [Literary Emotions in Motion: A Soft Robotics Installation for Tactile Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00418)
*Carolina Silva-Plata,Abraham Villavicencio-Carmona,Miguel Silva Plata,Stefan Escaida,Ruben Fernandez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个交互式装置，通过自然语言模型将叙事文本的情感语义分析映射到可变刚度的软气动模块中，利用七个六边形排列的软执行器表达两种主导情感，并通过用户研究验证了刚度与LED强度耦合对情感感知的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人在艺术语境中的应用日益增多， tactile交互能为观众提供超越视觉或听觉信号的身体参与体验，本文旨在探索如何通过可变刚度实现情感上有意义的触觉交互。

Method: 使用自然语言模型从预定义的六种情感中识别两种主导情感；设计并制作了具有薄膜层的硅胶软执行器（软模块），通过形态控制扩大刚度范围；采用七单元六边形布局，中心执行器表示主要情感，周围六个表示次要情感；通过用户研究评估刚度与LED强度多感官耦合对情感感知的影响。

Result: 刚度调制伴随颜色变化能够支持情感上有意义且吸引人的触觉交互体验。

Conclusion: 软体机器人结合自然语言处理的情感分析，通过可变刚度和多感官耦合，能有效增强交互式艺术装置中的情感表达与触觉体验。

Abstract: Soft robotics is increasingly explored in artistic contexts, where tactile interaction provides audiences with embodied engagement beyond visual or auditory signals. This work presents an interactive installation that maps semantic emotion analysis of narrative text into variable stiffness of soft pneumatic modules. A natural language model identifies two dominant emotions from a predefined set of six, driving the inflation of seven hexagonally arranged soft actuators. The central actuator represents the primary emotion, while the surrounding ones express the secondary. We develop and mechanically characterize silicone actuators, called soft modules, featuring a thin membrane layer, demonstrating how this morphological control expands the achievable stiffness range while preserving simplicity and low-cost fabrication. A user study with ten participants further evaluates how multisensory coupling of stiffness and LEDs intensity influences emotional perception. The results suggest that stiffness modulation accompanied by color change can support emotionally meaningful and engaging tactile interaction in soft robotic installations.

</details>


### [67] [Adaptive PD Gains for Energy-Conscious Control in Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00459)
*Danyal Saqib,Francisco Andrade Chavez,Marie Charbonneau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应PD控制器，能够限制机器人的动能和势能以实现安全的人机交互，无需外部力/力矩传感器，并在TALOS机器人上通过仿真和硬件验证了其柔顺性和能量限制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有柔顺力/力矩控制方法需要外部力传感器或关节力矩传感/估计，并非所有机器人均具备；能量基方法实现复杂且部分缺乏稳定性验证。

Method: 提出一种自适应PD控制器，通过可调参数精确设定能量截止限和锐度，同时限制机器人的动能和势能，并给出稳定性条件证明。

Result: 在TALOS机器人上进行的仿真和硬件实验验证了控制器的柔顺行为和能量限制效果。

Conclusion: 该自适应PD控制器能有效实现安全人机交互，且无需额外传感器，具有明确的稳定性保障。

Abstract: Compliant force or torque control are approaches often investigated to achieve safe physical human-robot interaction (pHRI). However, these approaches have limitations. Force control requires a robot to be equipped with external force sensors to track the amplitude and direction of applied forces. Torque control requires torque sensing or estimation in each joint. As this is not available on every robot, energy-based approaches offer a promising alternative. Such approaches aim to achieve safe pHRI by limiting the mechanical energy of the robot. Current schemes leveraging an energy-based approach tend to have a complex implementation, and some may require further stability verification. We hence propose an adaptive proportional-derivative (PD) controller that can limit a robot's energy under any given limit to achieve safe pHRI. The proposed controller can limit both the kinetic and potential energy of a robot, and the behaviour of the controller gains can be shaped using various parameters, defining precisely the cutoff limit and sharpness. We construct a stability proof for the controller and define a condition to ensure the controller's stability. The proposed controller's behaviour and compliance are tested on the TALOS robot from PAL Robotics both in simulation and on hardware, verifying the expected compliant and energy-limiting behaviour of the controller.

</details>


### [68] [ROG-Grasp: Root-Oriented Geometry for Robotic Grasping and Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00449)
*Zijian An,Augustus Sroka,Ran Yang,Bill Cai,Satoru Eto,Brian Poon,Kelvin Cai,Shijie Geng,Feng Liu,Yiming Feng,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROG-Grasp是一个基于几何的机器人抓取与放置框架，通过RGB-D感知和根表面几何估计农产品朝向，在番茄和洋葱上实现了高成功率和稳定执行时间，优于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采后农业加工中，农产品需要以一致的朝向被抓取和放置，而朝向感知操作是实现这一目标的关键挑战。

Method: 采用YOLO根检测器检测根位置，结合点云平面拟合推断根法线，从而生成稳定抓取姿态并执行朝向约束的笛卡尔运动规划。

Result: 在番茄和洋葱上的实验表明，该方法在孤立和杂乱场景中均实现了高成功率和稳定的执行时间，且比VLA策略更可靠、准确、快速。

Conclusion: 几何驱动的感知方法在面向实际朝向控制的操作任务中具有有效性。

Abstract: Orientation-aware manipulation is essential in post-harvest agricultural processing, where produce must be grasped and placed in consistent configurations. This paper presents ROG-Grasp, a geometry-based robotic grasping and placement framework that estimates the produce orientation from root surface geometry using RGB-D perception. A YOLO-based root detector and point cloud plane fitting are used to infer the root normal, enabling stable grasp pose generation and orientation-constrained Cartesian motion planning. Experiments on tomatoes and onions demonstrate high success rates and stable execution time in both isolated and cluttered scenarios. Compared with vision-language-action (VLA) policies, the proposed method achieves more reliable and accurate grasp completion with faster execution. These results highlight the effectiveness of geometry-driven perception for practical orientation-controlled manipulation tasks. A video of our paper is available online https://youtu.be/Ir2UtGODdMo.

</details>


### [69] [PaCo-VLA: Passivity-Shielded Compliance Prior for Contact-Rich Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00515)
*Haofan Cao,Zhaoyang Li,Zhichao You,Liang Guo,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PaCo-VLA提出了一种被动屏蔽的顺应性先验框架，将VLA模型输出重新解释为任务级顺应性提议，通过高频无源屏蔽层（能量罐核算+边界检查）防止无效/过时预测绕过底层接触物理，在连接器插拔任务中实现了零被动性违规和更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在接触丰富的操作任务中提供语义泛化能力，但其低频率输出缺乏力敏感任务所需的直接控制可靠性，存在语义到控制的鸿沟。

Method: 提出PaCo-VLA框架，将VLA网络输出视为任务级顺应性提议（语义绑定、任务阶段、导纳调度），通过独立的高频无源屏蔽层（能量罐核算+边界检查）进行治理，阻止无效/过时/未经验证的模型预测绕过底层接触物理。

Result: 在模拟和真实的连接器插拔实验中，PaCo-VLA相比未屏蔽的VLA基线实现了更优的精度，即使面对对抗性顺应性偏移也能维持零被动性违规。

Conclusion: 该框架在导纳端口建立了可证明的采样-被动运行时契约，为在接触丰富领域部署基础模型提供了运行时接口。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation demands both high-level semantic reasoning and the safe regulation of high-frequency contact dynamics. While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide unprecedented semantic generalization, their low-rate outputs lack the reliability required for direct plant authority in force-sensitive tasks. To bridge this semantic-to-control gap, we introduce PaCo-VLA, a passivity-shielded compliance prior that recasts the VLA interface. Rather than trusting VLAs with direct motor commands, PaCo-VLA treats network outputs as task-level compliance proposals: semantic bindings, task stages, and admittance schedules. A high-frequency, proposal-independent passivity shield governs these proposals through energy-tank accounting and boundary checks, preventing invalid, stale, or unverified model predictions from bypassing low-level contact physics. This decoupled architecture also enables causal evaluation, isolating semantic contributions from geometric shortcuts. Extensive simulated and real-world connector-insertion experiments demonstrate that PaCo-VLA achieves superior precision over unshielded VLA baselines, sustaining zero passivity violations even under adversarial compliance shifts. This framework establishes a provably sampled-passive runtime contract at the admittance port and provides a runtime interface for deploying foundation models in contact-rich domains.

</details>


### [70] [AI-IoT-Robotics Integration: Survey of Frameworks, Emerging Trends, and the Path Toward Connected Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01015)
*Ranulfo Bezerra,Satoshi Tadokoro,Kazunori Ohno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI、IoT与机器人三大领域的融合现状，提出了模块化系统架构，重点讨论了边缘端小语言模型(SLM)与云端大语言模型(LLM)的协同作用，并分析了实现互联机器人和物理AI范式所面临的关键挑战与解决路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AIoT和IoRT等两两融合已有显著进展，但缺乏将AI、IoT和机器人三者完整统一的设计框架，现有系统在互操作性和反馈控制方面仍存在持续空白。

Method: 通过综述现有研究，提出模块化系统架构，按集成深度对现有工作进行分类，并分析SLM-LLM混合系统与IoT基础设施及机器人代理的耦合方式。

Result: 总结了混合SLM-LLM系统在实时适应、可扩展性和可靠性方面解决挑战的能力，识别了互操作性和反馈控制方面的持续空白。

Conclusion: 为设计模块化、可解释且能在动态环境中学习的下一代AI-IoT-机器人生态系统提供了概念性和技术性路线图，推动了互联机器人和物理AI这一新兴范式的发展。

Abstract: The convergence of Artificial Intelligence, the Internet of Things, and Robotics is no longer a futuristic vision; it is rapidly becoming the foundation of real-time, intelligent, and context-aware systems. AI enables perception and reasoning, IoT provides scalable sensing and communication, and robotics delivers embodied actuation. Despite significant progress in pairwise combinations such as AIoT and the Internet of Robotic Things (IoRT), there remains a lack of unified design frameworks that fully integrate all three. This survey synthesizes the state-of-the-art across these domains, emphasizing the emerging role of Small Language Models (SLMs) at the edge and Large Language Models (LLMs) in the cloud for distributed cognition and autonomous decision-making. We propose a modular system architecture that aligns with these trends, analyze persistent gaps in interoperability and feedback control, and classify existing work by integration depth. Our review highlights how hybrid SLM-LLM systems, when coupled with IoT infrastructure and robotic agents, can address challenges in real-time adaptation, scalability, and reliability. This work offers a conceptual and technical roadmap for designing next-generation AI-IoT-Robotic ecosystems that are modular, interpretable, and capable of learning within dynamic environments, paving the way for the emerging paradigm of Connected Robotics and Physical AI.

</details>


### [71] [A passive universal grasping mechanism based on an everting shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00470)
*Mythra V. S. Balakuntala,Safvan Palathingal,G. K. Ananthasuresh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双稳态壳体外翻原理的被动式单片柔性抓取机构，通过弹性变形壳体与柔性臂协同工作，实现对任意形状刚性物体的包络抓取与释放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种结构简单、无需主动控制即可自适应抓取任意形状物体的被动式柔性抓取机构，克服传统抓取器对物体形状敏感、控制复杂的问题。

Method: 利用弹性可变形双稳态壳体的外翻行为，结合具有分布柔性的梁段式抓取臂；壳体接触物体时外翻驱动臂部包络物体形成封闭空间，反向驱动可使壳体回弹释放物体。

Result: 该机构能抓取任意形状的刚性物体，最大尺寸和重量由臂长和刚度决定；臂的分布柔性使其能贴合物体形状而不施加过大作用力。

Conclusion: 该被动式双稳态壳体外翻抓取机构实现了一种简洁、自适应的抓取方案，通过臂的刚度和尺寸设计可调节载荷与抓取范围，具有良好的形状自适应能力。

Abstract: A passive monolithic compliant grasping mechanism that works based on the eversion of an elastically deformable bistable shell is conceptualized. It comprises grasping arms made of beam segments that work in conjunction with the everting shell. The grasper is capable of picking up a stiff object of any shape up to a maximum size and weight. The bistable shell everts upon contact with the object to enable the grasping arms envelop the object forming an enclosure. The mechanism then stays in that configuration until it is actuated again to turn the shell back to its original configuration and thereby opening the enclosure to release the object. The stiffness of the arms decides the payload of the mechanism. The size of the arms decides the largest object that can be grasped and held. The arms have distributed compliance so that they can conform to the shape of the object without applying undue force on it.

</details>


### [72] [DeepIPCv3: Event-Aware Multi-Modal Sensor Fusion for Sudden Pedestrian Crossing Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01277)
*Oskar Natan,Andi Dharmawan,Aufaclav Zatu Kusuma Frisky,Jazi Eko Istiyanto,Jun Miura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeepIPCv3是一个融合LiDAR点云与动态视觉传感器(DVS)事件流的多模态自主导航框架，通过跨模态注意力机制和混合策略网络，在高速动态场景（如行人突然横穿）中实现了最先进的轨迹预测和控制精度，有效消除了曝光失败和运动模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于帧传感器的端到端自动驾驶系统在高度动态场景（如行人突然横穿）中存在固有的感知延迟和运动模糊问题，构成严重的安全隐患。为解决这一关键安全漏洞，作者提出融合LiDAR与DVS的解决方案。

Method: 提出DeepIPCv3框架：（1）融合LiDAR点云的密集3D空间几何信息与DVS的微秒级异步事件流；（2）引入Transformer启发的跨模态注意力机制动态关联两种模态，优先处理高速动态更新而不牺牲结构场景感知；（3）通过混合策略网络将融合的潜在表示映射为局部路径点和可执行控制命令，该网络融合了启发式轨迹跟踪与直接神经预测。

Result: 在自建多模态数据集（涵盖良好照明的正午和具有挑战性的傍晚条件）上的广泛对比和消融实验表明，DeepIPCv3达到了最先进的预测性能，有效消除了曝光失败和运动模糊，在轨迹和控制命令误差上均为最低，且在不同环境光照下均能实现高度响应、数学上有界的规避机动。

Conclusion: LiDAR与DVS的融合方案能够有效解决帧传感器在高速动态场景中的感知延迟和运动模糊问题，显著提升自动驾驶系统在突发危险场景中的安全性和响应能力。代码将开源在GitHub上供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Current end-to-end autonomous driving systems predominantly rely on frame-based sensors, which suffer from inherent perception latency and motion blur during highly dynamic encounters, specifically sudden pedestrian crossings. To address this critical safety vulnerability, we propose DeepIPCv3, a novel multi-modal autonomous navigation framework that synergizes the dense 3D spatial geometry of LiDAR point clouds with the microsecond-level asynchronous event streams of a Dynamic Vision Sensor (DVS). We introduce a Transformer-inspired cross-modal attention mechanism to dynamically correlate these distinct modalities, allowing the network to instantaneously prioritize high-speed dynamic updates without sacrificing structural scene awareness. The fused latent representations are then mapped to safe local waypoints and executable control commands via a hybrid policy network that blends heuristic trajectory tracking with direct neural predictions. Due to the severe physical risks associated with live testing of these sudden crossing scenarios, the framework is rigorously evaluated offline using a custom multi-modal dataset collected across both well-illuminated noon and challenging evening conditions. Extensive comparative and ablation studies demonstrate that DeepIPCv3 achieves state-of-the-art predictive performance. By effectively eliminating exposure failures and motion blur, the proposed LiDAR and DVS fusion yields the lowest trajectory and control command errors, enabling highly reactive, mathematically bounded evasive maneuvers regardless of ambient illumination. To support future research, we will release the codes to our GitHub repo at https://github.com/oskarnatan/DeepIPCv3.

</details>


### [73] [Autopilot-Preserving Residual Q-Learning with HJB-Inspired Finite-Action Risk Filtering for Fixed-Wing UAV Command Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01397)
*Mehmet Iscan,Batuhan Temiz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于HJB残差的监督学习框架，在保留经典自动驾驶仪不变的前提下，通过有限动作集上的半离散值迭代来修正航速、高度和航向指令，结合控制李雅普诺夫/控制屏障函数启发的安全屏蔽，使路径跟踪误差降低86.77%（相比基线）和49.54%（相比Q学习），同时诚实报告了空速误差上升等权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自动驾驶仪在强侧风与激进转弯等耦合场景下适应能力差，而直接作用于舵面的强化学习策略将探索风险集中在执行器接口，存在安全隐患。因此需要一种既能提升自适应能力又不牺牲飞行安全性的中间方案。

Method: 在经典自动驾驶仪外部放置一个可学习的主管，该主管从有限有界动作集中选择残差修正航速、高度、航向的指令参考值，修正后的参考通过可允许指令包络映射后送入自动驾驶仪。残差选择采用HJB半离散值迭代评论家对候选动作进行评分，通过无操作相对哈密顿优势排序，并经控制李雅普诺夫/控制屏障函数启发的有限动作屏蔽确保无操作回退始终可用。

Result: 在共享12状态运行时（固定飞行器、自动驾驶仪和执行器模型）的测试中，HJB残差方法的均方根路径跟踪误差为44.809米，而基线自动驾驶仪为338.617米，表格Q残差方法为88.809米，分别降低86.77%和49.54%。改进主要集中在基线表现最差的场景，但空速误差有所上升。

Conclusion: 本文提出的保留自动驾驶仪的残差指令监督设计能显著提升路径跟踪性能，尤其弥补了基线在最坏情况下的不足，但存在空速误差上升的权衡，无单一指标全面占优的方法。

Abstract: A fixed-wing UAV must hold airspeed, altitude, and heading references under wind, gusts, and turbulence, channels coupled so that correcting one can degrade another. Classical autopilots stabilize the airframe well but adapt poorly when a hard crosswind meets an aggressive turn, while reinforcement-learning (RL) policies acting directly on the surfaces concentrate exploration risk at the actuator interface. We place a learned supervisor above an unchanged autopilot rather than inside it: it selects a residual from a finite, bounded action set on the commanded airspeed, altitude, and heading; the modified reference is projected into an admissible command envelope before reaching the autopilot, which stays the only actuator-facing controller. What is new is how the residual is chosen. HJB residual scores candidates with a semi-discrete value-iteration critic in the spirit of the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation, ranks them by a no-op-relative Hamiltonian advantage, and filters them through a control-Lyapunov- and control-barrier-inspired finite-action shield that always keeps a no-op fallback. On a shared 12-state runtime holding the plant, autopilot, and actuator model fixed, so the comparison is at the package level, HJB residual lowers mean RMS path-tracking error to 44.809 m, against 338.617 m for the baseline autopilot and 88.809 m for a tabular-Q residual, an 86.77% reduction over the baseline and 49.54% over Q-learning. The gain concentrates where the baseline fails worst and comes with a measured rise in airspeed error, so no method dominates every metric. We present this autopilot-preserving residual command-supervision design and benchmark with its trade-offs reported intact.

</details>


### [74] [DriveAnchor: Progressive Anchor-based Flow Learning for Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00519)
*Limin Yan,Haoyun Tang,Yutao Qiu,Hongqing Liu,Haoyu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveAnchor是一个三阶段自动驾驶规划框架，通过示范流预训练构建2,398种轨迹词汇表、引导流后训练结合能量场实现可控性、以及奖励精炼流微调使用零阶强化学习优化避撞，在约200万驾驶场景中近距碰撞率降低89%，平均奖励提升32%，推理仅2.06毫秒，并通过实车验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划方法在行为多样性、可控性和安全性之间难以兼顾，缺乏一个可组合的流水线来同时满足这些需求。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 示范流预训练——用最远点采样构建2,398种轨迹形状词汇表替代无结构高斯先验；(2) 引导流后训练——联合训练能量场模块与流匹配，仅基于静态道路几何将锚点重定位到用户指定走廊多边形，无需可微引导；(3) 奖励精炼流微调——应用零阶强化学习对齐每个锚点输出与避撞目标，在锚点空间进行方向搜索。

Result: 在约200万驾驶场景中，近距碰撞率降低89%，平均奖励提升32%，模仿精度未下降，推理延迟2.06毫秒（NVIDIA Drive Orin），并通过实车测试验证。

Conclusion: DriveAnchor通过可组合的三阶段流水线有效实现了规划的行为多样性、可控性和安全性，具备生产部署的实用性。

Abstract: We present DriveAnchor, a three-stage framework for autonomous driving planning that achieves behavioral diversity, controllability, and safety in a composable pipeline. Demonstration Flow Pretraining replaces the unstructured Gaussian prior with a vocabulary of 2,398 trajectory shapes constructed by farthest-point sampling, structurally grounding behavioral diversity in vocabulary coverage. Guided Flow Post-training jointly post-trains an Energy Field module with flow matching (FM), conditioning the Energy Field on static road geometry alone, to relocate anchors toward user-specified corridor polygons before flow generation, adding controllability without differentiable guidance; after Stage 2, new corridor presets require only Energy Field updates, not FM retraining. Reward-Refined Flow Fine-tuning applies zeroth-order reinforcement learning to align each anchor's output with collision-avoidance objectives: because the flow-matching model is a deterministic feedforward network in single-step mode, each anchor uniquely determines the output trajectory, reducing reward optimization to a direction search in anchor space without log-likelihood computation or ODE-to-SDE conversion. Evaluated on approximately 2 million held-out driving scenarios, DriveAnchor reduces near-range collision rates by 89% and improves mean reward by 32% without degradation in imitation accuracy, with 2.06 ms inference on NVIDIA Drive Orin. DriveAnchor has been validated through real-world vehicle testing, confirming its practicality for production deployment.

</details>


### [75] [Crazyflow: An Accurate, GPU-Accelerated, Differentiable Drone Simulator in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01478)
*Martin Schuck,Marcel P. Rath,Yufei Hua,AbhisheK Goudar,SiQi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Crazyflow，一个面向空中机器人算法开发的高性能仿真平台，在速度上比现有顶尖无人机仿真器快一个数量级以上，支持单机到大规模集群（4000架无人机/集群），兼具高精度轨迹跟踪（亚厘米级误差）与超过5亿步/秒的避障采样速度。其独特之处在于支持飞行中在线强化学习——在0.38秒内从零训练一个恢复策略并成功稳定无人机，打破了传统"先训练后部署"的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中机器人仿真器虽然在保真度、可微分性和集群仿真等维度各有专长，但缺少一个统一平台能够同时涵盖所有这些领域的数据合成需求，限制了算法从基于模型到数据驱动、从梯度方法到采样方法、从单智能体到多智能体系统的全面发展。

Method: Crazyflow通过多级仿真抽象层次、轻量级系统识别管道、以及与所有开源Crazyflie模型直接兼容的设计，实现了极高的仿真速度和大规模并行化。它同时支持分析梯度策略学习（无需域随机化即可达到亚厘米级轨迹跟踪精度）和基于采样的避障（超过5亿步/秒）。

Result: 单无人机速度比现有最先进仿真器快一个数量级以上；可仿真数千个包含4000架无人机的集群；实现亚厘米级轨迹跟踪精度；避障采样速度超过5亿步/秒；在0.38秒内完成飞行中强化学习训练并成功稳定无人机。

Conclusion: Crazyflow通过同时推进精度、速度和可微分性，为合成数据生成提供开源资源，其大规模并行化能力支持在线、执行中的学习和优化，为新型算法开发打开大门。

Abstract: High-quality, large-scale synthetic data from simulations is becoming a cornerstone for pushing the capabilities of robot algorithms. While aerial robotics simulators have evolved to support specialized needs such as fidelity, differentiability, and swarms independently, a unified platform that can synthesize data across all these domains is missing. In this work, we propose Crazyflow, a simulator designed to push the limits of aerial-robotics algorithm development, from model-based to data-driven methods, gradient-based to sampling-based approaches, and single-agent to multi-agent systems. Compared to existing state-of-the-art drone simulators, it achieves speeds more than an order of magnitude faster for a single drone and can simulate thousands of swarms of 4000 drones each. Real-world experiments show Crazyflow supports both analytical-gradient-based policy learning, achieving sub-centimeter trajectory tracking accuracy without domain randomization, and sampling-based obstacle avoidance at speeds exceeding half a billion steps per second. Breaking the traditional train-then-deploy paradigm, we show that its unprecedented speed even enables in-flight reinforcement learning; we demonstrate this by throwing a physical drone into the air and training a recovery policy from scratch in 0.38 seconds, successfully stabilizing the drone. Crazyflow supports multiple levels of simulation abstraction, is directly compatible with all open-source Crazyflie models, and enables rapid reconfiguration across custom drone platforms and applications by providing a light-weight system identification pipeline. By pushing accuracy, speed, and differentiability simultaneously, Crazyflow serves as an open-source resource for synthetic data generation, with emerging capabilities for large-scale parallelization for online, in-execution learning and optimization, opening the door to novel algorithm development.

</details>


### [76] [PACE: Phase-Aware Chunk Execution for Robot Policies with Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00537)
*Junnan Nie,Jiayi Li,Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Tianle Zhang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE是一种无需训练、即插即用的测试时执行方法，通过从预测的动作块中在线选择执行时长，利用速度剖面中的低速过渡点作为重规划边界，显著提升了机器人操作任务的成功率（仿真任务57.8%→64.2%，真实任务50.7%→70.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作分块策略虽然通过预测连续动作序列改善了局部运动连续性，但执行时仍需人为设定固定的执行时长，而实验表明成功率与执行时长呈非单调的任务依赖性关系，固定时长无法作为可靠的部署规则。

Method: PACE在测试阶段无需重新训练，仅利用预测的动作块本身，通过识别速度剖面中的低速过渡点作为候选重规划边界，自适应地选择在线执行时长，从而在不同操作阶段动态调整执行长度。

Result: 在50个RoboTwin2.0仿真任务上，平均成功率从57.8%提升至64.2%；在双机械臂ALOHA和单臂Franka真实机器人实验中，平均任务得分从60.7提升至77.7，平均成功率从50.7%提升至70.4%。

Conclusion: PACE能够根据操作阶段动态调整执行时长，在过渡阶段缩短执行长度、在连贯运动中保持较长执行，从而有效提升机器人操作策略的鲁棒性和部署可靠性。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action and diffusion-based robot policies often use action chunking, where each policy query predicts a sequence of future actions and the robot executes an open-loop prefix before re-querying. While this interface improves local motion continuity, deployment still requires choosing the execution horizon: how much of each predicted chunk should be executed before acquiring a new observation. However, our experiments show that success is strongly task-dependent and non-monotonic with respect to the execution horizon, making a single constant horizon an unreliable deployment rule. We propose PACE (Phase-Aware Chunk Execution), a training-free test-time execution method that selects the execution horizon online from the predicted chunk itself. PACE exploits the phase-dependent kinematic structure of manipulation trajectories by identifying low-speed transition points in the predicted speed profile and using them as candidate replanning boundaries. Because PACE uses only the predicted action chunk, it is plug-and-play and requires no retraining or access to policy internals. We validate PACE through large-scale evaluations in both simulation and real-robot settings. On 50 RoboTwin2.0 tasks, PACE raises the average success rate from 57.8% to 64.2%. In real-robot experiments on bimanual ALOHA and single-arm Franka platforms, PACE improves the average task score from 60.7 to 77.7 and the average success rate from 50.7% to 70.4%. Ablations and rollout-level analyses show that PACE adapts execution horizons across manipulation phases, shortening near transitions while preserving longer execution during coherent motion.

</details>


### [77] [Dynamic Resilient Spatio-Semantic Memory with Hybrid Localization for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00576)
*Zhijie Yan,Shufei Li,Ze Zhang,Xin Liu,Yuhang Zheng,Zuoxu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DREAM是一个无需预建地图的实时机器人移动操作框架，通过在线构建时空语义体素记忆、位姿图感知的冗余感知记忆剪枝（RMP）以及语言条件3D检索+多模态大语言模型验证，在动态室内环境中显著提升了长时任务成功率（从40-60%提升至55-70%），同时保持内存占用0.37-0.63 GB和在线更新时间0.43-0.53秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动操作框架依赖预建地图、静态场景假设或高精度相机位姿，当目标物体被移动或位姿估计被校正时，场景信息会过时或错位，无法在动态变化的室内环境中保持几何一致、语义可查询且计算受限的场景表示。

Method: 1）在线构建时空语义体素记忆：从RGB-D观测中构建，由LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM后端注册；2）位姿图感知的冗余感知记忆剪枝（RMP）：在位姿校正后更新历史观测，同时控制长期观测历史的内存上限；3）目标定位与重新获取：结合语言条件3D检索、开放词汇图像检测和多模态大语言模型语义验证。

Result: 在四个动态室内实验室场景的真实机器人实验中，长时任务成功率从DynaMem的40%-60%提升至55%-70%，内存占用0.37-0.63 GB，在线记忆更新时间0.43-0.53秒。

Conclusion: DREAM通过在线构建可更新的时空语义记忆并结合多层次语义验证，有效解决了动态环境中移动操作场景表示过时与错位问题，在成功率、内存效率和实时性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Reliable mobile manipulation in dynamic indoor environments requires a scene representation that remains geometrically consistent, semantically queryable, and computationally bounded as the environment changes. Existing systems often rely on pre-built maps, static-scene assumptions, or highly accurate camera poses, which can lead to stale or misaligned scene information when target objects are relocated or pose estimates are corrected. This paper presents DREAM, a real-robot mobile manipulation framework that integrates perception, memory, localization, navigation, and manipulation in previously unseen indoor environments without a pre-built map. DREAM constructs an online spatio-semantic voxel memory from RGB-D observations registered by a LiDAR-inertial-visual SLAM backend. It further introduces pose-graph-aware Redundancy-Aware Memory Pruning (RMP) to update historical observations after pose corrections while keeping long-horizon observation history bounded. For target localization and reacquisition, DREAM combines language-conditioned 3D retrieval, open-vocabulary image detection, and multimodal large language model based semantic verification. Real-robot experiments in four dynamic indoor laboratory scenes show that DREAM improves long-horizon task success rates from 40%-60% with DynaMem to 55%-70%, while maintaining a memory footprint of 0.37-0.63 GB and an online memory-update time of 0.43-0.53 s across scenes.

</details>


### [78] [FlipItRight: Stable Pose-Targeted Throw-Flip Across Diverse Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01713)
*Axel Dawne,Shinkyu Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlipItRight是一个用于高自由度机械臂的稳定平面目标投掷翻转框架，通过将任务分解为对象级规划和机器人级规划，以释放状态作为显式中间表示，实现了无需先验数据或学习模型的鲁棒投掷，在120次实验中达到90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高自由度机械臂在平面目标投掷翻转任务中，应对释放时机不确定性、物体多样性及无需环境特定校准的鲁棒控制问题。

Method: 将任务分解为对象级规划器（生成满足期望着陆姿态的候选释放状态）和机器人级规划器（评估可执行性并构建可行的摆动运动）。以释放状态作为显式中间表示，实现原则性候选过滤、自适应选择释放和预摆动配置、结构化近释放运动设计（特别是最终摆动阶段近似恒定的末端执行器速度）。

Result: 在真实平台上对不同形状、大小和质量的物体进行120次实验，成功率达90%。消融实验证实每个设计选择都有助于投掷性能。

Conclusion: 该框架无需先验数据或学习模型，可直接部署于新物体和新目标，无需环境特定校准或数据收集。

Abstract: We propose FlipItRight, a framework for stable planar pose-targeted throw-flip with a high-DoF manipulator. The task is decomposed into an object-level planner, which generates candidate release states satisfying the desired landing pose, and a robot-level planner, which evaluates executability and constructs a feasible swing motion. Treating the release state as an explicit intermediate representation enables principled candidate filtering, adaptive selection of release and pre-swing configurations, and structured near-release motion design -- in particular, approximately constant end-effector velocities during the final swing phase to improve robustness to release-timing uncertainty. We validate on a real platform across objects of varying shape, size, and mass, achieving a 90% success rate across 120 trials. Ablation studies confirm that each design choice contributes to throwing performance, and the framework requires no prior data or learned model, enabling direct deployment on new objects and targets without environment-specific calibration or data collection.

</details>


### [79] [Global-Local Attention Decomposition for Terrain Encoding in Humanoid Perceptive Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00637)
*Shengcheng Fu,Yang Zhang,Zhanxiang Cao,Liyun Yan,Yizhi Chen,Yunpeng Yin,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GLAD（全局-局部注意力分解）方法，通过粗到细的地形编码器将全局地形感知与局部落脚点选择解耦，使双足机器人在稀疏立足点地形和受限环境中实现鲁棒 locomotion，并成功零样本迁移到真实 Unitree G1 机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知策略在稀疏立足点地形和受限环境中表现不佳，因为传统编码器将全局地形感知和局部落脚点选择这两种感知角色纠缠在一起，导致精细空间线索被稀释。

Method: 提出GLAD框架：一个基于机器人中心高程图的粗到细编码器，包含全局注意力分支（利用注意力池化总结周围地形上下文）和状态条件局部注意力分支（稀疏化并编码精确的落脚点相关几何信息），显式分离两种感知目标。

Result: 在 challenging 间隙、踏脚石和楼梯地形上实现可靠 locomotion；学习到的策略涌现出自主跟随窄路和避障行为；在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上使用机载 LiDAR 实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，在多种稀疏立足点和障碍物丰富场景中表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: GLAD通过显式注意力分解防止了细粒度空间线索的稀释，同时降低了训练开销，使得人形机器人在复杂地形上的感知运动策略具备更强的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Although reinforcement learning has significantly advanced humanoid locomotion, perceptive policies still struggle on sparse-foothold terrain and constrained environments. Success in these scenarios requires both broad terrain awareness and precise foothold selection, two perceptual roles that conventional encoders often entangle. To address this challenge, we propose Global-Local Attention Decomposition (GLAD) for terrain encoding in humanoid locomotion. Realized by a coarse-to-fine encoder over a robot-centric elevation map, GLAD explicitly separates these objectives: a global attention branch utilizes attention pooling to summarize the surrounding terrain context, while a state-conditioned local attention branch sparsifies and encodes precise foothold-relevant geometry. This explicit attention decomposition prevents the dilution of fine-grained spatial cues while reducing training overhead. Experiments demonstrate that GLAD enables reliable locomotion over challenging gaps, stepping stones, and stairs. Furthermore, the learned policy exhibits emergent terrain-responsive behaviors, autonomously following narrow paths and avoiding obstacles under simple velocity commands without explicit navigation planners. In real-world deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot using onboard LiDAR, the proposed method achieves robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across diverse sparse-foothold and obstacle-rich domains.

</details>


### [80] [Market-Based Replanning for Safety-Critical UAV Swarms in Search and Rescue Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01970)
*Luiz Giacomossi,Andrea Haglund,Claire Namatovu,Emily Zainali,Esaias Målqvist,Yonatan M. Beyene,Ivan Tomasic,Baran Çürüklü,Håkan Forsberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IRDS分布式协调架构，通过反向拍卖机制和几何共识协议实现无人机群在搜索救援任务中的容错协调，仿真实验表明在25%代理失效下仍能保持93%任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索救援任务中的自主无人机群需要容错协调机制，以应对代理退化（如故障或损毁）情况下仍能持续执行任务，保障系统鲁棒性。

Method: 提出IRDS分布式协调架构，采用反向拍卖市场机制（基于距离加权成本函数竞标服务搜索扇区）与几何共识协议（用于目标验证），在资源受限环境中实现自主重规划。

Result: 通过基于物理的仿真（8个智能体，8×8网格）并注入随机故障，实验表明集群能以低延迟自主重新分配故障代理的任务，在25%劳动力退化下维持93%任务成功率。

Conclusion: IRDS框架提供了一种鲁棒的、经实证检验的自愈式空中机器人协调方法，适用于资源受限的搜索救援任务场景。

Abstract: Reliable autonomous UAV swarms in Search and Rescue (SAR) missions require fault-tolerant coordination capable of sustaining operations despite agent degradation. This paper introduces the Intelligent Replanning Drone Swarm (IRDS), a distributed coordination architecture designed for resource-constrained environments. The proposed framework employs a Reverse-Auction market mechanism where agents bid to service search sectors based on a distance-weighted cost function, coupled with a geometric consensus protocol for target verification. We evaluate the approach through physics-based simulations (N=8 agents, 8x8 grid) subjected to stochastic fault injection. Results indicate that the swarm autonomously reallocates tasks from failed agents with low latency relative to the total mission duration, maintaining a mission success rate of 93% under 25% workforce degradation. The proposed framework demonstrates a robust, empirically tested method for self-healing aerial robotic coordination.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Pure Sampling: Hybrid Optimization Mechanisms for Non-Convex Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00737)
*Yuichiro Aoyama,Minchan Jung,Akash Ratheesh,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于最大熵差分动态规划（ME-DDP）的非凸模型预测控制（MPC）优化机制，提出了一个两阶段优化策略：先利用DDP进行梯度下降，再通过基于动作值函数逆Hessian采样的策略跳出局部最优。在四个机器人系统的导航任务中，与确定性DDP和MPPI控制进行了对比，结果显示低维系统中ME-DDP性能更优，高维系统中稳定性更高，并通过四旋翼飞行器硬件实验验证了实际部署的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸代价景观（由非线性动力学、多障碍物等引起）导致梯度方法频繁收敛到次优局部极小值，这是机器人领域的一个基本挑战。

Method: 提出双步优化机制：(1) 使用DDP利用代价景观梯度进行初始优化；(2) 通过从动作值函数逆Hessian表征的策略中采样来打破优化陷阱。分析了三种ME-DDP变体：单峰高斯ME-DDP、多峰高斯ME-DDP和Stein变分DDP。

Result: 在低维系统中，ME-DDP框架持续优于MPPI；在高维系统中，MPPI偶尔能发现更激进的机动策略，但ME-DDP方法保持更高且更稳定的成功率。四旋翼硬件实验验证了框架的实用性。

Conclusion: ME-DDP框架能有效解决非凸MPC中的局部最优问题，在复杂环境中具有鲁棒性和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the optimization mechanisms of non-convex Model Predictive Control (MPC) using the Maximum Entropy Differential Dynamic Programming (ME-DDP) framework. Navigating non-convex cost landscapes induced by nonlinear dynamics, multiple obstacles, etc. remains a fundamental challenge in robotics, where gradient-based methods frequently converge to suboptimal local minima. We demonstrate a dual-step optimization mechanism designed to overcome these traps. (1) an initial phase of using DDP to exploit the gradient of the cost landscape, followed by (2) disruption of the optimization via sampling from policies characterized by the inverse Hessian of the action-value function. We provide a rigorous analysis of this sampling mechanism of three ME-DDP variants: Unimodal Gaussian ME-DDP, Multimodal Gaussian ME-DDP, and Stein Variational DDP. Furthermore, with navigation tasks of four robotic systems under cluttered environments, we conduct extensive benchmarking of three variants of the ME-DDP, against deterministic DDP, and one of the most successful sampling-based schemes, Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control with three policy parameterizations and update laws that correspond to those of ME-DDPs.
  The results show that in low-dimensional systems where the cost landscapes are relatively simple and local information is sufficiently representative, our framework consistently outperforms MPPIs. In high-dimensional systems, MPPI can occasionally discover aggressive maneuvers that enable it to steer the systems faster than DDP-based methods, whereas our method maintains a higher, more stable success rate.
  Finally, we validate the practical efficacy of the framework through hardware experiments with a quadrotor navigating a dense, non-convex obstacle field, confirming the robustness of the proposed framework for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [82] [SKIP: Sparse Keyframe Interpolation Paradigm for Efficient Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00664)
*Ziheng He,Yixiang Chen,Ning Yang,Zhanqian Wu,Qisen Ma,Yuan Xu,Jiabing Yang,Peiyan Li,Xiangnan Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SKIP是一种事件保持的稀疏到密集视频生成框架，通过仅合成任务相关的关键帧并插值缺失帧，在机器人操纵视频生成中实现4.16倍加速，同时将FVD降低89%，生成视频作为策略训练数据效果接近真实演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身世界模型在像素空间中生成长序列操纵视频的计算成本极高，而简单丢弃帧会丢失稀疏但关键的任务事件（如接近、接触、抓取、释放），导致下游策略失效。

Method: SKIP首先利用机器人感知的多模态特征识别任务相关关键帧，然后用稀疏视频扩散模型仅合成这些关键帧，最后通过学习的间隔预测器和动作条件插值器根据机器人动作重建缺失帧。

Result: 在LIBERO上，SKIP比密集基线快4.16倍，FVD降低89%；完全替代真实演示时，LIBERO仿真成功率仅下降1.3个百分点，真实机器人下降6.7个百分点，而密集帧生成下降48-58个百分点。

Conclusion: SKIP证明了稀疏到密集的生成范式在保持任务关键事件的同时大幅提升效率，生成视频可作为有效的策略训练数据，显著优于密集帧生成。

Abstract: Embodied world models have emerged as a promising paradigm in robotics by predicting how robot actions affect the surrounding scene. However, the rollout inference remains computationally expensive in pixel space, as long-horizon manipulation videos typically have to be generated frame by frame. This cost cannot be easily reduced by indiscriminately dropping frames, since downstream policies rely on complete preservation of sparse task-relevant events such as approach, contact, grasp, and release. To address this challenge, we propose Sparse Keyframe Interpolation Paradigm (SKIP), an event-preserving sparse-to-dense framework that avoids dense frame-by-frame generation. SKIP first identifies task-relevant keyframes by leveraging robot-aware multimodal features. It then synthesizes only these keyframes with a sparse video diffusion model. A learned gap predictor and an action-conditioned interpolator subsequently reconstruct the missing intervals according to the robot actions. On LIBERO, SKIP generates dense rollouts $4.16\times$ faster than a dense baseline while improving visual fidelity and reducing aggregate FVD by $89.0\%$. Importantly, SKIP-generated videos are effective policy-training data. Even when they fully replace real demonstrations, $π_{0.5}$ success drops only $1.3$ pp in LIBERO simulation and $6.7$ pp on the real robot, whereas fully dense frame-by-frame generation collapses by $48$ to $58$ pp.

</details>


### [83] [Shape Your Body: Value Gradients for Multi-Embodiment Robot Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00702)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将通用多形态价值函数转化为机器人设计的可重用模型，通过价值梯度优化机器人形态，无需为每个机器人重新运行强化学习协同设计循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人设计需要为每个新设计重新运行强化学习协同设计循环，效率低下。本文旨在利用预训练的形态感知价值函数作为可微分代理，实现高效的机器人形态优化。

Method: 首先在多种机器人设计上训练形态感知策略和价值函数，然后冻结价值函数作为可微分代理，通过价值梯度反向传播优化候选形态的连续参数。

Result: 在多种机器人设计场景中验证，包括单机器人扰动、跨形态类别的未见机器人，使用多达50种机器人和超过1100个连续形态参数训练单模型。价值梯度不仅能优化完整形态，还能识别限制性能的关键设计和控制参数。

Conclusion: 预训练的多形态价值函数可作为可重用的可微分代理，高效优化新机器人设计，并能分析设计参数对性能的影响，为机器人设计分析与优化提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We propose to turn generalist multi-embodiment value functions into reusable models for robot design. Instead of running a new reinforcement learning co-design loop for each robot, we first train an embodiment-aware policy and value function across many robot designs. After training, the frozen value function is used as a differentiable surrogate to optimize candidate embodiments through value gradients. We evaluate our approach across different robot design settings, from perturbed single robots to held-out robots across morphology classes, with single models trained on up to 50 robots and design spaces of over 1100 continuous embodiment parameters. Beyond optimizing complete embodiments, we show that value gradients can identify performance-limiting design and control parameters, enabling both the optimization and the analysis of new robot designs.

</details>


### [84] [BEVIO: Efficient Bird's-Eye-View based Sparse-Update Visual-Inertial Odometry for Lunar Day-Night Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00709)
*Mohit Singh,Shehryar Khattak,Ashish Goel,Michael Paton,Kostas Alexis,Issa A. Nesnas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BEVIO方法，通过鸟瞰图（BEV）图像匹配方案，使视觉惯性里程计（VIO）能够在极低视觉更新率（低至0.25 Hz）下实现可靠导航，适用于资源受限的月球车在昼夜及自照明条件下的运行。实验通过高保真月球仿真和半比例月球车实地昼夜测试验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VIO性能依赖视觉更新频率，而月球车等资源受限平台帧率极低，且昼夜自照明条件下特征关联困难，导致传统VIO在稀疏视觉更新下不可靠。

Method: 提出基于鸟瞰图（BEV）的图像匹配方案BEVIO，通过BEV视角增强对大帧间运动和外观变化的鲁棒性，实现更可靠的特征匹配。

Result: 在高保真月球仿真和Plaster City半比例月球车昼夜实验中，BEVIO在低至0.25 Hz的视觉更新率下仍能实现可靠的昼夜及自照明导航。

Conclusion: BEVIO方法适用于功率和计算资源受限的月球车导航，证明了在极稀疏视觉更新条件下VIO的可行性。

Abstract: Visual-Inertial Odometry (VIO) provides smooth, high-rate state estimates and has been widely used for robotic navigation in both terrestrial and planetary applications. However, its performance is typically dependent on the frequency of visual updates, which is a challenge for planetary rovers operating under extreme resource constraints and low frame rates. This work investigates enabling reliable VIO with very sparse visual updates for lunar rover applications, addressing both day and night-time operations where feature associations become especially difficult under self-illumination conditions. We propose a Bird's Eye View (BEV)-based image matching scheme that remains robust to larger inter-frame motions and more reliable feature matching despite significant visual appearance changes. We extensively evaluate our proposed approach, BEVIO, through high-fidelity photorealistic lunar and real-time robotic experiments conducted using a half-scale lunar rover, in a long-term day-night deployment at Plaster City, CA, USA. The results demonstrate that our method enables reliable day and nighttime self-illuminated traverses at visual update rates as low as 0.25 Hz, underscoring its suitability for navigation on power- and compute-limited lunar rovers.

</details>


### [85] [Permissive Safety Through Trusted Inference: Verifiable Belief-Space Neural Safety Filters for Assured Interactive Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02562)
*Haimin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共形预测的算法，用于认证信念空间安全过滤器（BeliefSF）在高概率下的安全性，通过聚焦于推理可靠的区域，在保持共形预测简单性的同时，验证了一个比标准基线更宽松的安全过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使用信念空间安全过滤器的交互机器人提供正式的安全保证，解决运行时推理误差和神经网络近似带来的挑战，同时减少安全过滤的保守性。

Method: 利用共形预测认证BeliefSF的高概率安全性，通过聚焦于推理可靠的区域来利用信念空间安全过滤的结构特性，明确考虑机器人运行时推理模块的可靠性。

Result: 在模拟人-车交互基准测试中，该方法验证了一个比标准共形预测基线明显更宽松的信念空间安全过滤器。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持共形预测简单性和样本复杂度的同时，能够显著减少安全过滤的保守性，为交互机器人在人类不确定性下的安全运行提供了有效认证手段。

Abstract: Autonomous robots that interact with people must make safe and efficient decisions under human-induced uncertainty, such as their preferences, goals, competency, and willingness to cooperate. Safety filters are a popular approach for ensuring safety in interactive robotics, since their modular design separates safety from performance, allowing robots to operate safely around people with minimal impact on task efficiency. While traditional safety filters typically operate only in the physical space, neglecting the robot's ability to learn and adapt online, the recently proposed belief-space safety filter (BeliefSF) reasons about robot safety in closed-loop with runtime inference that actively reduces the robot's uncertainty online, thereby reducing conservativeness in filtering. However, providing formal safety guarantees for robots deploying BeliefSF remains a significant challenge due to errors in runtime inference and neural approximation of safety filters required to handle the high dimensionality of belief spaces. In this paper, we propose an algorithmic approach to certify high-probability safety of BeliefSF using conformal prediction, while explicitly accounting for the reliability of the robot's runtime inference module. Our method leverages the structure of belief-space safety filtering by focusing verification on a region where inference is expected to be reliable. It preserves the simplicity and sample complexity of standard conformal prediction, yet can certify a substantially less conservative safety filter. Through a simulated human-vehicle interaction benchmark, we show that our approach verifies a significantly more permissive belief-space safety filter than a standard conformal prediction baseline.

</details>


### [86] [Infeasible optimization problems and the hierarchical augmented Lagrangian method in imitation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00730)
*Roland Andrews,Justin Carpentier,Ajay Sathya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对模仿学习中的硬约束不可行性问题，提出基于增广拉格朗日方法的简单解决方案，驱使策略向最近可行约束优化问题的解靠近，并在带加速度约束和行人安全约束的驾驶任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习中引入硬约束（如安全性、稳定性）有时会导致约束不可行，引发训练不稳定或难以收敛，因此需要一种鲁棒的处理不可行约束的方法。

Method: 基于增广拉格朗日方法在处理不可行场景下的最新理论成果，将原始的不可行约束优化问题转化为逼近最近可行约束问题的求解过程。

Result: 在玩具驾驶示例中，面对总加速度约束和行人安全约束这两个可能自然产生不可行性的约束条件，所提方法仍能学习到安全的策略。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效处理模仿学习中约束不可行的情况，使策略收敛到具有良好性质的最近可行约束IL问题的解。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) is an effective approach to train complex robotics policies. Recent works have introduced hard constraints into imitation-learning optimization problems to ensure safety, stability, and robustness of the learned policy. However, we argue that these constraints are sometimes infeasible, which can lead to unstable or difficult training dynamics. We study a simple remedy for such situations based on recent theoretical results on the augmented Lagrangian method in infeasible settings. We show that our approach drives the learned policy toward the solution of a closest-feasible constrained IL problem with desirable properties. The method is illustrated on a toy driving example with a total-acceleration constraint and pedestrian-safety constraints, a setting in which infeasibility can naturally arise while still allowing a safe learned policy.

</details>


### [87] [STEM: Semantic Target Search and Exploration using MAVs in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00762)
*Nikhil Sethi,Max Lodel,Laura Ferranti,Robert Babuška,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用语义引导的视点规划器，使微型无人机在非结构化3D环境中快速搜索目标，结合组合规划、主动感知和LLM语义优先级，在仿真和真实实验中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，2D语义导航在复杂3D环境中效果不佳，而机器人探索又缺乏语义推理能力，无法高效搜索目标。本文旨在克服这些局限，实现无人机在非结构化3D环境中的快速目标搜索。

Method: 1) 语义引导的组合视点规划器，优先选择可能指向目标的视点；2) 主动感知管线，将观察到的物体语义优先级传播到前沿体素以计算语义信息增益；3) 利用LLM相似度分数作为语义优先级输入。

Result: 在两种仿真环境中持续优于基线方法，能快速找到目标且保持合理的探索时间。真实实验中验证了方法在电池续航、传感器范围有限和语义不确定性等实际约束下的有效性。

Conclusion: 语义引导的视点规划结合LLM优先级可以有效提升MAV在非结构化3D环境中搜索目标的效率，且具备实用鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous target search is crucial for deploying Micro Aerial Vehicles (MAVs) in emergency response and rescue missions. Existing approaches either focus on 2D semantic navigation in structured environments -- which is less effective in complex 3D settings, or on robotic exploration in cluttered spaces -- which often lacks the semantic reasoning needed for efficient target search. This paper overcomes these limitations by proposing a novel framework that utilizes a semantically-guided viewpoint planner to minimize target search and exploration time in unstructured 3D environments using an MAV. Specifically, we develop a combinatorial planner that generates efficient semantic exploration plans by prioritizing viewpoints that likely lead to the target. To guide the planner towards the target, an active perception pipeline is developed that propagates semantic priorities of observed objects into neighboring frontier voxels for computing semantic information gains of frontier viewpoints. In addition, we demonstrate how LLM-based similarity scores can be leveraged as semantic priority input to our pipeline. Evaluations in two distinct simulation environments show that the proposed method consistently outperforms baselines by quickly finding the target while maintaining reasonable exploration times. Real-world experiments with an MAV further demonstrate the method's ability to handle practical constraints like limited battery life, small sensor range, and semantic uncertainty.

</details>


### [88] [SafeVLA-Bench: A Benchmark for the Success-Safety Gap in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00773)
*Jialiang Fan,Weizhe Xu,Oleg Sokolsky,Insup Lee,Fanxin Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeVLA-Bench是一个面向现有VLA仿真基准的后验安全评估框架，通过信号时序逻辑形式化任务相关的安全约束，并引入不安全成功指标（SBU和VSI）来量化成功轨迹中的安全隐患。在LIBERO和RoboCasa-365上的实验表明，高任务成功率并不意味着安全执行，存在大量成功但不安全的轨迹行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA基准仅以二元成功指标衡量任务完成情况，忽略了成功轨迹中存在的安全隐患，如过度接触力、扰动无关物体、物体失稳或机器人自接触等。缺乏对安全性的系统评估。

Method: 提出SafeVLA-Bench框架，使用信号时序逻辑（STL）形式化任务相关的安全需求规范，并定义两个不安全成功指标：Succ-But-Unsafe（SBU）——成功但违反安全规则的轨迹比例，以及Violation Severity Index（VSI）——有界的最坏违规深度分数。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboCasa-365上评估了9个策略-基准组合；高成功率的桌面任务基线仍有13%-15%的不安全回合率，RoboCasa-365中36%-56%的成功轨迹至少违反一条安全条款。

Conclusion: 高任务成功率不能保证安全执行，现有VLA策略在安全方面存在显著不足，SafeVLA-Bench为评估和改进策略安全性提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) benchmarks measure whether a policy completes a requested manipulation task, but binary success can hide safety-relevant trajectory behavior: reaching the goal while applying excessive contact, disturbing bystander objects, destabilizing the held object, or entering robot self-contact. We present SafeVLA-Bench, a post-hoc safety-evaluation framework for existing simulator-based VLA benchmarks. It formalizes task-aware safety requirements as Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications and reports native success with two unsafe-success metrics: Succ-But-Unsafe (SBU), the fraction of rollouts that both succeed and violate safety, and Violation Severity Index (VSI), a bounded worst-violation depth score. We instantiate SafeVLA-Bench on LIBERO and RoboCasa-365, evaluating nine policy-benchmark entries across tabletop and kitchen manipulation tasks. High task success does not imply safe execution: high-SR tabletop baselines still leave 13 to 15 percent unsafe-episode rates,and 36 to 56 percent of successful RoboCasa-365 rollouts violate at least one active safety clause. Project page: https://safevla.org.

</details>


### [89] [Coarse-to-Fine Compositional Diffusion for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00837)
*Byoungwoo Park,Utkarsh A. Mishra,Jaemoo Choi,Juho Lee,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoFi（Coarse-to-Fine Compositional Diffusion），一种推理时采样方法，通过先构建全局粗结构再细化局部细节的方式，在长程机器人规划、全景图像生成、长视频生成等任务上，比现有组合扩散方法在全局一致性和局部质量上更优，且计算成本降低2-8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合扩散方法通过重叠局部计划组合生成超出训练规模的长程输出，但标准组合仅强制相邻局部计划一致，导致局部兼容但全局不合理。现有改进方法（如反复传播局部一致信号或推理时优化）在局部计划数量或维度增加时计算成本高昂。

Method: CoFi包含两步推理过程：1) 先对齐局部去噪估计形成共享的粗结构，捕获长程任务级布局；2) 将该粗结构扩散到中间噪声水平，再用同一预训练局部先验去噪，恢复局部精细结构的同时保留粗结构带来的全局一致性。

Result: 在长程机器人规划、全景图像生成、长视频生成任务上，CoFi在全局一致性和局部样本质量上均优于先前的组合扩散基线，且所需去噪器评估次数减少2-8倍。

Conclusion: 分离全局结构形成与局部细节精化是一种有效的推理时组合策略，能够在保持计算高效的同时兼顾全局一致性与局部质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide strong priors for generating structured data, but many tasks require outputs beyond the scale on which these models are typically trained. Compositional generation addresses this by composing overlapping local plans from a pretrained short-horizon prior into a long-horizon output. However, standard composition primarily enforces agreement between neighboring local plans, yielding local consistency without directly specifying the global structure of the full composition. As a result, locally compatible plans may still form an implausible route, task sequence, or temporal evolution. Existing methods improve global coherence by repeatedly propagating local consistency signals or by adding inference-time optimization, but these procedures become expensive as the number or dimensionality of local plans increases. We propose Coarse-to-Fine Compositional Diffusion (CoFi), an inference-time sampler that separates global structure formation from local detail refinement. CoFi first aligns local denoised estimates around a shared coarse structure, producing a global scaffold that captures the long-range task-level arrangement. It then diffuses this scaffold to an intermediate noise level and denoises it with the same pretrained local prior, restoring local fine structure while preserving the scaffold-induced global coherence. Across long-horizon robotic planning, panoramic image generation, and long video generation, CoFi not only improves both global coherence and local sample quality over prior compositional baselines, but also requires 2-8x fewer denoiser evaluations.

</details>


### [90] [From Cues to Horizons: Dynamic Risk Horizon Profiling for Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00857)
*Xinyi Ning,Zilin Bian,Dachuan Zuo,Semiha Ergan,Kaan Ozbay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种风险视野剖面(RHP)模块，通过连续可学习的势场模型计算周围物体的时空邻近度来刻画未来风险分布，从而提升轨迹预测的准确性和安全性。在highD和SHRP2数据集上，该方法在5秒RMSE和minFDE指标上分别降低了25.0%和29.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险感知轨迹预测方法仅将历史风险信息作为辅助信号，忽略了风险的未来演化及其不确定性，无法准确捕捉人类驾驶员所感知的关键时刻。

Method: 提出RHP模块，包含连续可学习的势场模型，通过计算周围物体的时空邻近度来刻画未来视野内的风险分布，并自适应识别关键时刻以辅助轨迹预测。

Result: 在highD数据集上5秒RMSE降低25.0%，在SHRP2数据集上5秒minFDE降低29.1%，涵盖安全、近碰撞和碰撞等多种风险场景。

Conclusion: 该方法在短时和长时预测上均表现优异，具备跨高速公路和城市道路场景的鲁棒泛化能力，可支持更安全的自动驾驶路径规划和策略选择。

Abstract: Accurate and reliable vehicle trajectory prediction is essential for safe autonomous driving. Recent studies have incorporated safety risk into trajectory prediction to quantify dangers posed by surrounding agents. However, most risk-aware approaches use past risk information as a secondary signal to help guide decisions, overlooking its future evolution and uncertainty. In this paper, we propose a risk horizon profiling (RHP) module that incorporates a continuous, learnable potential field model for risk-aware trajectory prediction. The RHP module calculates the spatial-temporal proximity of surrounding objects to profile risk distributions across future horizons, which supports better trajectory prediction by adaptively identifying what human drivers perceive as critical moments. We evaluate our method on two datasets from different driving settings, highD for highway corridors and SHRP2 for urban streets, which cover diverse risk scenarios including safe, near-crash, and crash events. Compared to the baseline methods, our framework achieves a 25.0\% reduction in 5s RMSE on the highD dataset and a 29.1\% reduction in 5s minFDE on SHRP2. These results indicate strong performance for both short and long horizon prediction and robust generalization across highway and urban scenarios. The proposed method enables more realistic AV path planning and strategic selection, thereby supporting safer autonomous driving and more advanced driver-assistance systems. The source code for this work is available at: https://github.com/bilab-nyu/RHP

</details>


### [91] [Generative Multi-Robot Motion Planning via Diffusion Modeling with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00933)
*Suk Ki Lee,Venkata Sai Deepak Mutta,Hyunwoong Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合去中心化扩散模型轨迹生成与多智能体强化学习价值引导的协调框架，无需集中式联合规划即可实现多机器人协调运动规划，在仿真迷宫环境中将智能体间干扰率从55.4%降至41.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式多机器人规划可扩展性差，去中心化规划又无法天然考虑智能体间交互，现有方法难以兼顾可扩展性与协调性。
