Method: 每个机器人独立使用基于单智能体运动数据训练的扩散模型生成候选轨迹；通过集中式价值函数（经MARL训练）以梯度引导方式干预逆向扩散过程，基于指数倾斜公式偏置去噪分布朝向更高多智能体联合回报的轨迹。

Result: 在四移动机器人仿真迷宫环境中，所提方法将智能体间干扰率从55.4%降低至41.8%。

Conclusion: MARL价值引导能有效将协调性引入去中心化生成式规划器，无需构建完全联合的多机器人模型即可实现协调。

Abstract: Coordinating multiple robots in shared environments requires generating feasible trajectories for each agent while accounting for interactions among agents. Centralized planning approaches become difficult to scale as the number of robots increases, while decentralized approaches that allow each agent to plan independently do not inherently account for inter-agent interactions. This paper presents a framework for coordinated multi-robot motion planning that combines decentralized generative trajectory planning with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL)-based coordination. Each robot independently generates candidate trajectories using a diffusion model trained on single-agent motion data, leveraging the generative model's ability to produce feasible and diverse trajectories. To reduce conflicts between agents, a centralized value function trained via MARL guides the reverse diffusion process through gradient-based steering, enabling interaction-aware trajectory generation without centralized joint planning or retraining of the generative model. This guidance follows an exponential tilting formulation, in which the value function biases the denoising distribution toward trajectories with higher expected multi-agent return. The framework is evaluated in a simulated maze environment with four mobile robots. Experimental results show that the proposed value-guided diffusion planning reduces the inter-agent interference rate from 55.4% to 41.8%, demonstrating that coordination can be effectively achieved while preserving the scalability of decentralized trajectory generation. These results suggest that MARL-based value guidance can effectively introduce coordination into decentralized generative planners without requiring a fully joint multi-robot model.

</details>


### [92] [Threading Optimization for Vision-Language-Action Model Inference in Low-Cost Smart Agricultural Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00966)
*Keith Truongcao,Christopher Nhu,Zijian An,Phong Nguyen,Siwei Cai,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对VLA模型在低成本机械臂上推理速度慢和精细运动调整困难的问题，提出了RTAC算法的完整系统级实现，通过优化线程架构提升了控制稳定性和速度，并在农产品（大蒜和核桃）操作任务中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型存在推理速度慢和难以进行细粒度运动调整的问题，限制了其在工业中的广泛应用；RTAC算法虽被提出但仅停留在伪代码层面，缺乏稳定可靠的实系统实现。

Method: 提出RTAC的完整系统级实现，针对低成本机械臂操作系统进行优化；通过优化策略推理和控制管线的线程实现来减少端到端延迟并提高响应性，而不修改底层策略本身。

Result: 在农产品（大蒜球和核桃）操作任务上的实验表明，自定义线程实现相比RTAC基础实现显著提升了控制稳定性和速度。

Conclusion: 通过系统级线程优化可以在不修改底层策略的前提下有效提升VLA模型在低成本机械臂上的实时控制性能，弥合了算法伪代码与稳定部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Vision-Language Action (VLA) models continue to face challenges such as slow inference speed and difficulty performing fine-grained motion adjustments, limiting their widespread adoption in industry. While the Real-Time Action Chunking (RTAC) algorithm has been proposed to address these bottlenecks, bridging the gap between the algorithm provided in pseudocode to a stable, real-world deployment on a low-cost robotic arm remains a challenge. In this work, we present a complete system-level implementation of RTAC tailored for a low-cost robotic manipulation system. We advance beyond the original high-level pseudocode by optimizing the threading implementation for the policy inference and control pipeline, reducing end-to-end latency and improving responsiveness without modifying the underlying policy. We evaluate this system on tasks involving the manipulation of agricultural produce, specifically garlic bulbs and walnuts. Experimental results demonstrate that our custom threading implementation significantly improves control stability and speed compared to the base implementation of RTAC.

</details>


### [93] [Make Your VLA More Robust Without More Data By Interleaving Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00985)
*Dan BW Choe,Sundhar Vinodh Sangeetha,Samuel Coogan,Shreyas Kousik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MPVI框架，通过将基于模型的运动规划与VLA模型交错集成，无需额外训练即可显著提升移动操控在长时域任务中的鲁棒性，在BEHAVIOR-1K基准上任务进度提升113%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在长时域移动操控任务中表现不佳，主要面临两个挑战：(1) 需要在广泛分布的子任务序列中维持对高层目标的进度；(2) 早期执行错误会随任务时间快速累积。仅靠大规模人类遥操作数据的微调无法解决这些问题，表明需要超越数据扩展的新方法。

Method: 提出MPVI（Motion Planner / VLA Interleaving）框架，将基于模型的运动规划与VLA模型交错集成。具体包括：(1) 利用开放词汇目标检测、前沿探索和运动规划，实现对远处或遮挡目标的定位与导航；(2) 通过VLM（视觉语言模型）驱动的完成度检查与本体感觉触发器，实现模块间的可靠切换。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K基准上，MPVI相比顶级端到端VLA基线模型，任务进度提升了113%。

Conclusion: 通过将基于模型的运动规划与VLA交错集成，无需额外训练即可显著提升长时域移动操控任务的鲁棒性，证明了数据驱动方法与经典规划方法互补的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable progress for mobile manipulation, but their performance on long-horizon tasks remains poor. These tasks are especially challenging because (1) progress toward high-level goals must be maintained across extended sequences of spatially distributed subtasks, and (2) early execution errors compound rapidly over the task horizon. These challenges persist despite finetuning on large human teleoperated mobile manipulation data, indicating that more data alone may not resolve the problem. To address these challenges, we propose MPVI: Motion Planner / VLA Interleaving, a framework that integrates model-based motion planning with VLAs to improve robustness without further training. The proposed integration enables localization and navigation to distant or occluded target objects through cluttered scenes using open-vocabulary object detection, frontier exploration and motion planning. However, such integration is non-trivial, requiring reliable switching between modules; we show one way forward via VLM-based completion checking with proprioceptive triggers. We evaluate our approach on the BEHAVIOR-1K benchmark and demonstrate 113% improvement in task progress over a top end-to-end VLA baseline. Additional details are available at the project page: https://mpvi.netlify.app/.

</details>


### [94] [OSCAR: Obstacle Survival Curves for Adaptive Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00990)
*Hshmat Sahak,Aoran Jiao,Nicholas Rhinehart,Tim Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OSCAR框架，让移动机器人利用生存分析模型学习不同障碍物的持续时间分布，自适应地决定在受阻边前等待还是重新规划路径，从而在知识图谱导航中最小化到达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人沿已知路线图导航时，临时障碍物阻塞关键边会导致两难：等待太久浪费时间，立即绕行也会因障碍物很快移走而不高效。标准反应式避障只处理局部运动，固定等待/绕行规则忽略了不同障碍物类型的持续时间差异。

Method: 提出OSCAR框架，包括：1) 在线学习类条件残余清除时间分布（含右删失观测）；2) 将生存模型集成到时间相关图规划器中；3) 维护障碍物记忆，计算每个受阻边的耐心阈值（等待与绕行的平衡点）；4) 跨episode持续更新清除时间估计。

Result: 仿真中，学习策略的时间到目标收敛到与oracle（已知真实清除分布）相差1%以内，每类障碍物仅需不到20次观测即超越所有启发式基线。真实环境中，机器人在50次导航episode中在线提升性能，自适应调整耐心阈值。

Conclusion: OSCAR通过生存分析建模障碍物持续时间，使机器人能根据经验自适应地在等待与绕行之间做出最优权衡，显著提升图导航效率。

Abstract: A mobile robot following a graph of known routes can make costly navigation errors when a temporary obstacle blocks a critical edge: waiting too long behind a parked cart wastes time, but immediately rerouting around a person who would move in a few seconds is also inefficient. Standard reactive obstacle avoidance addresses local motion around obstacles, while fixed wait-or-reroute rules ignore how long different obstacle types tend to persist. We propose OSCAR: an adaptive survival-modeling framework for graph-based navigation with temporary blockages. Assuming obstacle class labels are available at encounter time, the robot learns class-conditioned residual clearance-time distributions from online experience, including right-censored observations when it reroutes before observing clearance. These survival models are integrated into a time-dependent graph planner that maintains obstacle memory and computes a patience threshold at each blocked edge: how long to wait before taking an alternate route. The method continuously updates its clearance estimates across episodes and uses them to balance waiting against rerouting. We evaluate the approach in simulation and on a real mobile robot in a university atrium with obstacles including people, chairs, bins, and tubes. In simulation, the learned policy's time-to-goal converges to within 1% of an oracle with access to ground-truth clearance distributions after fewer than 20 observations per obstacle class, outperforming all heuristic baselines. Real-world deployment confirms that the policy improves online, adapting its patience thresholds from experience across 50 navigation episodes.

</details>


### [95] [GraspGen-X: Cross-Embodiment 6-DOF Diffusion-based Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00998)
*Beining Han,Yu-Wei Chao,Erwin Coumans,Clemens Eppner,Balakumar Sundaralingam,Jia Deng,Stan Birchfield,Adithyavairavan Murali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨本体6-DOF机器人抓取方法，利用扩散模型和扫描体积启发式编码，在20亿抓取数据集上训练，实现了对新型夹爪形态和物体的零样本泛化，在仿真和真实实验中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6-DOF抓取模型只能泛化到新物体/场景，无法泛化到新的夹爪形态（不同末端执行器）及对应的物理抓取过程，限制了机器人抓取在不同硬件平台间的迁移能力。

Method: 基于扩散模型的生成式6-DOF抓取框架，通过条件注入夹爪表示实现跨本体泛化；提出扫描体积启发式编码（Swept-Volume Heuristic）来表示夹爪几何与运动特征；使用程序化生成的多样化夹爪和20亿抓取的大规模数据集进行训练。

Result: 在仿真中，对新型真实世界夹爪和物体的零样本泛化性能最佳；可作为微调的良好初始化；消融实验验证了扫描体积表示和程序化训练数据的有效性；在真实机器人上实现了对新型夹爪的零样本6-DOF抓取泛化，超越基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的跨本体6-DOF抓取方法首次实现了抓取模型在夹爪形态层面的零样本泛化，扫描体积编码和程序化数据生成策略是关键有效组件，为机器人抓取在不同本体间的迁移提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We study cross-embodiment 6-DOF robot grasping. Unlike prior works, we require the model not only to generalize to novel objects / scenes but also to novel gripper morphologies and physical grasping processes. Our method extends diffusion model based generative 6-DOF grasping models to condition on the additional gripper's representation. We propose a swept-volume heuristic for encoding the gripper. We train our cross-embodiment model with procedural grippers and a large-scale dataset of 2 Billion grasps. In simulation experiments, our model has the best zero-shot generalization to novel real-world grippers and objects over baseline methods. Our model also serves as a good initialization for fine-tuning to adapt to novel grippers. In ablations, we demonstrate the efficiency of our sweep-volume gripper representation and our procedural gripper training dataset. Last, we show zero-shot generalization to real-world novel grippers for 6-DOF grasping, surpassing baselines in cross-embodiment generalization.

</details>


### [96] [$τ_0$-WM: A Unified Video-Action World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01027)
*Pengfei Zhou,Shengcong Chen,Di Chen,Jiaxu Wang,Rongjun Jin,Bingwen Zhu,Yike Pan,Songen Gu,Kuanning Wang,Shufeng Nan,Xingyu Qiu,Chenhao Qiu,Pu Yang,Yunuo Cai,Jianxiong Gao,Yifan Li,Yanwei Fu,Xiangyu Yue,Zhi Chen,Jianlan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了τ₀-World Model（τ₀-WM），一个统一的视频-动作世界模型，将策略学习、视频预测和动作评估整合在单一的未来预测框架中。该模型基于共享的视频扩散骨干网络，提供视频动作模型和动作条件视频模拟器两个互补接口，在约27,300小时的真实机器人数据上训练，并通过测试时计算进行动作候选采样、重去噪一致性排序和模拟器修正，在长时序和精细操作任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要模型在物理执行之前生成可执行动作并预测评估其未来后果，现有方法缺乏统一的未来预测框架来同时实现策略学习、视频预测和动作评估。

Method: 提出τ₀-WM，基于共享的视频扩散骨干网络，包含：(1) 视频动作模型，联合预测未来视觉潜变量和连续动作块；(2) 动作条件视频模拟器，将候选动作块展开为多视角未来并预测任务进度分数。使用约27,300小时真实机器人数据训练，推理时通过测试时计算采样候选动作、重去噪一致性排名和模拟器修正。

Result: 在长时序和精细机器人操作任务上，τ₀-WM表现出优于其他相关基线的性能。

Conclusion: τ₀-WM通过统一的视频-动作世界模型框架，有效地整合了策略学习、视频预测和动作评估，为机器人操作提供了一种强大的未来预测驱动方法。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires models that generate executable actions while anticipating and evaluating their future consequences before physical execution. We present $τ_0$-World Model ($τ_0$-WM), a unified video-action world model that integrates policy learning, video prediction, and action evaluation within a single future-predictive framework. Built on a shared video diffusion backbone, $τ_0$-WM provides two complementary interfaces. First, a video action model jointly predicts future visual latents and continuous action chunks from multi-view observations, language instructions, and robot state. Second, an action-conditioned video simulator rolls out candidate action chunks into multi-view futures and predicts dense task-progress scores. The model is trained on approximately $27{,}300$ hours of real-robot teleoperation, UMI-style interaction, egocentric human videos, and rollout or failure trajectories using modality-specific supervision masks. At inference time, $τ_0$-WM uses test-time computation to sample action candidates, rank them with re-denoising consistency, and invoke simulator-based rectification for low-quality candidates. On challenging long-horizon and fine-grained robotic manipulation tasks, $τ_0$-WM shows superior performance over other relevant baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Position: Good Embodied Reward Models Need Bad Behavior Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01036)
*Ran Tian,Yilin Wu,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文主张构建可靠的具身奖励模型必须投入“坏”机器人数据（失败、次优、易错甚至危险的行为），因为当前模型因缺乏此类数据而系统性高估不安全、执行差或取巧的行为，而少量真实负面数据即可显著改善与人类偏好的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身奖励模型主要基于成功行为训练，导致其对不安全交互、不良执行和表面取巧策略给出不当高分，与人类真实评价严重偏离。作者旨在揭示这一数据缺口并呼吁社区重视。

Method: 分析三种最先进的具身奖励模型，系统对比其评分与人类评估者的差异；通过实验证明少量真实负面数据即可提升模型与人类偏好的一致性、减少假阳性错误。

Result: 现有模型系统性高估人类会惩罚的行为（不安全交互、执行差、取巧策略）；引入少量真实失败数据后，奖励模型与人类偏好对齐程度显著提升，假阳性率降低。

Conclusion: 呼吁具身AI社区整理并发布负面机器人数据、构建合成负面数据生成引擎、开发去中心化物理评估系统、设计细粒度具身奖励模型评估基准。

Abstract: This position paper argues that to obtain reliable embodied reward models, the community must invest in ``bad'' robot data: failed, suboptimal, error-prone, and even hazardous behaviors. While reward models are central to any foundation model's lifecycle, today's embodied reward models are trained primarily on successful behaviors. We analyze three state-of-the-art embodied reward models and find that they systematically over-reward behaviors that real human evaluators would penalize, including unsafe interactions, poor execution, and shortcut strategies that only superficially satisfy tasks. We attribute these failures to a key data gap: the scarcity of negative embodied data which is costly to collect and often filtered out or withheld in existing robotics datasets. Furthermore, we show that even modest exposure to real bad behavior data can improve alignment with human preferences and reduce costly false positives. We therefore call on the embodied AI community to curate and release their bad robot data, build synthetic bad data generation engines, develop more decentralized physical evaluation systems, and design benchmarks for fine-grained embodied reward model evaluations.

</details>


### [98] [Robust Integrated Planning and Control for Quadrotors in Dynamic Environments via NMPC with CBF Penalties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01038)
*Zeinab Shayan,Mohammadreza Izadi,Reza Faieghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多旋翼无人机的新型鲁棒集成规划与控制（IPC）策略，采用嵌入控制障碍函数（CBF）作为指数惩罚项的非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架，结合高增益扰动观测器（HGDO）和卡尔曼滤波器（KF），在硬件实验中验证了其优越的可行性、安全性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NMPC-CBF方法在处理紧输入约束下的平滑避障时可行性不足，且缺乏硬件验证；同时需要对外部扰动和动态障碍物运动进行实时补偿与预测，以提升系统在动态环境中的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出NMPC-CBF IPC框架，将CBF约束以指数惩罚项形式嵌入NMPC代价函数，避免硬约束导致的可行性问题；采用HGDO估计并补偿外部扰动；引入KF实现计算高效的障碍物运动实时预测。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真和实际硬件实验中，与常规NMPC及硬约束NMPC-CBF方法相比，所提策略在可行性、安全性和鲁棒性方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个经硬件验证的NMPC-CBF IPC框架，为四旋翼无人机在动态环境中的安全部署提供了实用的工程方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a new robust integrated planning and control (IPC) strategy for multirotor uncrewed aerial vehicles. We propose a nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) formulation that embeds control barrier functions (CBFs) as exponential penalties, improving feasibility while ensuring smooth obstacle avoidance under tight input bounds. The penalty weights provide a practical tuning knob to trade off tracking accuracy against avoidance aggressiveness. We enhance the system robustness by employing a high-gain disturbance observer (HGDO) to estimate and compensate for external disturbances. We also incorporate a Kalman filter (KF) for computationally efficient, real-time prediction of obstacle motion, enabling avoidance of moving obstacles. Comparative studies against both conventional NMPC and NMPC with hard CBF constraints, validated in Gazebo and hardware experiments, demonstrate superior feasibility, safety, and robustness. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first hardware-validated NMPC-CBF IPC framework, offering a practical step toward safe quadrotor deployment in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [99] [Learning Multi-Modal Trajectory Policies for Data-Efficient Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01047)
*Zijia Chen,Yuenan Hou,Xinhua Jiang,Yu Li,Weijie Li,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MATE框架，一种基于混合专家模型（MoE）的多模态轨迹预测策略，通过多模态MoE架构实现细粒度子标记特征解耦，并设计跨模态余弦路由器以实现稳定、尺度不变的专家分配，同时采用温度控制路由和随机噪声注入来防止专家坍缩。在LIBERO基准上，MATE在数据稀缺场景下平均成功率提升4.75%，并在真实机器人乒乓球实验中验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的策略将视觉、语言和轨迹等多模态输入在共享参数空间中处理，导致模态干扰和表示学习效率低下，尤其在数据稀缺场景下问题更突出。虽然MoE提供了专家 specialization 的解决方案，但传统路由机制对跨模态表示差异敏感，导致专家分配不稳定和专家坍缩。

Method: 1) 提出多模态MoE架构，实现细粒度子标记特征解耦；2) 设计跨模态余弦路由器，实现跨异构模态的稳定、尺度不变的专家分配；3) 采用温度控制路由和随机噪声注入，提升专家平衡性，防止数据稀缺下的路由过早坍缩。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，MATE在数据稀缺条件下持续优于先前工作，平均成功率比轨迹引导基线提升4.75%。真实机器人乒乓球实验验证了预测轨迹对下游机器人执行的指导意义和实际可行性。

Conclusion: MATE通过多模态MoE架构和创新的路由机制，有效解决了多模态输入在共享参数空间中的模态干扰问题，在数据稀缺场景下显著提升了轨迹预测性能和机器人操作的成功率，并验证了其在真实世界机器人任务中的实用性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires the effective integration of heterogeneous inputs, including visual observations, language instructions, and trajectory representations, to generate accurate actions. Existing transformer-based policies typically process these heterogeneous modalities within a shared parameter space, which often leads to modality interference and inefficient representation learning, especially in data-scarce scenarios. While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) offers a scalable solution through expert specialization, conventional routing mechanisms are often sensitive to such cross-modal representation discrepancies, resulting in unstable expert assignment and expert collapse. In this work, we propose MATE (Multi-ModAl TrajEctory Policies), a novel trajectory prediction framework built upon MoE. Specifically, we introduce a Multi-Modal MoE architecture to achieve fine-grained sub-token feature decoupling, and design a cross-modal cosine router for stable and scale-invariant expert assignment across heterogeneous modalities. We further employ temperature-controlled routing and stochastic noise injection to improve expert balance and prevent premature routing collapse under scarce demonstrations. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that our MATE consistently outperforms prior work under data scarcity. It achieves a 4.75% improvement in average success rate over the trajectory-guided counterpart. Real-world experiments on robotic ping-pong also suggest that the predicted trajectories can provide useful guidance for downstream robotic execution, further indicating the practical feasibility of our algorithm.

</details>


### [100] [Expanding Spatial and Temporal Context for Robotic Imitation Learning With Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01072)
*Jianing Qian,Qinhe Peng,Emmanuel Panov,Leonor Fermoselle,Dinesh Jayaraman,Bernadette Bucher,Tarik Kelestemur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出使用场景图作为模仿学习中的显式结构化记忆机制，通过在动态场景图中维护物体中心关系及其时间演化，使智能体在部分可观测的长时域任务中保留历史上下文，显著提升策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境（如家庭、办公室）存在严重部分可观测性，且许多任务需要跨长时间跨度执行子任务序列，现有模仿学习方法缺乏有效的显式记忆机制来应对这两大挑战。

Method: 在模仿学习中引入动态场景图作为显式结构化记忆，持续维护物体间关系及其随时间的变化，使智能体在任务执行过程中保留相关历史上下文，逐步累积场景信息以进行高效推理。

Result: 在模拟移动操作和真实桌面操作实验上，该方法显著提升了策略性能，尤其在需要长时推理和部分可观测下鲁棒泛化的场景中效果突出。

Conclusion: 场景图作为一种结构化记忆机制，能有效解决模仿学习在部分可观测、长时域任务中的局限性，提升策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to learn how to execute tasks via observation. However, real-world environments like homes and offices are often severely partially observed due to their large spatial scales. In addition, many tasks involve executing a series of subtasks requiring autonomous robots to reason over extended time horizons. To address these challenges, we propose using scene graphs as an explicit and structured memory mechanism in imitation learning. By maintaining a dynamic scene graph that captures object-centric relationships and their evolution over time, our method allows the agent to retain relevant historical context during task execution to efficiently reason over incrementally accrued scene information. Our experiments on simulated mobile manipulation and real-world tabletop manipulation demonstrate that our approach substantially improves policy performance, particularly in settings that demand long-term reasoning and robust generalization under partial observability.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Task Success: Behavioral and Representational Diagnostics for WAM and VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01095)
*Hung Mai,Bin Zhu,Tuan Do*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的诊断框架，系统比较了VLA策略和World-Action Models在机器人操作中的行为与表征差异。实验发现WAMs虽能改善目标选择性行为，但收益依赖架构且伴随更高推理成本，仅序列式WAMs展现出清晰的预测性表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管WAMs在机器人操作中引入未来预测机制，但尚不清楚这种预测能力是否带来了超越任务成功率的行为层面实质性改进，以及WAMs是否真正改变了机器人的行为和内部表征结构。

Method: 提出模型无关的诊断框架，包含两个互补视角：(1) 行为级卷展分析——测量动作动态一致性、目标物体进展、干扰物扰动和运行时成本；(2) 基于稀疏自编码器的特征分析——将内部表征分类为记忆型、反应型或预测型。在LIBERO和RoboTwin2.0上评估了7种策略，涵盖直接VLA、联合WAM、序列WAM和辅助WAM。

Result: (1) 仅靠成功率会掩盖关键差异；(2) WAMs通常能改善物体级行为和目标选择性；(3) 收益依赖具体架构且伴随更高推理成本；(4) 序列式WAMs展现出最清晰的预测性结构；(5) 辅助WAM和联合WAM分别压缩或纠缠了未来信息。

Conclusion: 未来WAM设计应致力于保留行为上可操作的未来表征，以实现高效的机器人操作控制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies and World-Action Models (WAM) represent two increasingly important paradigms for robotic manipulation. However, it remains unclear whether future prediction in WAMs leads to behaviorally meaningful improvements beyond final task success. In this paper, we ask whether WAMs merely add future prediction, or whether they change robot behavior and internal representations in ways that are actionable for control. We introduce a model-agnostic diagnostic framework that compares WAMs and VLAs through two complementary lenses: behavioral rollout analysis and sparse-autoencoder-based feature analysis. The behavioral protocol measures action dynamics consistency, target-object progress, distractor disturbance, and runtime cost. The feature-space protocol characterizes internal representations as memorized, reactive, or predictive, revealing whether models encode future-oriented structure. Across LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0, we evaluate 7 policies spanning direct VLAs and joint, sequential, and auxiliary WAMs. Our results show that success alone hides key differences: WAMs often improve object-level behavior and target selectivity, but their gains depend on architecture and incur higher inference cost. Sequential WAMs show the clearest predictive structure, while auxiliary and joint WAMs respectively compress or entangle future information. These findings suggest future directions for WAMs design to preserve behaviorally actionable future representations for efficient manipulation.

</details>


### [102] [Implicit Drifting Policy: One-Step Action Generation via Conditional Expert Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01098)
*Zemin Yang,Yaoyu He,Yiming Zhong,Yuhao Zhang,Xinge Zhu,Yao Mu,Qingqiu Huang,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式漂移策略(IDP)，一种一步模仿学习框架，通过局部几何结构隐式恢复训练时的漂移校正机制，无需显式向量场估计，在保持一步生成速度的同时实现对有效动作流形的约束，在2D、3D和真实操作任务上性能优于显式漂移方法且与强一步基线方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散或流匹配的生成式动作策略在行为克隆中表现出色，但其迭代采样过程对高频机器人控制而言延迟过高。近期的一步法公式解决了延迟问题，但不可避免地丢弃了提供关键动作校正的中间轨迹演化。直接通过显式估计训练时的漂移场来恢复这一机制，由于条件示范的极端稀疏性，在数学上是病态的。

Method: IDP从观测相似专家动作的局部变化中提取条件专家几何，并与全局参考几何进行对比以分离条件特定约束。这种局部几何结构自适应地加权标量势目标函数，结合专家近邻终端评估，在训练过程中直接对一步生成器施加流形约束。

Result: 在2D、3D和真实机器人操作任务上的广泛评估表明，IDP有效维持了对有效动作流形的遵循，优于显式漂移方法，并与强一步基线方法达到竞争力相当的性能。

Conclusion: IDP成功地将训练时的漂移校正机制引入一步模仿学习策略中，在不牺牲推理速度的前提下保持了动作流形约束，为高频机器人控制提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative action policies based on diffusion or flow matching excel in behavior cloning, yet their iterative sampling is prohibitive for high-frequency robot control. While recent one-step formulations alleviate this latency, they inevitably discard the intermediate trajectory evolution that provides crucial action correction. Directly recovering this mechanism by explicitly estimating a training-time drifting field is mathematically ill-posed due to extreme conditional demonstration sparsity. We introduce Implicit Drifting Policy (IDP), a one-step imitation learning framework that brings the training-time correction of Drifting into policy learning without explicit vector field estimation. IDP extracts a conditional expert geometry from the local variation of observation-similar expert actions, and compares it against a global reference geometry to isolate condition-specific constraints. This local geometric structure adaptively weights a scalar potential objective. Combined with an expert-proximal terminal evaluation, IDP directly enforces manifold constraints on the one-step generator during training. Extensive evaluations across 2D, 3D, and real-world manipulation tasks show IDP effectively maintains adherence to valid action manifolds, improving upon explicit drifting methods and achieving competitive performance with strong one-step baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Tether-Aware Dynamic Collision Avoidance for USV-HROV Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01112)
*Yang Gu,Ziyang Hong,Xuanlin Chen,Hao Wei,Cheng Wang,Shujie Yang,Yulin Si*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于缆绳感知的USV-HROV协同避障方法，通过平面安全域和速度障碍法，在跟踪HROV时避免水面障碍物并减少缆绳绷紧风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: USV在跟踪HROV时，动态避障面临缆绳与过往船只刮擦、以及规避机动导致USV-HROV间距增大进而引发缆绳绷紧的双重挑战，现有方法未充分考虑缆绳安全。

Method: 1) 提出缆绳安全感知平面域来表示缆绳与障碍船的三维碰撞风险，无需显式缆绳形状模型；2) 提出缆绳绷紧感知速度障碍法，实现安全避障同时降低缆绳绷紧可能性；3) 结合视线导引法协调HROV跟踪与避障。

Result: 基于Gazebo的仿真实验表明，该方法能有效规避动态障碍船，同时维护缆绳安全并降低USV规避机动时的缆绳绷紧风险。

Conclusion: 该方法为USV-HROV异构海洋机器人系统的水下缆绳巡检任务提供了一种有效的缆绳感知动态避障方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous marine robotic systems composed of an unmanned surface vehicle (USV) and a hybrid remotely operated vehicle (HROV) have shown great potential for subsea cable inspection. In such missions, the USV tracks the HROV at the surface while supplying power and communication through an umbilical tether. However, dynamic collision avoidance for the USV during HROV tracking is challenging because the submerged tether may scrape against passing vessels, while evasive maneuvers can enlarge the USV--HROV separation, thereby increasing the likelihood of tether tautness and compromising HROV operations. To address these challenges, this work proposes a tether-aware dynamic collision avoidance method for a USV tracking an HROV. First, a tether safety-aware planar domain is introduced to represent the three-dimensional collision risk between the tether and obstacle vessels without an explicit tether shape model. Second, a tether tautness-aware velocity obstacle method is developed to achieve safe avoidance while reducing the likelihood of tether tautness. Finally, the method is integrated with line-of-sight guidance to coordinate HROV tracking and collision avoidance. Gazebo-based simulations show that the proposed method avoids dynamic obstacle vessels while maintaining tether safety and reducing the likelihood of tether tautness during USV evasive maneuvers.

</details>


### [104] [ImagineUAV: Aerial Vision-Language Navigation via World-Action Modeling and Kinodynamic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01205)
*Xuchen Liu,Jiawei Huang,Shihao Xia,Bingxi Liu,Jinqiang Cui,Jiankun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ImagineUAV框架，通过级联世界-动作建模（潜视频扩散生成未来观测+动作提取器推断6-DoF运动+运动规划器优化轨迹），以1.3B参数实现无人机VLN任务，在基准测试和真实飞行中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在视觉-语言-导航任务中因几何不一致和动力学不匹配而表现脆弱，缺乏对未来环境演化的显式建模能力。

Method: 采用级联世界-动作建模：1) 潜视频扩散模型生成指令条件化的未来观测；2) 动作提取器从生成视频中推断6-DoF运动；3) 运动规划器优化无碰撞轨迹；4) 步蒸馏推理管线保证实时执行。

Result: 以1.3B参数在VLN基准测试和真实世界飞行中超越先前VLN和VLA基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了想象驱动（即显式建模环境演化）在无人机空中导航中的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) for UAVs demands grounding free-form instructions into 6-DoF flight under partial observability. While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at semantic reasoning, they suffer from brittleness due to geometric inconsistency and dynamics mismatch. To address this, we propose ImagineUAV, an imagination-driven framework leveraging cascaded world-action modeling. Instead of direct regression, ImagineUAV employs a latent video diffusion model to generate instruction-conditioned future observations, explicitly imagining environmental evolution, from which 6-DoF motions are inferred via an action extractor. A kinodynamic planner then refines these estimates into collision-free trajectories. Additionally, a step-distilled inference pipeline ensures real-time execution. With only 1.3B parameters, ImagineUAV outperforms prior VLN and VLA baselines on benchmarks and real-world flights, validating the practicality of imagination-driven aerial navigation.

</details>


### [105] [Training-Free Imitation Learning with Closed-Form Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01238)
*Raghav Mishra,Ian R. Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出闭式扩散策略（CFDP），一种无需训练的模仿学习方法，直接从演示数据集推导闭式分数进行策略生成，在毫秒级推理速度下达到与需要数小时训练的神经基线方法相当的性能，并支持推理时编辑与组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的策略虽然表现优异，但离线训练时间过长，拖慢了数据收集与策略部署的循环速度，亟需一种无需训练且高效的替代方案。

Method: 从演示数据集中直接推导出闭式分数（closed-form score），以此构建训练免费的扩散策略；在移动CPU上实现实时推理，并支持策略引导和演示增强等推理时编辑功能。

Result: 在模仿学习基准测试中，CFDP与需要数小时训练的神经基线方法性能相当；硬件实验表明其在移动CPU上毫秒级完成推理，速度快于神经扩散策略。

Conclusion: 闭式扩散策略在训练时间与性能之间提供了有利的权衡，并可作为可组合原语增强预训练的神经扩散策略的推理时编辑能力。

Abstract: While diffusion-based policies have impressive performance and expressivity, their long offline training slows down the data collection and policy deployment loop. We introduce Closed-Form Diffusion Policies, a class of training-free diffusion-based policies for imitation learning using the closed-form score derived from the demonstration dataset. We deploy CFDP with real-time inference with a mobile CPU in hardware experiments, showing it can successfully perform imitation directly from the dataset in milliseconds and with faster inference than neural diffusion policies. In experiments on imitation learning benchmarks, we show that CFDP is competitive against neural baselines that require hours of training, providing a favorable tradeoff between training time and performance. Finally, we show how closed-form diffusion policies act as a composable primitive that enables data-driven inference-time editing of pre-trained neural diffusion policies, including policy guidance and novel demonstration augmentation.

</details>


### [106] [OneVLA: A Unified Framework for Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01241)
*Lingfeng Zhang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Yingbo Tang,Lei Zhou,Shuyi Zhang,Jinkun Liu,Hongsheng Li,Chenhao Zhang,Qiang Zhang,Hangjun Ye,Xiaojun Liang,Long Chen,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OneVLA是一个统一导航与操作任务的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过统一动作头、多阶段渐进训练策略（包含数据构建和思维链微调）实现跨任务正向迁移，在仿真和真实环境中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型受限于任务特定的架构，分别专精于导航或操作任务，阻碍了通用机器人智能体的发展。

Method: 1) 设计统一动作头，可同时生成导航和操作动作；2) 提出多阶段渐进训练策略，包含精心构建的数据集和思维链(CoT)微调，促进两个领域间的正向迁移和相互增强。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中，OneVLA达到了SOTA性能，显著优于专门单任务模型和现有跨任务模型。

Conclusion: 通过统一导航与操作这两项核心能力，OneVLA为构建真正通用型机器人系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Navigation and manipulation are fundamental capabilities of embodied intelligence, enabling robots to interpret natural language commands and interact physically with their surroundings. However, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remain constrained by task-specific architectures, specializing in either navigation or manipulation, which hinders the development of general-purpose robotic agents. To bridge this gap, we introduce OneVLA, a unified architecture that integrates these distinct tasks into a single, cohesive framework. Specifically, we design a unified action head capable of generating both navigation and manipulation actions without requiring task-specific variants. Furthermore, we propose a multi stage progressive training strategy-incorporating curated data construction and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) fine-tuning that facilitates strong positive transfer and mutual reinforcement between the two domains. Extensive experiments in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that OneVLA achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming both specialized single-task and existing cross-task models. By unifying these core capabilities, OneVLA paves the way for truly general-purpose robotic systems. The model and source code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [107] [PSG-Nav: Probabilistic Scene Graph Navigation via Multiverse Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01313)
*Rufeng Chen,Yue Chang,Xiaqiang Tang,Hechang Chen,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PSG-Nav，通过构建3D概率场景图来建模感知不确定性，并引入多宇宙决策与证据经验校准器，在开放词汇导航任务中取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多采用局部最优的确定性方法，无法处理语义歧义和模型错误带来的感知不确定性，导致导航决策在全局上并非最优。

Method: 1) 构建3D概率场景图，用完整语义类别分布建模感知不确定性；2) 提出多宇宙决策，从联合分布中采样多个最可能的场景设置，评估导航地标与多宇宙的兼容性；3) 引入证据经验校准器，通过交叉验证当前检测与历史成功/失败记忆，实现在线终身适应性学习，缓解认知不确定性带来的误检。

Result: 在MP3D、HM3D、HSSD基准上分别达到66.1%、44.8%、67.9%的成功率，均取得SOTA。

Conclusion: PSG-Nav通过概率建模与多假设推理有效应对开放词汇导航中的感知不确定性，显著提升导航决策的全局最优性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary navigation requires embodied agents to manage significant perception uncertainty stemming from semantic ambiguity and model errors.
  However, most existing works settle for local optimal deterministic approaches, depriving complex navigation decision-making over multiple composite possibilities that are critical for globally better solutions.
  In this paper, we propose Probabilistic Scene Graph Navigation (PSG-Nav), which constructs a 3D Probabilistic Scene Graph that uses full semantic categorical distributions to account for perception uncertainty.
  To efficiently use the local distributions to compose and reason about the optimal navigation landmarks, we propose Multiverse Decision to sample multiple most likely world settings from the joint distribution, and evaluate navigation landmarks based on the compatibility between landmarks and multiverses.
  To mitigate false positives due to epistemic uncertainty in open-vocabulary navigation, we introduce the Evidential Experience Calibrator, which enables online lifelong adaptation by cross-validating detections against memories of past successes and failures.
  Extensive experiments on widely-used benchmarks MP3D, HM3D, and HSSD demonstrate that PSG-Nav establishes new state-of-the-art results, achieving Success Rates of 66.1%, 44.8%, and 67.9%, respectively. Code is available at: https://psg-nav.github.io/

</details>


### [108] [S2M-Trek: From Single to Multi-Sphere Transport via Per-Frame Deep Sets on a Wheel-Legged Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01332)
*Zong Chen,Xuebin Li,Jinpeng Xiao,Shaoyang Li,Ben Liu,Min Li,Zhouping Yin,Yiqun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究轮腿四足机器人背部同时运输多个自由滚动球体的动态操作问题，提出Per-Frame Deep Sets (PFDS) 编码器以显式处理每帧置换对称性，解决了标准历史拼接编码器因对称性不匹配导致的课程学习失败问题，最终在仿真中实现无掉落五球运输，并进一步通过DAgger蒸馏为基于触觉表征的TactSet策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准历史拼接集合编码器（如历史拼接Deep Sets）对多帧历史施加的是全局对角置换对称性，但多个相同自由滚动球体在每帧的排列可能独立变化（即每帧置换对称性），这种对称性不匹配导致强化学习出现具体失败模式——编码器利用球体槽位索引作为课程学习的捷径，而非学习无身份标识的多球动力学，在课程RL中无法超越两球阶段。

Method: 提出Per-Frame Deep Sets (PFDS)：先在每个历史帧内进行置换不变的池化操作（如Deep Sets的sum pooling），再对池化后的帧序列进行时序读取，从架构上确保对整个帧历史集合的置换不变性。在此基础上，通过DAgger将PFDS教师策略蒸馏为TactSet学生策略，用16×16布尔联合接触图替代特权球体状态观测，得到紧凑且天然具有每帧置换不变性的触觉表征。

Result: 在PPO训练预算内，扁平MLP、分支编码器和HCDS基线均无法通过两球阶段（除非对HCDS进行训练时球槽随机化）；而PFDS在所有5个随机种子下均达到五球阶段且100%无掉落运输。2×2消融实验分离了架构与数据增强（球槽随机化）的贡献。

Conclusion: 显式编码每帧置换对称性（PFDS）是解决多球动态操作中身份模糊性问题的关键；蒸馏后的触觉表征TactSet在保持对称性正确性的同时实现了紧凑部署。

Abstract: We study the problem of scaling dynamic loco-manipulation from a single free-rolling sphere to multiple spheres transported simultaneously on the back of a wheel-legged quadruped, without fences, grippers, or mechanical stops. Multiple identical free-rolling spheres form an unordered set with no persistent identity: their ordering may change independently at each history frame, creating a \emph{per-frame permutation symmetry} that standard history-concatenation set encoders do not explicitly enforce -- these encoders impose only a shared, diagonal permutation symmetry over the full history. We show that this symmetry mismatch leads to a concrete failure mode in curriculum-based reinforcement learning. Within the same PPO training budget, flat MLPs and branch-wise encoders plateau at or below the two-sphere stage, while a history-concatenation Deep Sets baseline (\HCDS) fails to progress past the two-sphere stage in our runs unless ball-to-slot assignments are randomised during training, suggesting that it exploits slot indices as a curriculum shortcut rather than learning identity-free multi-sphere dynamics. We propose \textbf{Per-Frame Deep Sets (\PFDS)}, which performs permutation-invariant pooling within each history frame before temporal readout; we prove that \PFDS is $\Gframe$-invariant and universally approximates continuous $\Gframe$-invariant policies. A $2{\times}2$ ablation over encoder architecture and slot randomisation separates the architectural and data-augmentation pathways, and \PFDS reaches the five-sphere stage with 100\% no-drop transport in simulation across all five random seeds. We further distill the \PFDS teacher into \TactSet via DAgger, replacing privileged sphere-state observations with a $16{\times}16$ Boolean union contact map, yielding a compact and naturally $\Gframe$-invariant tactile representation.

</details>


### [109] [ActMVS: Active Scene Reconstruction with Monocular Multi-View Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01367)
*Guo Pu,Yixuan Han,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActMVS是首个支持单目主动重建的框架，通过视图因子图构建和全局深度优化，在线生成高质量、全局一致的稠密深度图，使单目机器人/UAV能在重建过程中维护可靠占用地图，实现安全轨迹规划，性能媲美RGB-D方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动场景重建依赖深度传感器更新占用地图，增加了平台成本与重量；而单目场景重建方法离线运行且无法提供全局一致的稠密深度，难以满足机器人/UAV导航的帧率要求。为推进空间智能，需要一个纯视觉单目解决方案。

Method: 提出ActMVS框架，集成视图因子图构建以指导多视图立体深度预测，并结合全局深度优化，实现在线生成高质量、全局一致的稠密深度图。

Result: 在Replica数据集上，性能与RGB-D方法相当。

Conclusion: ActMVS填补了单目主动重建的空白，使单目机器人/UAV无需深度传感器即可实现安全的主动场景重建与轨迹规划。

Abstract: Active scene reconstruction enables robots/UAVs to autonomously plan trajectories and reconstruct environments without costly manual data acquisition. Unlike passive methods, active reconstruction requires real-time construction of high-confidence occupancy maps for collision-free navigation. Existing approaches rely on depth sensors for occupancy map updates, increasing platform cost and weight. To advance spatial intelligence, we aim for a vision-only monocular solution. However, current monocular scene reconstruction methods operate offline and fail to deliver globally consistent dense depth at the frame rates required for robots/UAVs navigation. To bridge this gap, we introduce ActMVS, the first framework for monocular active reconstruction. Our framework integrates a view factor graph construction for informed Multi-View Stereo depth prediction, along with a global depth optimization, to enable the online generation of high-quality, globally consistent dense depth maps. This enables monocular robots/UAVs to maintain reliable occupancy maps for safe trajectory planning during reconstruction. Experiments on Replica datasets demonstrate performance competitive with RGB-D methods. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/TrickyGo/ActMVS.

</details>


### [110] [A Sonar-Visual Dataset for Cross-Modal Underwater Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01398)
*Weitung Chen,Phil Tinn,Per Gunnar Auran,Martin Ludvigsen,Peter Halland Haro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SOVIS，一个包含76,000+配对帧的水声-视觉跨模态水下感知数据集，并展示了跨模态鱼检测任务中相较于单目相机基线7倍的mAP@0.10提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人通常同时使用相机和声纳进行感知，但受限于缺乏配对的水声-视觉数据集，跨模态预测映射的学习尚未充分探索。

Method: （1）构建了SOVIS数据集，包含在特隆赫姆峡湾6个地点17次潜水中收集的76,000+配对帧；（2）开发了端到端的数据清洗与同步管道；（3）设计了交互式标注工具以加速配对数据的标注过程。

Result: 在跨模态鱼检测任务中，使用少量标注数据实现了相较于单目相机基线7倍的mAP@0.10提升。

Conclusion: SOVIS作为跨模态水下感知研究的第一步，推动了从单目图像进行密集声纳预测等研究方向的发展。

Abstract: Underwater robots typically use both cameras and sonar for perception to leverage the rich semantic details of vision and the robust range measurements of acoustics. However, learning to map between these modalities via cross-modal prediction remains underexplored due to limited sonar-visual paired datasets. We present SOVIS, a sonar-visual dataset for cross-modal underwater perception. SOVIS comprises over 76,000 paired frames collected across 17 dives at six sites in the Trondheimfjord, supported by an end-to-end pipeline that cleans and synchronizes the cross-modal sensor data. We also introduce an interactive annotation tool designed to accelerate the labeling process for this paired data. Finally, we demonstrate a proof-of-concept cross-modal fish detection task using a small subset of labeled data, achieving a 7x improvement in mAP@0.10 over a monocular camera baseline. SOVIS serves as the first step toward advancing cross-modal underwater perception research, enabling research directions such as dense sonar prediction from monocular images.

</details>


### [111] [LEGS: Fine-Tuning Teleop-Free VLAs for Humanoid Loco-manipulation in an Embodied Gaussian Splatting World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01458)
*Hojune Kim,Timothy Chen,Jiankai Sun,Lars W. Osterberg,Qianzhong Chen,Ke Wang,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LEGS，一种混合仿真器，将前景网格（机器人、物体）叠加在通过手持设备重建的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）照片级真实背景上，无需人工遥操作即可大规模生成标注演示，并成功将策略从仿真迁移到真实人形机器人操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身操作（loco-manipulation）策略训练受限于遥操作数据采集的高成本和复杂性，且先前仿真微调的VLA策略无法有效迁移到真实人形机器人任务。

Method: LEGS混合仿真器：前景用网格（mesh）渲染机器人和物体，背景用3DGS重建照片级真实场景；使用过程化运动原语生成器自动合成带标注的演示数据；采用确定性两阶段颜色校准对齐渲染图像与部署相机的色彩空间。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人的三个拾取-放置任务和三种VLA骨干网络上，纯LEGS数据训练的策略在所有实验上匹配或超越遥操作数据训练的策略；在场景变化下，基于重渲染LEGS-AUG数据训练的策略保持任务成功，而遥操作基线完全失败。

Conclusion: 照片级真实渲染（3DGS背景）是仿真数据迁移的关键使能技术；LEGS的人形运动记录与场景外观解耦，使得同一自动生成演示可在新背景和物体网格下重渲染，成本比遥操作低15倍以上，有效增强策略对场景变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Training vision-language-action (VLA) policies for humanoid loco-manipulation is constrained by the high cost and complexity of collecting human teleoperation demonstrations. VLA policies fine-tuned in simulators have, until now, failed to transfer effectively in humanoid loco-manipulation tasks. We present LEGS (Loco-manipulation via Embodied Gaussian Splatting), a hybrid simulator that composites a mesh foreground (robot, objects, props) over a photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) background reconstructed from a handheld scene capture. LEGS uses a procedural motion-primitive generator to synthesize labeled demonstrations at scale without human teleoperation, and a deterministic two-stage color calibration to align the rendered 3DGS image to the robot's deployment camera. On a Unitree G1 humanoid robot, across three pick-and-place tasks of increasing whole-body difficulty and three VLA backbones (psi_0, pi_0.5, GR00T N1.6), a policy trained purely on LEGS data matches or exceeds one trained on human teleoperation demos on every experiment. It also outperforms a mesh-only simulation baseline that ablates the effect of the 3DGS background, showing that photorealistic rendering is a key enabler for synthetic data transfer. Humanoid motion is recorded independently of scene appearance in LEGS, allowing the same auto-generated demonstrations to be re-rendered under new backgrounds and object meshes--covering a new scene at more than 15x lower cost than teleoperation--to augment training data for robustness to scene variations. Under combined object-and-scene appearance shift, the policy trained on re-rendered LEGS-AUG data maintains task success while the baseline trained on teleoperation data fails entirely. Our project page is located at https://legsvla.github.io/.

</details>


### [112] [Spatio-Temporal Reconnection for Multi-Robot Networks using Adaptive Prescribed-Time CBFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01526)
*Hao Liu,Yupeng Yang,Yanze Zhang,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应预设时间控制屏障函数（adaptive PT-CBF）框架，允许多机器人系统在有限通信范围内临时断开并可在可调的预设时间内重新连接，同时引入考虑任务执行与重连紧迫性的触发机制，提升任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人系统中，传统方法要求保持通信图持续连通，这在机器人通信范围有限但工作环境广阔时过于严格。允许临时断开再重连更有利于高效任务执行，同时保证团队间及时的信息共享。

Method: 提出自适应预设时间控制屏障函数（adaptive PT-CBF）框架，使机器人能在可调的预设时间内临时断开并重新进入通信范围；引入重连触发机制，联合考虑任务执行与重连紧迫性，以原则性方式决定何时重连。

Result: 理论分析证明了在预设有限时间内实现满意重连的收敛性；实验验证了所提自适应PT-CBF在提升任务效率和实现满意重连方面的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应PT-CBF框架能够在保证预设时间内重连的前提下，允许机器人临时断开通信，从而提高任务执行效率，为重连决策提供了理论依据和实用方案。

Abstract: In multi-robot systems, maintaining persistent communication graph connectivity is often overly restrictive, especially when robots have limited communication ranges but operate in large environments. Instead, allowing robots to temporarily disconnect and later reconnect is often more desirable for efficient task execution while still ensuring timely information sharing across the team. In this paper, we propose an adaptive prescribed-time control barrier function (adaptive PT-CBF) framework that enables robots to temporarily disconnect and re-enter the communication range within an adjustable and feasible prescribed time. Moreover, we introduce a reconnection triggering mechanism that jointly considers task execution and reconnection urgency, thereby providing a principled way to decide when reconnection should occur. Theoretical analysis justifies convergence to the satisfying reconnection within a prescribed finite time. Experimental results validate the performance of our proposed adaptive PT-CBF with improved task efficiency and satisfying reconnections.

</details>


### [113] [Hierarchical Object Representation for Spatial Robot Perception: Points, Meshes, and Superquadrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01545)
*Ceng Zhang,Wan Su,Mohamed Samshad,Gregory S. Chirikjian,Rajat Talak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层物体表示方法，从原始传感器数据到稠密3D网格再到超二次曲面等解析基元，用于3D场景图中的高保真物体重建、鲁棒重定位和碰撞检测，并在室内外场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图中物体的几何表示被忽视，大多使用简化几何模型（如部分点云或3D边界框），缺乏高保真、可分析的表示。

Method: 提出四层分层物体表示：原始传感器数据→稠密3D网格→解析基元（超二次曲面），并从RGB-D图像流构建该表示。

Result: 在HOPE、ReplicaCAD、Kimera-Multi和NUS Campus数据集上验证了有效性，基于超二次曲面的地图对齐方法优于当前SOTA方法ROMAN。

Conclusion: 分层物体表示可同时支持高保真重建、鲁棒重定位和高效碰撞检测，适用于机器人在密集杂乱环境中的长期自主导航。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs (3DSG) have emerged as an actionable and scalable representation for long-term autonomy incorporating metric, semantic, and topological information in the scene. However, the question of geometric representation of objects in 3DSG has been overlooked as most methods use simplified geometric models such as partial point clouds or 3D bounding boxes. In this work, we introduce a hierarchical object representation that can be leveraged for high-fidelity object-level reconstruction, object-based robust re-localization or map alignment, and efficient and analytical collision checking for safe robot navigation planning in dense and cluttered environments. The representation is structurally organized into four distinct layers, progressively abstracting the scene from raw sensor data to dense 3D meshes to analytical primitives such as superquadrics, which provide a sparse and analytical representation for object geometry. We develop a pipeline that builds the hierarchical object representation from RGB-D image stream captured by a robot, and demonstrate its working in real-world open-set object scenes in both indoor and outdoor environments. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets including HOPE, ReplicaCAD, Kimera-Multi, and NUS Campus Dataset collected using Unitree B2 Robot validate our pipeline in both indoor and outdoor environments. We show that our superquadric-based map alignment method outperforms the current state-of-the-art object based map alignment method ROMAN. Our code can be found at https://github.com/perceptica-robotics/Hickory.

</details>


### [114] [Hierarchical Semantic-Augmented Navigation: Optimal Transport and Graph-Driven Reasoning for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01565)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HSAN框架通过构建动态层次化语义场景图、基于最优传输的拓扑规划器和图感知强化学习策略，在连续环境视觉-语言导航任务中实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在长程任务中因场景理解有限、规划效率低和缺乏鲁棒决策框架而表现不佳。

Method: 提出HSAN框架，包含三个创新：1) 利用视觉-语言模型构建动态层次化语义场景图，实现从物体到区域到区域的多级环境表示；2) 基于Kantorovich对偶的最优传输拓扑规划器，平衡语义相关性和空间可达性选择长期目标；3) 图感知强化学习策略实现精确的低级控制与障碍物规避。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的VLN-CE数据集上达到了最先进性能，在导航成功率和未知环境泛化能力上有显著提升。

Conclusion: HSAN通过融合谱图理论、最优传输和先进多模态学习，克服了静态地图和启发式规划器的局限性，为连续环境中的视觉-语言导航提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) poses a formidable challenge for autonomous agents, requiring seamless integration of natural language instructions and visual observations to navigate complex 3D indoor spaces. Existing approaches often falter in long-horizon tasks due to limited scene understanding, inefficient planning, and lack of robust decision-making frameworks. We introduce the \textbf{Hierarchical Semantic-Augmented Navigation (HSAN)} framework, a groundbreaking approach that redefines VLN-CE through three synergistic innovations. First, HSAN constructs a dynamic hierarchical semantic scene graph, leveraging vision-language models to capture multi-level environmental representations, from objects to regions to zones, enabling nuanced spatial reasoning. Second, it employs an optimal transport-based topological planner, grounded in Kantorovich's duality, to select long-term goals by balancing semantic relevance and spatial accessibility with theoretical guarantees of optimality. Third, a graph-aware reinforcement learning policy ensures precise low-level control, navigating subgoals while robustly avoiding obstacles. By integrating spectral graph theory, optimal transport, and advanced multi-modal learning, HSAN addresses the shortcomings of static maps and heuristic planners prevalent in prior work. Extensive experiments on multiple challenging VLN-CE datasets demonstrate that HSAN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with significant improvements in navigation success and generalization to unseen environments.

</details>


### [115] [Physics-Informed Modeling and Control of Emergent Behaviors in Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01597)
*Zixuan Jin,Wenzhuo Zhang,Shuxian Quan,Zirui Dong,Fangwen Ye,Yuchen Shi,Cheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhySwarm是一个物理信息驱动的微观-宏观框架，通过多相平流-扩散-反应模型描述群体密度场演化，结合神经网络控制器学习物理参数，实现多阶段机器人集群涌现行为的建模、学习和控制，并在多种任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人集群可通过局部感知和去中心化决策展现涌现行为，但多阶段涌现行为的建模与控制仍然困难，现有方法缺乏统一的物理信息框架来同时描述、学习和解释多阶段集群组织过程。

Method: 提出PhySwarm框架：宏观层使用多相平流-扩散-反应模型（Macro-ADR）描述密度场演化；微观层使用等效力运动模型（Micro-EDM）通过势场平流、密度梯度补偿和事件门控实现机制对应；神经物理控制器（NPC）将局部观测和历史映射为有界物理参数，采用强化学习-PINN联合目标函数训练。

Result: 在轨迹引导觅食、队形重构导航、角色自适应搜索救援等任务中，PhySwarm能生成多样化的多阶段涌现行为；学习到的密度场和物理参数提供了平流、扩散和反应如何联合调控多阶段集群组织的可解释证据。

Conclusion: PhySwarm建立了一条物理信息驱动的路径，用于学习、解释和控制机器人集群中的涌现行为。

Abstract: Robot swarms can exhibit coherent collective behaviors through local perception, limited communication and decentralized decision-making, yet modeling and controlling such emergence remains challenging when behaviors unfold over multiple phases. Here we introduce PhySwarm, a physics-informed micro--macro framework that represents multi-stage swarm emergence as physically constrained density-field evolution coupled to executable robot motion. At the macroscopic level, a multi-phase advection--diffusion--reaction model (Macro-ADR) describes phase-dependent swarm-density evolution through directed transport, diffusion-based spatial regulation and behavioral phase transitions. At the microscopic level, an equivalent deterministic motion model (Micro-EDM) realizes these mechanisms through potential-field advection, density-gradient compensation and rate- or event-gated phase switching. A neural-physics controller (NPC) maps local observations and temporal memory to bounded physical parameters, and is trained with a reinforcement learning--PINN objective that combines task rewards with macro-scale density residuals and micro-scale motion-consistency constraints. In several proof-of-concept swarm missions -- including trail-guided foraging, formation-reconfigurable navigation and role-adaptive search and rescue -- we demonstrate that PhySwarm can generate distinct multi-stage emergent behaviors within a unified physics-informed modeling framework. The learned density fields and physical parameters provide interpretable evidence of how advection, diffusion and reaction jointly regulate multi-stage swarm organization. These results establish a physics-informed route for learning, interpreting and controlling emergent behaviors in robot swarms.

</details>


### [116] [Embedding Semantic Risk into Distance Fields and CBFs for Online Monocular Safe Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01605)
*Dawei Zhang,Nuo Chen,Shuo Liu,Roberto Tron,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线单目感知-控制框架，通过将语义风险直接嵌入欧几里得符号距离场（ESDF），使得不同类别障碍物在安全场中具有差异化的空间影响，从而在控制优化前编码语义风险，实现基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的语义感知安全导航与远程操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于感知的安全滤波器对所有障碍物赋予相同的基于距离的安全裕度，或仅将语义信息作为下游控制器调整手段，而未将语义风险编码到空间表示中，导致高/低风险障碍物无法被差异化处理。

Method: 1) 基于基础模型的SLAM前端从单目RGB视频重建稠密3D几何；2) 逐帧语义分割提供像素级类别标签，与重建几何融合；3) 将几何-语义表示转换为ESDF，在计算距离场前根据语义标签对高风险区域进行类别依赖的膨胀处理；4) 语义感知ESDF为CBF控制器提供局部距离值和空间导数，类别依赖增益进一步调节控制器响应。

Result: 在仿真和硬件实验中实现了10-20 Hz的在线运行，并在远程操作和自主导航中展现出语义感知的安全行为。

Conclusion: 将语义风险直接嵌入距离场表示而非仅在控制器后端调整，能够更有效地实现类别感知的安全导航与远程操作，且保持在线运行效率。

Abstract: We propose an online monocular perception-to-control framework that embeds semantic risk into the distance field used by Control Barrier Function (CBF)-based safe navigation and teleoperation. Many perception-based safety filters assign the same distance-based safety margin to all mapped obstacles or use semantics only as a downstream controller adjustment, rather than encoding semantic risk in the spatial representation. Our framework instead reasons online about obstacle geometry and class-dependent risk by embedding semantic information directly into the Euclidean Signed Distance Field (ESDF). This design encodes semantic risk before control optimization, so high-risk objects exert a larger spatial influence in the safety field while retaining efficient ESDF queries at runtime. Specifically, a foundation-model-based SLAM front end reconstructs dense 3-D geometry from monocular RGB video, while per-frame semantic segmentation provides pixel-level class labels that are fused into the reconstructed geometry. The resulting geometric-semantic representation is then converted into an ESDF, where semantic labels identify safety-relevant regions and impose class-dependent inflation before field computation. The semantic-aware ESDF provides the local distance values and spatial derivatives required by the CBF controller, while class-dependent gains further regulate the controller response. Extensive simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate online operation at 10--20 Hz and semantic-aware safe behavior in both teleoperation and autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [117] [Trans2Occ: Voxel Occupancy Estimation and Grasp for Transparent Objects from Simulation to Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01777)
*Yixuan Yang,Sha Zhang,Rui Li,Zhenfei Yin,Xinzhu Ma,Yiran Qin,Lei Bai,Xudong Xu,Shilin Shan,Wangmeng Zuo,Yanyong Zhang,Wanli Ouyang,Feng Zheng,Shixiang Tang,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单视图RGB输入的透明物体感知与操作框架，通过预测体素空间占据直接实现3D理解，无需深度传感器或多视图重建。利用仿真管道生成多样化训练数据，该占据表示对域迁移鲁棒，可零样本迁移至真实机器人环境，并结合简单规则式抓取策略实现可靠操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明物体因折射和反射导致深度传感不可靠，现有方法依赖多视图重建或深度补全，难以在真实机器人系统中规模化部署。

Method: 提出单视图RGB到体素占据预测框架；构建仿真管道生成多样化材质与光照条件下的配对数据；采用规则式抓取策略基于占据表示实现抓取。

Result: 在仿真与真实环境中均实现了准确的3D理解与可靠抓取，占据表示对域迁移鲁棒，无需微调即可从仿真零样本迁移至真实场景。

Conclusion: 单视图占据预测为透明物体感知提供了一种可扩展且有效的解决方案，适用于机器人操作任务。

Abstract: Transparent objects remain challenging for robotic perception due to unreliable depth sensing caused by refraction and reflection. While prior approaches rely on multi-view reconstruction or depth completion, they are often difficult to scale or deploy in real-world robotic systems. In this paper, we present a practical framework for transparent object perception and manipulation based on single-view RGB input. Our approach predicts voxel-space occupancy directly from a single image, providing a geometry-aware representation that supports downstream robotic grasping. To enable large-scale training, we construct a simulation pipeline that generates paired RGB images and voxel occupancy annotations under diverse materials and lighting conditions. We demonstrate that the predicted occupancy representation is robust to domain shifts and transfers effectively from simulation to real-world robotic setups without fine-tuning. A simple rule-based grasping strategy built on top of the occupancy further achieves reliable grasp performance on transparent objects. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments show that our framework provides accurate 3D understanding and enables practical manipulation of transparent objects. These results suggest that single-view occupancy prediction offers a scalable and effective solution for transparent object perception in robotics.

</details>


### [118] [DisFlow: Scene Flow from Distance Field for Object Pose, Velocity Tracking, and Dynamic Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01824)
*Lan Wu,Sheila Sutjipto,Jennifer Wakulicz,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DisFlow是一个基于距离场的在线场景流估计框架，能够同时实现6DoF动态物体姿态估计、运动追踪和表面重建，采用高斯过程隐式曲面表示并在物体坐标系中进行概率融合，以实时速度输出密集几何、法线、姿态轨迹、速度和不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常在相机或世界坐标系中操作，物体在时间上缺乏几何一致性，且难以同时实现姿态估计、运动追踪和表面重建。

Method: 使用GPIS表示场景，以表面法线作为导数约束实现精确有符号距离计算；从距离场计算场景流描述表面点随时间的传输；通过在物体坐标系中进行增量式点云配准的闭式优化来估计物体姿态和运动。

Result: 在动态物体序列上实现了准确的姿态和运动追踪，同时重建了高质量的物体表面，且达到了实时运行速度。

Conclusion: DisFlow通过时空紧耦合在物体坐标系中实现概率融合，统一了6DoF姿态估计、运动追踪和表面重建三个任务，验证了该框架的有效性和实时性。

Abstract: We present \emph{DisFlow}, a novel framework for online scene flow estimation from distance field that enables \emph{6DoF dynamic object pose estimation}, \emph{motion tracking}, and \emph{surface reconstruction}. The scene is represented by Gaussian Process Implicit Surfaces (GPIS), with surface normals serving as derivative constraints, enabling accurate signed distance computations near the surface and gradient queries with uncertainty. With this representation as a foundation, we compute a scene flow from the distance field that describes how surface points are transported over time in consecutive frames. Through our flow, we can estimate an object's pose and motion by incrementally registering a new observed point cloud via an elegant closed-form optimisation. Unlike prior methods that operate in the camera or world frame, our approach performs probabilistic fusion directly in the \emph{object frame}, where the object remains geometrically consistent over time. The tight coupling of the DisFlow method in space and time yields dense geometry, surface normals, object pose trajectories, velocities, and uncertainty, all at real-time rates. We evaluate DisFlow on dynamic object sequences and demonstrate that it achieves accurate pose and motion tracking while simultaneously reconstructing high-quality object surfaces. Code publicly available at \href{https://github.com/LanWu076/disflow_ros2}{https://github.com/LanWu076/disflow\_ros2}

</details>


### [119] [The Lie We Tell: Correcting the Euclidean Fallacy in Vision Language Action Policies via Score Matching on Tangent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01847)
*Bing-Cheng Chuang,I-Hsuan Chu,Bor-Jiun Lin,YuanFu Yang,Min Sun,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lie Diffuser Actor (LDA)，一种在SE(3)流形上本征地运行的扩散框架，通过左不变SDE注入噪声、在切空间预测分数、利用指数映射回缩样本，解决了将SE(3)位姿表示为平坦ℝ¹²向量所导致的欧几里得谬误，消除了流形漂移、保证了坐标框架等变性及测地线最优性。在CALVIN ABC→D任务上平均任务长度从3.27提升至3.51（+7.3%），并在真实机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视觉-语言-行为策略将SE(3)位姿近似为平坦ℝ¹²向量，这种欧几里得谬误导致了三个根本性几何问题：(1)违反SO(3)约束的流形漂移，(2)坐标变换下等变性被破坏，(3)产生非测地线轨迹导致过高的运动学代价。

Method: 提出Lie Diffuser Actor (LDA)，在SE(3)流形上构建扩散框架：(1)通过左不变随机微分方程（SDE）注入噪声，(2)在切空间（李代数）中预测分数，(3)利用指数映射将样本回缩至流形。该方法通过构造保证了无流形漂移、坐标框架等变性和测地线最优性。

Result: 在CALVIN ABC→D基准上，平均任务长度从3.27提升至3.51，相对提升7.3%。在真实机器人实验中，LDA在大部分任务上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 在SE(3)流形上本征地建模扩散过程能够有效消除欧几里得谬误带来的几何误差，提升机器人操作策略在长任务序列上的表现，并适用于真实世界场景。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action policies achieve remarkable success in robotic manipulation, yet commit a fundamental geometric error we term the $\textbf{Euclidean Fallacy}$: representing SE(3) poses as flat $\mathbb{R}^{12}$ vectors. This approximation induces (1) manifold drift violating SO(3) constraints, (2) broken equivariance under coordinate transformations, and (3) non-geodesic trajectories with excessive kinematic cost. We introduce $\textbf{Lie Diffuser Actor (LDA)}$, a diffusion framework operating intrinsically on SE(3). Our method injects noise through left-invariant SDEs, predicts scores in the tangent space, and retracts samples via the exponential map. This formulation eliminates manifold drift by construction while guaranteeing coordinate-frame equivariance and geodesic optimality. On CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, LDA improves average task length from $3.27$ to $3.51$ ($+7.3\%$). We further validate our method on real robot and the results show that our methodology outperforms the baseline on majority tasks.

</details>


### [120] [PHASOR: Phase-Anchored Universal Action Representations for Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01851)
*Kihyun Kim,Chaeyun Kim,Jongho Shin,Taeyoun Kwon,Junghyun Kim,Mijin Koo,Haon Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将动作嵌入空间本身作为机器人策略学习的一等设计目标，通过将运动分解为周期性相位流形（FFT参数化）和非周期性姿态分支，并结合运动语义蒸馏，构建跨本体的可解释、与本体无关的运动流形，最终在多个人形机器人上实现统一的动作嵌入空间，提升了跨本体检索和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将动作隐变量视为任务特定的中间表示而非一等表征，导致嵌入空间无结构、与具体本体绑定、与运动语义关联弱，限制了可解释性、可控性和跨机器人迁移能力。本文旨在解决这一问题，将动作嵌入空间本身作为核心设计目标。

Method: 利用运动的固有周期性，将其分解为：(1) 相位流形，通过FFT参数化系数捕捉循环结构；(2) 姿态分支，将非周期性构型细节作为流形的条件。结合运动语义蒸馏，得到跨本体的可解释且与本体无关的运动流形，并将多个人形机器人锚定到共享的人类预训练流形上，形成统一的动作嵌入空间。

Result: 在多样化的机器人平台上实现了强跨本体检索性能和一致的下游任务性能提升。

Conclusion: 动作嵌入空间作为一等设计目标是可行且有效的，通过周期分解和语义蒸馏可获得与本体无关、可解释的统一运动表征，为跨机器人策略学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Learning a good action embedding space is fundamental to scalable robot policy learning, yet existing methods treat action latents as task-specific intermediates rather than first-class representations. The resulting latents are unstructured, embodiment-specific, and weakly tied to motion semantics, limiting interpretability, controllability, and transferability across robots. We position the action embedding space itself as a first-class design target, with downstream policy quality emerging from representation quality. Exploiting motion's intrinsic periodicity, we factorize it into a phase manifold that captures cyclic structure via FFT-parametric coefficients, together with a pose branch that conditions the manifold on non-periodic configuration detail. Combined with motion-semantic distillation, this factorized structure yields a cross-embodiment motion manifold that is interpretable and embodiment-agnostic by design. Anchoring multiple humanoid robots to a shared human-pretrained manifold then produces a unified action embedding space across diverse platforms, achieving strong cross-embodiment retrieval and consistent gains on downstream robot tasks.

</details>


### [121] [Set-Supervised Diffusion Policy: Learning Action-Chunking Diffusion through Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01865)
*Zhaoting Li,Gang Chen,Javier Alonso-Mora,Cosimo Della Santina,Jens Kober*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Set-Supervised Diffusion Policy (SDP)，一种利用人类纠正中的正负动作块对比数据来训练扩散策略的新框架，通过集合监督方式提升机器人操作策略的鲁棒性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略在机器人操作中易受分布偏移影响，需要人类在线纠正。但传统行为克隆方法忽略了纠正过程中产生的负动作（机器人错误动作）信号，导致对专家数据过度依赖且鲁棒性不足。

Method: SDP从人类纠正中提取配对的正确（正）和错误（负）动作块，构建期望动作块集合，设计训练流程使扩散策略与集合对齐，利用对比学习信号增强策略学习。

Result: 在多个机器人操作任务中，SDP持续提升策略性能，尤其在噪声数据下鲁棒性显著增强，并能生成高质量聚合数据集以提升后续学习效率。

Conclusion: SDP通过有效利用负样本信号，改进了人类在线纠正场景下的扩散策略学习范式，降低了对专家数据的依赖，提升了策略的鲁棒性和数据利用效率。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have recently emerged as a powerful framework for robotic manipulation. However, like other behavior cloning methods, they remain vulnerable to distributional shift, often requiring human-in-the-loop interventions to correct failures during deployment. These interactions naturally provide paired supervision in the form of the robot's undesired actions and the human teacher's corrective actions. Yet existing data aggregation pipelines and standard behavior cloning losses largely ignore this negative signal from undesired actions, leading to overfitting to teacher's actions and an increasing reliance on costly expert data. To address this limitation, we propose Set-Supervised Diffusion Policy (SDP), a novel learning framework that utilizes contrastive action-chunk data to train diffusion policies from human corrections. From paired positive and negative action-chunks, SDP constructs a set of desired action-chunks and designs a training pipeline that encourages the diffusion policy to align with the set. Through extensive experiments across multiple robotic manipulation tasks, we demonstrate that SDP consistently improves policy performance, with particularly strong gains in robustness to noisy data. Moreover, SDP induces high-quality aggregated datasets, enabling more efficient and reliable policy learning from human-in-the-loop corrections. Our code is available at https://set-supervised-diffusion-policy.github.io/.

</details>


### [122] [Closed-Form Pose Estimation of Endoluminal Medical Devices via Gradiometer-Based Electromagnetic Localization System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01946)
*Zhiwei Wu,Jiahao Luo,Yubo Pu,Siyi Wei,Yuankai Chen,Jinhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度计的电磁定位系统（GELS），利用紧凑型磁力计阵列作为准梯度计，通过欧拉齐次关系和多源Procrustes配准实现封闭形式的六自由度位姿恢复，无需预校准场图或迭代优化，实验显示位置误差10.80–15.57 mm，更新率14.49 Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入式磁跟踪方法需要预校准场图或迭代非线性优化，限制了其在远程导航中的实用性和实时性，亟需一种无需预校准、计算高效的封闭形式位姿恢复方案。

Method: 使用紧凑型磁力计阵列作为准梯度计估计局部磁场和梯度张量，通过欧拉齐次关系映射源与阵列之间的位移，再结合多源Procrustes配准（至少三个非共线源）恢复阵列的方位和位置，无需预校准场图、初始位姿猜测或校准源矩。

Result: 序列平均位置误差10.80–15.57 mm，最高更新率14.49 Hz，中位数求解器运行时间172.00 μs；扰动误差传播分析表明传感器间不一致性和偶极子模型失配是主要精度限制因素。

Conclusion: GELS提供了一种无需预校准的封闭式电磁定位框架，具有高计算效率和实时性潜力，误差分析为未来传感器阵列和磁源设计提供了优化方向。

Abstract: Embedded magnetic tracking holds highly attractive prospects for remote navigation of endoluminal medical devices. However, existing six-degree-of-freedom pose recovery approaches often require pre-calibrated workspace field maps or iterative nonlinear optimization. This letter presents a Gradiometer-Based Electromagnetic Localization System (GELS), a closed-form tracking framework that uses a compact magnetometer array as an embedded quasi-gradiometer to estimate local magnetic fields and gradient tensors. These quantities are mapped by the Euler homogeneous relation to displacements between source and array, from which multi-source Procrustes registration recovers the array orientation and position using at least three non-collinear sources. The algorithm requires known source positions and array geometry, but no pre-calibrated workspace field maps, initial pose guesses, or calibrated excitation-source moments. The recovered pose also enables a proof-of-concept sub-level dipole localization task by serving as a mobile magnetic reference frame. Benchtop experiments across sensor-array configurations and excitation modes demonstrate sequence-averaged position errors of \SI{10.80}{\milli\meter}--\SI{15.57}{\milli\meter}, a fastest update rate of \SI{14.49}{\hertz}, and a median solver runtime of \SI{172.00}{\micro\second}. A perturbation-based error propagation analysis further identifies inter-sensor inconsistency and dipole-model mismatch as the dominant accuracy limits, thereby informing future sensor array and magnetic source design for further reducing pose-estimation error.

</details>


### [123] [Learning Action-Conditional and Object-Centric Gaussian Splatting World Models for Rigid Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01950)
*Jens U. Kreber,Lukas Mack,Joerg Stueckler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MRO-GWM，一种以物体为中心的高斯世界模型，通过时空变换器架构从历史高斯和未来动作预测刚体运动，支持多物体场景和部分观测，在合成数据集和模型预测控制任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在处理3D环境中多刚体物体的动作条件动力学时存在局限，缺乏能够高效表示任意形状物体并处理多物体场景遮挡问题的统一框架。

Method: 以物体为中心的高斯表示（物体规范坐标系）+ 时空变换器架构（从历史物体高斯和未来动作预测刚体变换）。

Result: 在合成数据集（典型家居物体的多物体动态与机器人交互）上进行了预测性能分析，并在模型预测控制框架下评估了非抓取操控任务的表现。

Conclusion: MRO-GWM能够学习多刚体物体在3D环境中的动作条件动力学，支持任意形状物体的表示，并能从多视角重建下的遮挡观测中进行有效预测，为机器人非抓取操控提供了可行的世界模型方案。

Abstract: World models enable intelligent agents to predict the consequences of their actions on the environment. In this paper, we propose Multi Rigid Object Gaussian World Model (MRO-GWM), a novel model that learns action-conditional dynamics of rigid objects in 3D. By representing the scene by object-centric Gaussians, we can represent arbitrary object shapes and multi-object scenes. We develop a novel spatio-temporal transformer architecture that predicts future rigid body motion from a history of object Gaussians and future actions. Objects are represented by their Gaussians in a canonical frame, which allows for describing object motion as rigid body transformation. Our model is trained on reconstructions from multiple viewpoints, which requires the model to handle partial observations of objects due to occlusions. We analyze prediction performance of our approach on synthetic datasets composed of typical household objects with multi-object dynamics and interactions by a robot end effector. We also evaluate our model in model-predictive control for non-prehensile manipulation in simulation.

</details>


### [124] [Co-training with Ego-centric Video and Demonstration for Robot Navigation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01951)
*Shoya Kuno,Yumo Ouchi,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将人类第一人称行走视频转化为移动机器人模仿学习数据集的框架，通过估计相机运动并转换为地面机器人兼容的动作表征，联合训练VLA模型以提升语言理解和动作生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人收集大规模高质量训练数据成本高、耗时长，而现有将人类视频用于机器人操作任务的方法因移动机器人导航中的视角变化问题难以直接应用。

Method: 提出一个框架，从人类第一人称视频中估计相机运动，并将其转化为地面移动机器人可兼容的动作表征。通过联合训练VLA模型（人类衍生数据+机器人采集数据）来提升性能。

Result: 在水果搜索导航任务上，联合训练相比单独使用任一数据源，实现了更好的语言理解和更鲁棒的动作生成。

Conclusion: 人类第一人称视频可为移动机器人学习提供有效且可扩展的数据来源。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are promising for diverse robotic tasks, but their performance heavily depends on large-scale high-quality training data, whose collection on real robots is costly and time-consuming. While prior work has explored augmenting manipulation datasets with egocentric human videos, applying such approaches to mobile robot navigation remains challenging due to viewpoint changes during locomotion. In this paper, we propose a framework that converts egocentric walking videos into datasets for mobile robot imitation learning. The proposed method estimates camera motion from human videos and transforms it into action representations compatible with ground mobile robots. By jointly training a VLA model on human-derived and robot-collected datasets, the model achieves improved language understanding and more robust action generation than training with either data source alone. Experiments on a fruit-search navigation task demonstrate that human egocentric videos provide an effective and scalable data source for mobile robot learning.

</details>


### [125] [WALL-WM: Carving World Action Modeling at the Event Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01955)
*Shalfun Li,Victor Yao,Charles Yang,Truth Qu,Regis Cheng,Ryan Yu,Howard Lu,Newton Von,Vincent Chen,Yohann Tang,Maeve Zhang,Ellie Ma,Gody Li,Sage Yang,Lorien Shu,J. W. Gao,Ethan Chen,Colin Ye,Yu Sun,Elise Mon,PS Zhang,Neo Li,Lily Li,James Wang,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Lucy Liang,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WALL-WM提出了一种基于语义事件的世界动作模型，通过事件级视觉-语言-动作预训练解决固定长度动作块与语义粒度不匹配的问题，支持可变长度执行和统一推理两种模式，在大规模真实世界泛化评估中达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）通常将多模态或视频基础模型初始化为固定长度的动作块预测，但语言描述语义事件、视觉呈现连续场景动态、动作运行于控制级时间尺度，三者粒度不一致。将三者强制对齐到同一固定长度预测窗口，导致VLA训练退化为短视的相关性拟合。

Method: 提出事件级VLA预训练范式，以语义连贯的动作事件为原子学习单元；构建配套数据生态（事件级标注、聚类平衡采样）；支持两种推理模式：事件模式（消费下一事件描述，输出可变长度执行块）和统一模式（结合VLM与Staircase Decoding，在固定长度推理中保持梯度连续的VLA路径）；采用Muon优化器进行大规模预训练。

Result: 在语言、场景和任务上均展现出广泛泛化能力，在大规模真实世界泛化评估中达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 基于事件粒度的VLA预训练是构建通用世界动作模型的有效可扩展方案，解决了固定长度动作块预测的语义粒度错配问题。

Abstract: WALL-WM is a World Action Model that shifts video-action learning from chunk-centric optimization to event-grounded Vision-Language-Action pretraining, using semantically coherent action events as the atomic unit of learning. Existing WAMs commonly initialize from multimodal or video foundation models and then optimize fixed-length action chunks conditioned directly on the current observation and instruction. Although convenient, this chunk-centric formulation creates a fundamental granularity mismatch. Language describes semantic goals and events, vision evolves through continuous scene dynamics, and actions operate at control-level timescales; forcing all three into the same fixed-length prediction window turns VLA training into short-horizon correlation fitting. WALL-WM addresses this mismatch by organizing both supervision and data around semantic events. Specifically, it pairs event-grounded VLA pretraining with a data ecosystem built from event-level captions and cluster-balanced sampling, enabling scalable learning over diverse behaviors, scenes, and task structures. From the same event-pretrained backbone, WALL-WM supports two complementary inference modes. The event mode consumes next-event descriptions and enables variable-length execution chunks, while the unified mode uses a VLM with Staircase Decoding to condition conventional fixed-length chunk inference while preserving a gradient-continuous VLA path. Together with Muon-optimizer-based large-scale pretraining infrastructure, WALL-WM provides a practical scale-up recipe for general-purpose WAMs. Experiments show that WALL-WM generalizes broadly across language, scenes, and tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance in large-scale real-world generalization evaluation.

</details>


### [126] [World-Task Factorization for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02027)
*Eduardo Sebastián,Adrian Pfisterer,Vito Mengers,Oliver Brock,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将机器人策略分解为世界因子与任务因子的基本方法，通过AICON可微图模型与紧凑策略的梯度接口实现结构泛化，在多种机器人、环境和任务中优于端到端基线方法，并实现零样本泛化与真实硬件迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习需要产生能够泛化到新约束、新队友和新环境组合的策略。现有方法在结构设计上差异很大，缺乏对策略最根本的分解方式的理解。作者认为最基础的分解是将世界与任务分离，研究这种分解成立的条件及其优越性。

Method: 通过贝叶斯模型证据形式化世界因子（具身系统与环境属性，独立于意图）与任务因子（任务逻辑）的不对称性。实例化方法：AICON（可微递归估计器与互连图）+ 紧凑学习策略，以梯度作为两个因子之间的接口，梯度携带世界结构（通过图）和任务结构（通过代价函数）。

Result: 在异构机器人、环境、任务逻辑和感觉运动模态的三类问题中，该方法在所有设置下优于端到端基线和解析启发式方法，能零样本泛化到分布外配置，并可无需重新训练迁移到真实硬件。

Conclusion: 世界/任务分解是机器人策略设计的基本原则性方法，通过梯度作为接口实现了低维学习与结构泛化的兼顾，验证了该分解在理论上的合理性和实践中的有效性。

Abstract: Robot learning must produce policies that generalize to new combinations of constraints, teammates, and environments. To achieve this, we must structurally factor the policy, which is a choice that dictates what generalizes, what requires retraining, and what remains entangled. Existing methods span a wide spectrum, from expecting structure to emerge from data scaling, to hand-designing it via hierarchies, skill libraries or learned specializations. In this paper, we study what we argue is the most fundamental factorization in robotics: separating the world from the task. We investigate the conditions under which this factorization is principled. World factors are properties of the embodied system and the environment; they exist independently of intent. Task factors are defined by the task's logic over what the world admits. We formalize this asymmetry through Bayesian model evidence: it aligns with the data-generating process, maintains high likelihood through an analytical world model, and reduces the Occam razor's penalty on task parameters. We instantiate this factorization by pairing AICON, a differentiable graph of recursive estimators and interconnections that is compositional, operates without task-specific data, and propagates cost gradients to actuators, with a compact, learned policy that modulates gradient paths. Gradients serve as the interface between the two factors: they carry world structure through the graph and task structure through costs, enabling low-dimensional learning while preserving structural generalization. We test the world/task factorization across three problems that encompass heterogeneous robots, environments, task logic and sensorimotor modalities. Our framework outperforms end-to-end baselines and analytical heuristics in all settings, generalizes zero-shot to out-of-distribution configurations, and transfers to real hardware without retraining.

</details>


### [127] [Network Distributed Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Consensus Control of Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02107)
*Youssef Mahran,Zeyad Gamal,Aamir Ahmad,Ayman El-Badawy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种网络分布式多智能体强化学习（ND-MARL）框架，用于四旋翼无人机一致性控制。该框架将通信拓扑融入决策过程，采用2-邻居通信拓扑，每个智能体仅观测两个邻居信息并通过分布式策略输出动作。高层分布式一致性规划器使用MASAC训练，嵌入层次化架构生成参考目标位置，由底层四旋翼控制器跟踪。结果表明，相比集中式MARL，该方法实现了平滑的一致性轨迹和规划-跟踪器集成。最值得注意的是，在三智能体系统上训练的策略无需微调即可零样本扩展到250个智能体的大规模集群，展现了出色的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体强化学习（MARL）在处理四旋翼无人机一致性控制时，要么依赖集中式规划（可扩展性差），要么完全分散执行（忽略通信结构）。现有方法未能有效利用群组通信图来指导决策，导致大规模部署时性能受限。

Method: 提出ND-MARL框架：1) 将群组通信图（2-邻居拓扑）显式融入决策过程；2) 每个智能体仅观测两个邻居信息，通过分布式策略输出动作；3) 高层使用多智能体SAC（MASAC）训练分布式一致性规划器；4) 层次化架构：高层规划器生成参考目标位置，底层四旋翼控制器执行跟踪。

Result: 1) 相比集中式MARL，ND-MARL实现了更平滑的一致性轨迹和更好的规划-跟踪器集成；2) 关键发现：在三智能体系统上训练的policy，无需重新训练或微调，即可零样本扩展到250个智能体的大规模集群；3) 在大规模情况下，由于稀疏信息传播，稳态散布增加但仍能一致收敛。

Conclusion: ND-MARL是一种稳定、可扩展的分布式通信感知四旋翼一致性控制框架，其零样本可扩展性为大规模无人机集群部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Network Distributed Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (ND-MARL) framework for quadcopter consensus control. Compared to conventional multi-agent MARL formulations that rely on centralized planning or fully decentralized execution, ND-MARL incorporates the swarm communication graph into the decision process. Under a 2-Neighbor communication topology, each agent observes information of only two neighbors and outputs an action through a distributed policy. A high-level distributed consensus planner is trained using Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC) and embedded in a hierarchical stack to generate reference target positions tracked by a low-level quadcopter controller. Results demonstrate smooth consensus trajectories and planner-tracker integration when compared to a centralized MARL controller. Most notably, the learned controller exhibits zero-shot scalability, as policies trained on a three-agent system are deployed to swarms of up to 250 agents under the same 2-Neighbor communication topology without retraining or fine-tuning, achieving consistent convergence with increasing steady-state spread at large team sizes due to sparse information propagation. These findings highlight ND-MARL as a stable framework for distributed, communication-aware quadcopter consensus control.

</details>


### [128] [FW-NKF: Frequency-Weighted Neural Kalman Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02251)
*Adnan Harun Dogan,Berken Utku Demirel,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.RO
