TL;DR: 本文提出频率加权神经卡尔曼滤波器（FW-NKF），通过将因果频谱整形算子嵌入卡尔曼测量残差，并联合学习观测网络与状态转移网络，实现对频带噪声的主动抑制，在混沌系统与人体位姿估计等任务中定位误差降低最高10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典卡尔曼滤波器难以处理频率相关干扰与模型失配（如传感器振动、电磁干扰、周期性噪声），现有深度卡尔曼滤波变种虽能学习潜在状态转移，但缺乏显式抑制频带噪声的机制。

Method: 将因果频谱整形算子（频域加权）嵌入卡尔曼测量残差，联合学习观测网络与状态转移网络，使滤波器频谱和潜在状态表示同时自适应调整，衰减噪声主导频带并捕获残差结构。

Result: 在多维Lorenz混沌系统、全身惯性位姿估计等四个异构基准上，定位误差降低最高10%，方位精度显著提升；消融实验证实频率加权与深度潜状态建模各有贡献。

Conclusion: FW-NKF通过频域与潜状态联合自适应学习，有效抑制频带噪声，优于经典卡尔曼滤波与现有深度卡尔曼滤波方法。

Abstract: Robust state estimation is central to robotic autonomy, yet classical Kalman filters struggle with frequency-dependent disturbances and model mismatch such as sensor vibrations, electromagnetic interference, and periodic noise. Although Deep Kalman Filter (DKF) variants extend the Extended Kalman Filtering (EKF) framework by learning latent transitions, they lack explicit mechanisms to suppress band-limited noise components that typically corrupt sensor measurements in real-world scenarios. We introduce the Frequency-Weighted Neural Kalman Filter (FW-NKF), a unified hybrid approach that embeds a causal spectral-shaping operator into the Kalman measurement residual and jointly learns observation, and transition networks. By adapting both the filter spectrum and the latent state representation, FW-NKF attenuates the noise-dominated frequency bands while capturing complex residual structures. We conduct extensive experiments on four heterogeneous benchmarks, including chaotic systems such as multi-dimensional Lorenz systems and full-body inertial pose estimation, and find a reduction in localization error of up to 10% as well as marked improvements in orientation accuracy. Our ablation studies confirm that frequency weighting and deep latent-state modeling contribute to overall performance.

</details>


### [129] [Dexterity-BEV: Aligning 3D World and Actions for Generalizable Robot Policies Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02274)
*Huayi Zhou,Wei Gao,Dekun Lu,Ruiji Liu,Zhanqi Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Jian Chen,Wenlve Zhou,Sheng Xu,Shumin Li,Kangyi Guo,Shichen Xu,Zixin Huang,Yongyi Su,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Dex-BEV，通过引入对齐顶点图和顶点谱作为3D表示，并构建规范鸟瞰图对齐框架，解决端到端操作策略中2D基础模型忽略3D特性及空间对齐不足的问题，提升了操作策略的时空一致性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端操作策略结合大规模预训练VLM虽有前景，但继承了两个关键局限：依赖2D RGB输入忽略操作的3D本质，以及输入输出空间及不同机器人本体、相机设置、轨迹数据集之间缺乏3D空间对齐。

Method: 1) 提出对齐顶点图（aligned vertex map）和顶点谱（vertex spectrum）作为像素级3D表示；2) 通过将每像素3D信息与机器人动作映射到共享坐标系实现输入输出对齐；3) 设计规范BEV对齐框架并创新性构建BEV图像，得到视角不变表示；4) 开发数据处理流水线和对齐方案，包括跨机器人、操作员和数据集的时空对齐。

Result: 贡献了一套完整的解决方案，包括3D表示、输入输出空间对齐、BEV规范框架、数据处理流水线，有效减轻了输入输出时空错位，提升了真实世界操作的一致性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将2D VLM的泛化能力与3D空间感知相结合，并系统性地解决跨视角、跨本体、跨数据集的时空对齐问题，为通用灵巧机器人操作提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: End-to-end manipulation policies, combined with web-scale pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs), show the promise for generalizable and dexterous robotic manipulation. However, they inherit two key limitations from 2D foundation models: 1) the reliance on 2D RGB inputs that ignores the intrinsically 3D nature of manipulation; and 2) the lack of spatial 3D alignment between input-output spaces as well as across diverse robot embodiments, camera setups, and trajectory datasets. In this paper, we present a series of contributions to address these issues. First, we introduce aligned vertex map and vertex spectrum -- a pixel-wise 3D representation that elevates 2D visual inputs to 3D, using camera calibration and optional depth. This novel input representation marries 3D awareness with the generalization of 2D large VLMs. Then, we propose to align the inputs and outputs of manipulation policies by expressing per-pixel 3D information of each camera view and robot actions to a shared coordinate. Based on this, we designate a canonical Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) alignment frame and innovatively propose to construct BEV images, producing a view-invariant representation robust to camera pose variations. To enable training and evaluation at scale, we develop a comprehensive data processing pipeline to perform such alignments; we also introduce a novel temporal alignment scheme for trajectories across diverse robots, human operators, and datasets. These contributions collectively mitigate input and output spatial-temporal misalignments, improving the consistency and generalization for real-world manipulation. Pretrained checkpoint, source code and data processing pipeline are available in https://hnuzhy.github.io/projects/Dex-BEV.

</details>


### [130] [RoboSemanticBench: Diagnosing Semantic Grounding in Action Prediction for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02277)
*Bin Yu,Yao Zhang,Haishan Liu,Shijie Lian,Yuliang Wei,Xiaopeng Lin,Zhaolong Shen,Changti Wu,Ruina Hu,Bailing Wang,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboSemanticBench (RSB)基准，用于诊断VLA模型在动作预测中是否真正利用了指令语义。实验发现，许多VLA模型虽然能执行抓取动作，但在需要根据数学或常识知识选择正确目标时，其语义选择准确率接近或低于随机水平，揭示了骨干网络的语义能力与动作预测之间的持续鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型基于预训练语言/视觉骨干的语义理解来指导机器人动作预测，但微调过程仅优化为模仿任务特定的动作分布，许多评估可通过视觉或指令-动作捷径解决。作者希望通过RSB基准诊断VLA模型在复杂语义理解下的真实动作选择能力。

Method: 设计了RSB基准，包含多选数学或常识问题，机器人需观察候选答案块并抓取正确块。覆盖控制算术、小学数学理解、常识/事实理解三种难度，设置四选和十选两种选择规模。通过控制抓取成功后的语义选择准确率评估模型。

Result: 代表性VLA模型在抓取成功率尚可，但控制抓取成功后的语义选择准确率接近或低于随机水平，表明模型存在"能抓但不会选"的问题。

Conclusion: VLA模型骨干网络的语义能力未能有效转化为动作预测中的正确目标选择，存在骨干语义水平与动作预测之间的根本性脱节。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are built on the premise that semantic understanding from pretrained language or vision-language backbones should guide robot action prediction. Yet robot fine-tuning is optimized as imitation over task-specific action distributions, and many evaluations can be solved through visual or instruction-action shortcuts. We introduce RoboSemanticBench (RSB), an embodied benchmark for diagnosing semantic grounding in action prediction: whether post-trained VLA models can use complex instruction semantics to select and manipulate the correct physical target. In each episode, a robot receives a multiple-choice math or general-knowledge question, observes candidate answer blocks, and must grasp the block corresponding to the correct answer. RSB covers controlled arithmetic, grade-school mathematical understanding, and commonsense or factual understanding under four-choice and ten-choice suites. Across representative VLA models, we find that many policies learn to grasp candidate blocks but select the semantically correct block at near-random or below-random rates after controlling for grasp success, revealing a persistent gap between backbone-level semantic competence and action prediction.

</details>


### [131] [Dynamics Are Learned, Not Told: Semi-Supervised Discovery of Latent Dynamics Geometries For Zero-Shot Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02280)
*Zhiming Xu,Weitao Zhou,Xianghui Pan,Nanshan Deng,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen,Chenpeng Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结果导向的动力学自适应方法，通过对比学习约束轨迹编码器的Lipschitz常数，构建平滑且任务相关的潜在拓扑结构，使机器人策略能够适应未建模和时变的动力学变化，在MuJoCo基准测试中优于传统的参数中心方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习策略在物理动力学发生变化时容易失效，而现有参数中心方法依赖于预先指定的物理参数维度，无法应对未建模或复合动力学变化，亟需一种更鲁棒的动力学自适应方法。

Method: 提出从结果导向视角出发，通过对比学习对轨迹动力学编码器的Lipschitz常数进行上界约束，构建平滑的潜在拓扑结构，使策略能够学习动力学如何影响交互结果，而无需显式的动力学参数信息。

Result: 在MuJoCo基准测试中，该方法在严重动力学变化（包括未建模和时变参数）下持续优于参数中心基线方法，同时提升了分布内稳定性和潜在表征可解释性。

Conclusion: 控制潜在几何结构是实现鲁棒动力学自适应的一种有原则的有效机制。

Abstract: Real-world dynamics shifts pose a critical challenge for reinforcement learning in robotics, as policies tightly coupled to nominal environments often fail catastrophically when physical conditions change. Most existing methods rely on encoding explicitly identified physical parameters into a latent context, a parameter-centric paradigm that depends on pre-specified axes of variation and becomes brittle under unmodeled or compound dynamics changes. We revisit dynamics adaptation from an outcome-centric perspective: rather than telling policies what the dynamics are, we enable them to learn how dynamics affect interaction outcomes. Theoretically, this is grounded in a monotonic relationship between target-domain regret and the Lipschitz constant of a trajectory dynamics encoder. Practically, this constant can be upper-bounded through contrastive learning, yielding a smooth, task-relevant latent topology without privileged dynamics information. On MuJoCo benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms parameter-centric baselines under severe dynamics shifts, including unmodeled and time-varying parameters, while also improving in-distribution stability and latent interpretability. Overall, these results validate that controlling latent geometry is a principled mechanism for robust adaptation.

</details>


### [132] [A Kinetic Theory of Encounter-Based Information Propagation in Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02296)
*Alkesh K. Srivastava,Philip Dames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遭遇的信息传播动力学理论，用于分析多机器人系统中通过物理相遇传递目标跟踪信息时的性能瓶颈，识别了访问限制、时效限制和几何限制三个极限，并通过大规模仿真验证了该分解框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统无法保证持续的网络连接，而目标跟踪性能依赖于信息感知、传输和使用的时效性。当机器人仅通过物理遭遇交换信息时，跟踪问题转化为动力学信息传输问题，需要理解信息传播的极限和性能边界。

Method: 建立基于遭遇的信息传播动力学理论，识别访问、时效和几何三个极限；通过大规模仿真，改变团队规模、操作区域、通信范围和目标速度等参数进行验证。

Result: 支持访问-时效-几何分解：通信覆盖主导访问转变；信息可访问后，跟踪误差由目标位移塑造；该响应在受限区域内是局部线性的，但更广范围内因感知刷新和有界几何呈现非线性。访问和时效坐标能可靠描述跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 建立了一个动力学理论框架，用于预测和设计基于遭遇的多机器人系统，为无持续网络连接下的多机器人协作提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems cannot assume persistent network connectivity. We study this problem through target tracking, where performance depends on how quickly target information is sensed, transported through the team, and used before it becomes stale. When robots exchange information only through physical encounters, tracking becomes a kinetic information-transport problem: robot motion induces encounters, encounters carry target-state estimates, information age determines staleness, and stale information produces tracking error. This paper develops a kinetic theory of encounter-based information propagation and identifies three limits. The first is an access limit -- information cannot support team-level coordination unless it spreads beyond the robots that sensed it. The second is a staleness limit -- even propagated information loses value as the target moves. The third is a geometry limit -- when target motion outpaces information transport, tracking error approaches a saturation regime where communication improvements alone have diminishing returns. We evaluate the theory through large-scale simulations varying team size, operating area, communication range, and target speed. Results support the proposed access-staleness-geometry decomposition: communication coverage governs the access transition; once information is accessible, tracking error is shaped by target displacement; and this response is locally linear in restricted regimes but nonlinear over broader ranges because of sensing refreshes and bounded geometry. Across controlled sweeps and joint variation, the derived access and staleness coordinates reliably describe tracking performance. Together, these results establish a kinetic-theoretic framework for predicting and designing encounter-based multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [133] [FATE-VLA:Failue-aware test generation for vision-language-action models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02307)
*Arusa Kanwal,Pablo Valle,Shaukat Ali,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型评估中静态基准测试难以发现高维具身空间中稀疏且聚类的失败案例的问题，本文提出了一种主动发现失败的测试生成方法，通过多样性驱动探索与代理模型结合，将测试引导至高风险区域。在四个SOTA VLA模型上，该方法最多可多发现29.7%的失败，并揭示了更多样的失败模式，例如GR00T-N1.6的成功率从64.4%降至34.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型评估依赖静态基准测试，随机采样任务场景，但在高维具身空间中，失败是稀疏且聚类的，静态基准测试无法充分暴露鲁棒性风险，因此需要将VLA评估重构为主动的失败发现问题。

Method: 提出了一种失败感知的测试生成方法：结合多样性驱动的探索与从观测执行中学到的代理模型，将测试引导向高风险且多样化的场景区域。

Result: 在四个SOTA VLA模型上，该方法相比基线最多多发现29.7%的失败，并揭露了更多样化的失败模式；例如GR00T-N1.6的成功率从64.4%下降至34.7%。

Conclusion: VLA评估应从被动地在固定任务套件上测量，转向适应性、主动寻求失败的测试生成，在部署前暴露模型弱点的结构。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly used as generalist robot policies, yet their evaluation still relies largely on static benchmarks that randomly sample task scenes. In high-dimensional embodied spaces, failures are sparse and clustered, so static benchmarking can underestimate robustness risks. We reframe VLA evaluation as an active failure-discovery problem and propose a failure-aware test-generation approach that combines diversity-driven exploration with surrogate models learned from observed executions. The method steers testing toward high-risk yet diverse scene regions. Across four state-of-the-art VLA models, it uncovers substantially more failures (up to +29.7 % over selected baselines) while revealing more diverse failure modes. This mean that, for instance, in the case of GR00T-N1.6, success rate dropped from 64.4% to 34.7%. More broadly, our findings call for a shift in VLA evaluation: from passive measurement on fixed task suites to adaptive, failure-seeking test generation that exposes the structure of model weaknesses before deployment.

</details>


### [134] [Towards Precise Intent-Aligned VLA Aerial Navigation via Expert-Guided GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02313)
*Tianyang Chen,Wenjun Li,Xin zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EG-GRPO框架，通过强化学习微调VLA模型实现无人机精准意图对齐导航，相比SFT基线成功率提升2.13倍，意图对齐性能提升60.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准监督微调（SFT）在无人机精细指令导航任务中面临数据稀缺、泛化有限和对复杂人类意图监督弱等挑战，而强化学习微调虽能通过可设计反馈对齐策略行为，但在大规模连续空间中探索效率低下。

Method: 提出EG-GRPO（专家引导群体相对策略优化），利用少量专家数据增强在线rollout；设计异构流水线实现并行仿真与推理，减少43.5%的rollout时间。

Result: 在多个复杂人类意图任务中，EG-GRPO将成功率提升至SFT基线的2.13倍，意图对齐性能提升60.9%。

Conclusion: 该框架能推动无人机导航向精准意图对齐飞行迈进。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising end-to-end paradigm for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to accomplish complex tasks specified by fine-grained instructions. However, standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) suffers from data scarcity, limited generalization, and weak supervision for nuanced and complicated human intents. Reinforcement fine-tuning offers a natural way to mitigate these challenges and align policy behaviors with human intents through designable feedback, but applying it to aerial navigation remains challenging due to inefficient exploration in expansive continuous spaces. To address these challenges, we introduce an efficient reinforcement learning (RL) framework for VLA-based aerial navigation. At its core, we propose EG-GRPO (Expert-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization) to augment online rollouts with few-shot expert data. Additionally, we design a heterogeneous pipeline enabling parallel simulation and inference, which reduces rollout time by 43.5%. Across multiple tasks specified by complex human intents, EG-GRPO improves the success rate to 2.13x that of the SFT baseline, while improving intent alignment performance by 60.9%. These results demonstrate that our framework can move aerial navigation toward precise intent-aligned flight.

</details>


### [135] [A Simulation Platform for Flapping-Wing Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02370)
*Haichuan Li,Tomi Westerlund*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FWAV-Sim，一个基于Unity的高保真扑翼飞行器仿真框架，通过集成复合气动模型、分形噪声湍流生成和真实传感器模拟，弥合了仿真与现实之间的差距，显著提升了自主系统的仿真能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扑翼飞行器仿真平台依赖过度简化的层流假设和理想化传感器模型，无法捕捉真实运行中复杂的湍流模式和感知限制，导致仿真与现实的差距严重阻碍了自主系统的发展。

Method: FWAV-Sim框架包含：(1) 结合准稳态叶素理论与钝体阻力效应的复合气动模型，(2) 通过分形噪声合成生成时空相关湍流，(3) 含噪声IMU、LiDAR点云和RGB相机的真实传感器模拟，支持可扩展的同步数据集生成。

Result: 在FWAV-Sim中开发的自主管道（包括控制器和感知系统）展现出显著的仿真能力提升，推进了基于仿真的扑翼飞行系统开发性能。

Conclusion: FWAV-Sim通过高保真仿真有效弥合了仿真与现实差距，为扑翼飞行器自主系统的开发提供了更可靠的仿真平台。

Abstract: Flapping-wing aerial vehicles (FWAVs) demonstrate remarkable agility but face substantial autonomy challenges due to their high sensitivity to aerodynamic disturbances and limited sensor payload capacity. Current simulation platforms typically rely on oversimplified laminar flow assumptions and idealized sensor models, failing to capture the complex turbulence patterns and perceptual limitations encountered in real-world operation. This simulation-to-reality discrepancy significantly impedes the development of robust autonomy systems for FWAVs. We introduce FWAV-Sim, a high-fidelity Unity-based simulation framework that integrates: (1) a composite aerodynamic model combining quasi-steady blade-element theory with bluff-body drag effects, (2) spatiotemporally correlated turbulence generation through fractal noise synthesis, and (3) realistic sensor simulation including noisy IMU measurements, LiDAR point clouds, and RGB camera feeds. Our platform enables scalable generation of synchronized datasets containing ground-truth vehicle states, aerodynamic forces, turbulent wind fields, and multi-modal sensor streams. Experimental validation demonstrates that autonomy pipelines (including both controllers and perception systems) developed in FWAV-Sim exhibit significantly improved simulation capability, thereby advancing the outstanding performance in simulation-based development for flapping-wing aerial systems.

</details>


### [136] [NDPP-Grasp: Non-Differentiable Physical Plausibility Constraint-Guided Task-Oriented Dexterous Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02432)
*Qiuchi Xiang,Haoxuan Qu,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将物理合理性引导直接注入任务对齐抓取扩散模型去噪过程的框架，无需后处理修正，在保持任务对齐的同时提升生成抓取的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法将任务对齐与物理合理性解耦处理，先训练扩散模型对齐任务，再通过后处理修正物理合理性。这种事后修正策略仅在抓取生成后施加物理引导，生成轨迹本身缺乏物理约束，导致次优抓取。

Method: 提出一个将物理合理性引导直接注入任务对齐抓取扩散模型去噪过程的框架，即使物理合理性约束不可微，也能在去噪全过程引导抓取生成，同时保持任务对齐。

Result: 大量实验证明了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 将物理合理性引导注入去噪过程可替代事后修正策略，在不牺牲任务对齐的前提下显著提升生成抓取的物理合理性。

Abstract: Task-oriented dexterous grasp generation aims to produce dexterous grasp poses that are both physically plausible and functionally suitable for specified manipulation tasks. Existing diffusion-based methods often address these two requirements in a decoupled manner: they first train a grasp diffusion model for task alignment and then rely on post-generation refinement to improve physical plausibility. However, this after-the-fact correction strategy applies physical plausibility guidance only once the grasp has already been generated, leaving the generation trajectory itself unguided by physical constraints and potentially leading to suboptimal grasps. To address this problem, we propose a novel framework that directly injects physical plausibility guidance into the denoising process of a task-aligned grasp diffusion model in a practical and effective manner, even when physical plausibility constraints are non-differentiable. This allows physical plausibility to shape grasp generation throughout denoising while preserving task alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy of our framework.

</details>


### [137] [Intercepting the Future: Latent-Space Predictive World Model for Dynamic VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02486)
*Shahram Najam Syed,Arthur Jakobsson,Haoran Hao,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AHEAD是一个轻量级预测-然后-行动包装器，通过运动感知的潜世界模型预测未来视觉特征，使冻结的VLA模型能够处理动态物体抓取任务，在仿真和物理机器人上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型假设场景在观测和执行之间保持静止，导致物体运动时因延迟而抓取失败，亟需一种能预测物体运动并提前规划动作的方法。

Method: 提出AHEAD包装器：训练一个小型世界模型，在VLA特征空间中预测未来patch token，利用光流提取的逐token速度和加速度进行条件预测；通过语言-运动显著性掩码聚焦任务相关区域；自适应预测步长，在不确定性超过阈值时停止并替换当前特征为预测的未来特征。

Result: 仅增加4.9M参数到7B OpenVLA上，在20个动态仿真场景中达到79-97%成功率（最强基线31-58%）；在物理xArm 7机器人上，传送带和滚球任务29-30/30，球拍拦截23/30，抛射物接住19/30（基线均为0/30）。

Conclusion: AHEAD通过轻量级运动预测有效弥合了VLA模型在动态场景中的延迟-精度鸿沟，为低成本适配现有VLA模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models generalize across static manipulation but fail when objects move during task execution. They map the current observation to an action and assume the scene is stationary between observation and execution, so at any non-trivial object speed the resulting latency exceeds the time available to grasp. We close this gap with AHEAD (Anticipatory Horizon Extrapolation with Adaptive Dynamics), a predict-then-act wrapper that augments a frozen VLA with a motion-aware latent world model. A small world model trained on manipulation video forecasts future patch tokens in the VLA's feature space, conditioned on per-token velocity and acceleration from optical flow. A language-and-motion saliency mask concentrates prediction on task-relevant patches, and the model rolls forward for an adaptive horizon, halting when prediction uncertainty crosses a threshold. The frozen action decoder then receives the predicted future tokens in place of the current ones. AHEAD adds 4.9M parameters to a frozen 7B OpenVLA and reaches 79 to 97% success across 20 dynamic simulation scenarios where the strongest baseline reaches 31 to 58%. On a physical UFactory xArm 7, AHEAD succeeds on 29/30 to 30/30 on three conveyor and rolling-ball tasks, 23/30 on paddle interception, and 19/30 on projectile catching where every baseline scores 0/30.

</details>


### [138] [IMAC-AgriVLN: Can Agricultural Vision-and-Language Navigation Agents be Aware of Instruction Mistakes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02519)
*Xiaobei Zhao,Xingqi Lyu,Xin Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对农业VLN任务中指令可能包含错误这一现实问题，本文提出A2A-MI基准（包含半自动数据标注器生成三类错误指令）和IMAC模块（分析指令与前视图像，判断并纠正指令错误），实验表明现有模型在错误指令上性能大幅下降（SR降57%，NE升9%），而IMAC能有效弥补这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业VLN方法假设指令总是正确的，但在现实场景中用户可能说出包含错误的指令，因此需要让机器人具备识别和纠正指令错误的能力。

Method: 1) 构建A2A-MI基准，设计半自动数据标注器向原始指令中插入三类错误；2) 提出IMAC模块，通过分析指令与前视图像的语义一致性来判断指令是否有误，并在必要时尝试纠正。

Result: SOTA农业VLN智能体在A2A-MI上SR下降57%、NE上升9%；集成IMAC后性能显著提升，接近无错误指令时的水平。

Conclusion: 农业VLN智能体需要具备对指令错误的感知与纠正能力，IMAC模块是填补这一能力空白的高效方案。

Abstract: Agricultural robots are serving as powerful assistants across a wide range of agricultural tasks, nevertheless, still heavily relying on manual operations or railway systems for movement. The AgriVLN method and the A2A benchmark pioneeringly extended Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) to the agricultural domain, enabling a robot to navigate to a target position following a natural language instruction. However, almost all the prior methods adopt an ideal assumption that the given instructions themselves are correct, which does not align with the realistic scenarios, because anybody may say an instruction with mistakes. To bridge this gap, we propose the A2A-MI benchmark, in which we build a semi-automatic data annotator to insert three mistake classifications into each original instruction in a more diversified and efficient way. We test several state-of-the-art agricultural VLN agents on it and observe a sufficient drop with -57% on SR and -9% on NE, from which we suggest that an agricultural VLN agent tends to assume that the given instruction is correct, so does not have the awareness to doubt it when the scenes it sees do not align with the instruction it receives. To build the awareness on instruction mistake, we propose the IMAC module analyzing the instruction and the current front-facing image, to judge whether the instruction has mistakes and attempt to correct it when needed. We integrate IMAC into the baseline model, and observe a noteworthy improvement, sufficiently narrowing the gap to the performance on instructions without mistakes. Project: https://github.com/AlexTraveling/IMAC-AgriVLN.

</details>


### [139] [AFUN: Towards an Affordance Foundation Model for Functionality Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02551)
*Zhaoning Wang,Yi Zhong,Jiawei Fu,Henrik I. Christensen,Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AFUN，一个面向功能理解的 affordance 基础模型，能从单张RGB-D图像和语言任务描述中预测任务条件功能掩码（在哪里交互）和3D接触后运动曲线（如何交互），并通过大规模标准化数据管道实现开放世界泛化，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法，且无需微调即可部署于真实机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 affordance 方法要么仅定位任务相关区域而不指定可执行运动，要么预测运动但可扩展性有限，缺乏一个既能理解交互位置和方式、又能跨多样环境、物体和任务泛化的基础模型。

Method: 1) 从单张RGB-D观测和语言任务描述出发，预测任务条件功能掩码和3D接触后运动曲线；2) 构建大规模标准化数据管道，将异构的机器人、人类、仿真和真实世界扫描数据转换为统一的 affordance 标注模式（包含语言、掩码和以物体为中心的3D运动标签）。

Result: 1) 功能分割：在4个基准的8个测试集上超越所有基线，平均gIoU/cIoU提升+23.9/+26.3；2) 接触点预测：预测精度显著更高，命中率较最佳基线提升12.7%-61.3%；3) 3D运动：在所有三个测试集上均达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: AFUN可作为 affordance 基础模型，无需针对机器人具体形态微调或使用任务特定启发式规则，即可部署于真实世界机器人操作，展现出适应开放世界 affordance 任务的能力。

Abstract: Affordance understanding bridges visual perception and physical action, serving as an explainable interface for robot manipulation in open and unstructured real-world environments. Yet, building an affordance foundation model that not only understands where and how the interaction should happen, but also generalizes across diverse environments, objects, and tasks, remains a long-standing research challenge. Existing methods typically address only part of this challenge, either localizing task-relevant regions without specifying executable motion, or predicting motion but with limited scalability. In this paper, we present ourmodel, a step towards an affordance foundation model for functionality understanding. From a single RGB-D observation and a language task description, ourmodel predicts a task-conditional functional mask (where to interact) and a 3D post-contact motion curve (how to interact). To support open-world generalization, we build a large-scale standardized data pipeline that converts heterogeneous robot, human, simulation, and real-world scan data into a shared affordance schema with language, masks, and object-centric 3D motion labels. We evaluate ourmodel from three aspects: for affordance segmentation, ourmodel outperforms all baselines by a large margin across 8 test sets from 4 benchmarks, improving mean gIoU/cIoU by +23.9/+26.3; for contact-point prediction, it predicts substantially more accurate points, with a 12.7--61.3% hit-rate gain over the best baseline; and for 3D motion, it achieves the best performance on all three test sets. ourmodel can be deployed for real-world robot manipulation without finetuning for robot embodiment or using task-specific heuristics, demonstrating the ability to adapt to open-world affordance tasks. Project page: https://www.zhaoningwang.com/AFUN

</details>


### [140] [RoboDream: Compositional World Models for Scalable Robot Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02577)
*Junjie Ye,Rong Xue,Basile Van Hoorick,Runhao Li,Harshitha Rajaprakash,Pavel Tokmakov,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以具身为中心的世界模型，通过将生成过程锚定于渲染的机器人运动并显式条件化场景与物体先验，实现可扩展的演示数据合成。该方法支持两种数据扩展能力：轨迹重生（将现有轨迹迁移至新场景）和无道具遥操作（操作员空手操作，模型后验生成物体与场景）。实验证明生成数据能有效提升下游策略性能并减少真实数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习的可扩展性受限于遥操作收集真实世界演示数据的高昂成本和时间消耗，现有生成方法仅停留在表面视觉增强或产生不可行的物理运动。

Method: 提出可泛化的以具身为中心的世界模型，将生成锚定于渲染的机器人运动轨迹，并显式条件化场景和物体先验，从而解耦轨迹执行与环境合成。实现两种数据扩展能力：检索与重生（将已有轨迹复用到全新上下文）和无道具遥操作（操作员空手操作，模型后验生成目标物体和场景）。

Result: 在多种操作任务上的真实实验表明，生成的数据能持续提升下游策略性能，并显著减少真实世界数据需求。

Conclusion: 该方法通过解耦轨迹与环境生成，在无需新运动数据的情况下实现大规模多样化演示合成，为机器人学习的数据扩展提供有效途径。

Abstract: Scaling robot learning requires large-scale, diverse demonstrations, yet real-world data collection via teleoperation remains prohibitively expensive and time-consuming. While video diffusion models offer a promising avenue for data scaling, existing generative approaches are often limited to superficial visual augmentation, or suffer from embodiment hallucinations that yield physically infeasible motions. We present a generalizable embodiment-centric world model that achieves scalable data generation by synthesizing photorealistic demonstrations with novel objects, in novel scenes, and from novel viewpoints. Our approach anchors generation to rendered robot motion while conditioning on explicit scene and object priors, effectively decoupling trajectory execution from environment synthesis. This formulation has the potential to unlock two powerful data scaling capabilities: (1) retrieval and rebirth, which repurposes existing trajectories into entirely new contexts without new motion data; and (2) prop-free teleoperation, where operators manipulate empty air and the model hallucinates the target objects and scene afterwards, eliminating reset time. We demonstrate with real-world experiments that our generated data consistently improves downstream policy performance and significantly reduces real-world data requirements across diverse manipulation tasks.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Contraction Analysis of Time-Delay Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00179)
*Rintaro Watanabe,Yu Kawano*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性时滞系统的收缩分析，将Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函和Lyapunov-Razumikhin函数的概念扩展到微分框架下，证明了它们的存在性等价于一致增量指数稳定性，并基于线性矩阵不等式设计了单时滞非线性系统的镇定反馈控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性时滞系统广泛存在于工程实践中，但其稳定性分析尤其是收缩分析（增量稳定性）在时滞系统中尚未得到充分研究，缺乏系统化的理论框架。

Method: 首先将Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函扩展到微分框架下，证明其存在性与增量Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函等价且保证一致增量指数稳定性；然后将Lyapunov-Razumikhin函数扩展到微分框架下，同样证明其保证一致增量指数稳定性；最后将理论结果应用于单时滞非线性系统的镇定控制设计，转化为线性矩阵不等式条件。

Result: 建立了微分Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函和微分Lyapunov-Razumikhin函数与时滞系统一致增量指数稳定性之间的等价关系，并给出了基于LMI的镇定控制器设计方法。

Conclusion: 本文为非线性时滞系统的收缩分析提供了完整的理论框架，并通过LMI条件为控制器设计提供了可计算的实现路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate contraction analysis for nonlinear time-delay systems described by functional differential equations. We first extend the concept of Lyapunov-Krasovskii functionals within the differential framework. We then show that its existence is equivalent to that of an incremental Lyapunov-Krasovskii functional and guarantees uniform incremental exponential stability. Next, we extend the concept of Lyapunov-Razumikhin functions within the differential framework, whose existence also ensures uniform incremental exponential stability. As an application of our results, we formulate stabilizing feedback control design for nonlinear time-delay systems with single delays in terms of linear matrix inequalities.

</details>


### [142] [Predicted-Flow Control Barrier Functions for Real-Time Safe Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00297)
*Amirsaeid Safari,Jesse B. Hoagg*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出预测流控制障碍函数（P-CBF），通过将CBF从当前状态的函数推广到预测流上的泛函，结合终端候选P-CBF和规划时间偏移，实现了可行且向前不变的安全最优流控制，并在非完整约束地面机器人上验证了其优于非线性MPC和CBF安全滤波方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CBF合成困难且控制器具有短视性，难以在保证安全性的同时实现长远优化目标。

Method: 提出P-CBF，将CBF推广为预测流上的泛函；引入终端候选P-CBF要求预测流终止于备份安全集；引入规划时间偏移作为额外自由度；通过单一凸优化联合确定控制计划参数和规划时间偏移。

Result: 在100次试验中，FlowBarrier实现了最高的目标到达率、零安全违规和最低计算时间，优于非线性MPC和两种CBF安全滤波方法。

Conclusion: P-CBF框架统一了有限时域积分成本优化与安全认证，且当控制约束为凸多面体时可简化为二次规划，具有良好的实时性和安全性保证。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide real-time safety guarantees through pointwise conditions on the state. However, synthesizing a valid CBF is difficult and the resulting controllers are myopic. To address myopia, this article introduces predicted-flow control barrier functions (P-CBFs), which generalize the CBF from a function of the current state to a functional of a predicted flow under a parametrized control plan over a finite prediction horizon. For safety, a P-CBF can certify that the predicted flow is in a safe set over the entire prediction horizon. However, candidate P-CBFs suffer from the same challenge as candidate CBFs, namely, control constraints make it difficult to guarantee that the P-CBF is valid. This article resolves this challenge by introducing a terminal candidate P-CBF requiring that the predicted flow end in a backup safe set at the terminal time, and a planning-time shift that modulates the prediction horizon, providing an additional degree of freedom to ensure feasibility. The real-time control and the evolution of the control-plan parameter and planning-time shift are determined jointly by a single convex optimization that is guaranteed to be feasible and renders the associated safe set forward invariant. The resulting safe optimal flow control provides a safety certificate over the entire prediction horizon and unifies finite-horizon integral-cost optimization with safety certification. This optimization reduces to a quadratic program (QP) if the control constraints are a convex polytope. The QP implementation, termed FlowBarrier, is validated on a nonholonomic ground robot navigating a dense environment. FlowBarrier is compared to nonlinear model predictive control and two CBF-based safety filter methods across 100 trials, where FlowBarrier achieves the highest goal-reaching rate, zero safety violations, and the lowest computation time.

</details>


### [143] [Generalized Model Predictive Path Integral Control as Expectation--Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00317)
*Jiarui Wang,Sina Sharifi,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文证明MPPI控制可视为EM算法在概率最优控制中的特例，提出了广义EM-MPPI框架，分析了其收敛性，并给出了高斯参数化下的全局与局部收敛特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI方法在复杂机器人系统中取得了实证成功，但其理论基础尚不充分，缺乏对其算法本质和收敛行为的深入理解。

Method: 将MPPI重新解释为EM算法在概率推断最优控制问题中的特例，提出广义EM-MPPI框架；分析局部收敛率与后验轨迹分布协方差及探索分布的关系；针对指数族分布，在对数配分函数强凸条件下证明对数似然的充分增长性质；对高斯MPPI进行特例分析。

Result: 建立了MPPI与EM算法的理论联系，给出了广义EM-MPPI框架的收敛性分析，包括局部收敛率的定量刻画以及指数族分布下的充分增长性质，并获得了高斯MPPI的全局与局部收敛显式特征。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上统一了MPPI与EM算法，为MPPI的改进和应用提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is a powerful sampling-based method for solving stochastic optimal control problems and has enabled real-time control in complex robotic systems. Despite its empirical success, its theoretical understanding remains limited. In this work, we show that MPPI can be interpreted as a special case of the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm applied to a probabilistic inference formulation of optimal control. This perspective leads to a generalized EM-MPPI framework that extends MPPI beyond the commonly used Gaussian parameterization. We analyze the convergence behavior of this algorithm and characterize the local convergence rate in terms of the covariance of the posterior trajectory distribution and the exploration distribution. For exponential-family distributions, we establish a sufficient increase property of the log-likelihood when the log-partition function is strongly convex. Specializing the analysis to Gaussian MPPI yields explicit global and local convergence characterizations. The code for the experiments will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [144] [Benchmarking Recursive-Collapse Warning Claims Under Matched False-Positive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00329)
*David Mullett*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: Loopzero是一个基于声明边界（claim-bounded）的基准测试框架，用于检验递归系统在崩溃前是否遵循“增益上升、递归持续、多样性下降”的方向性遥测模式。在固定误报率合约下，所有检测器均未达到可接受的工作点，非接受性结果被作为首要的科学结论报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归系统可能在表面失败发生之前就进入类似崩溃的状态（自我强化放大、持续递归、多样性收窄但内部退化加剧），现有方法缺乏可复现、可证伪的基准框架来评估递归崩溃预警声明的有效性。

Method: 提出Loopzero基准框架，核心是方向性遥测模式（增益G、递归持续性p、多样性δ），声明边界用Lean形式化指定。在两个冻结的公开基准（Volmageddon 2018/COVID MWCB 2020市场数据、MovieLens-25M推荐回放）上，在锁定等误报率合约（FP∈[0.03,0.07]）下评估检测器性能。

Result: 标准比较器和Loopzero预注册的分位数检测器均未达到可接受的工作点。方向性证伪对齐在两个基准上成立（但存在相邻时间窗口和行级限制）。Shumailov等人(2024)的LLM训练轨迹数据与模式方向一致。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个可复现、可证伪的基准框架，在明确的预警预算合约下评估递归崩溃预警声明，并将非接受性结果作为第一类科学结果报告。

Abstract: Recursive systems can enter collapse-like regimes -- self-reinforcing amplification, persistent recursion, and narrowing diversity that mask accelerating internal degradation -- before overt failure becomes visible. We introduce Loopzero, a claim-bounded benchmark framework for testing whether recursive failures follow a directional telemetry pattern: rising gain (G), recursive persistence (p), and declining diversity ($δ$). The claim boundary is specified in Lean; the Lean artifact does not verify real telemetry, benchmark validity, or detector performance.
  We evaluate the bridge on two frozen public-artifact benchmarks: a segmented public-markets benchmark (Volmageddon 2018, COVID MWCB 2020) and a MovieLens-25M offline deterministic recommender replay. Detectors are evaluated under a locked equal-false-positive contract (FP $\in$ [0.03, 0.07], pre-registered) so all configurations face the same alert budget. Neither tested standard comparators nor Loopzero's pre-registered quantile detector achieved an accepted operating point. Directional witness alignment held on both canonical benchmarks, with adjacent-horizon and row-level limitations disclosed. Digitized Shumailov et al. (2024) LLM training-loop trajectories are directionally consistent with the pattern; matched-FP evaluation in that domain is deferred.
  The contribution is a reproducible, falsifiable benchmark framework for evaluating recursive-collapse warning claims under an explicit alert-budget contract -- non-acceptance reported as a first-class scientific outcome.

</details>


### [145] [Valorisation of Fermentation Side-Stream for Waste-to-Mycoprotein: Nutrient Composition, Metabolic Insights and Process Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00391)
*Nipon Sarmah,Tim Finnigan,Mark Taylor,Tom Vinestock,Ethan Errington,Miao Guo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本研究探究了工业发酵侧流（Fusarium venenatum 发酵离心液）作为真菌生物质生产营养源的可行性。通过成分表征、合成离心液培养基设计及响应面优化（Box-Behnken设计，46组实验），发现氨氮和葡萄糖是影响生物量生产的关键因素。模型预测最大细胞干重为4.17 g/L，实验验证为3.99 g/L，碳转化效率达29.02%。结果表明发酵离心液可有效支撑真菌生长，有助于营养回收并影响真菌蛋白的生化组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发酵衍生的侧流作为可持续蛋白质生产的资源尚未得到充分利用，本研究旨在挖掘工业Fusarium venenatum发酵离心液的营养潜力，以实现营养回收和可持续真菌蛋白生产。

Method: 对工业发酵离心液进行成分表征，设计合成离心液培养基；采用Box-Behnken设计结合响应面法进行46组实验优化；分析氨氮和葡萄糖等关键因素对生物量（细胞干重）的影响，并进行模型预测与实验验证。

Result: 细胞干重范围为0.22–3.87 g/L；模型预测最大CDW为4.17 g/L，实验验证值为3.99 g/L；碳转化效率高达29.02%；氨氮和葡萄糖是主导因素且具有显著非线性效应。

Conclusion: 发酵离心液能够有效支撑真菌生物量生产，在营养回收和可持续真菌蛋白（菌蛋白）生产方面具有潜力，且可通过优化条件影响菌蛋白的生化组成。

Abstract: Fermentation-derived side streams represent an underutilised resource for sustainable protein production. This study investigates the potential of centrate from industrial Fusarium venenatum fermentation as a nutrient source for fungal biomass generation. Following compositional characterisation, a synthetic centrate medium was formulated and evaluated using a Box-Behnken design combined with response surface methodology. Across 46 experimental runs, cell dry weight (CDW) ranged from 0.22 to 3.87 g per liter, demonstrating a strong dependence on nutrient composition. Ammonia and glucose were identified as the dominant factors influencing biomass production, with significant nonlinear effects. The model predicted a maximum CDW of 4.17 g per liter under optimised conditions, which was experimentally validated at 3.99 g per liter. Carbon conversion efficiency reached up to 29.02%, indicating effective substrate utilisation. These findings demonstrate that fermentation-derived centrate can support substantial fungal growth, while highlighting its potential to enhance nutrient recovery and influence the biochemical composition of sustainable mycoprotein.

</details>


### [146] [Traffic Characterization of Event-Triggered Control Systems: A Geometric-Algebraic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00492)
*Tao Chen,Hongju Wang,Wenfeng Hu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文从几何-代数角度刻画事件触发控制系统的触发行为，将事件间时间转换关系的可行性建模为非凸二次约束满足问题并重构为线性锥问题，建立了可行性的充要条件，提出了计算所有可行转换关系的算法，并通过数值仿真展示了可行性随控制参数σ的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件触发控制系统中，事件间时间转换关系的可行性缺乏严格的几何-代数刻画，现有方法难以准确描述可行域，导致系统分析与参数设计不够可靠。

Method: 将非凸二次约束满足问题重构为等价的线性锥问题，获得更清晰的可行域几何描述；在此基础上推导可行性的充要条件，并设计算法计算所有可行转换关系。

Result: 建立了事件间时间转换关系可行性的充要条件，提出了计算全部可行转换关系的算法，数值仿真展示了可行域随参数σ的演化规律及可视化结果。

Conclusion: 该几何-代数框架为事件触发控制系统的参数选择与系统设计提供了可靠的理论依据和直观的视觉洞察。

Abstract: This paper characterizes the triggering behaviors of event-triggered control systems from a geometric-algebraic perspective. We first model the feasibility of inter-event time transition relations as a nonconvex quadratic constraint satisfaction problem and reformulate it as an equivalent linear cone problem, which provides a clearer geometric description of the feasible region, making subsequent analysis more reliable. Building on this formulation, we establish necessary and sufficient conditions that rigorously determine whether a given transition relation is feasible. Based on this condition, we propose an algorithm that computes the set of all feasible transition relations. Numerical simulations further demonstrate how the feasibility of specific transitions evolves with the control parameter σ, with visualizations of the feasible state space offering intuitive insight into parameter selection and system design.

</details>


### [147] [Like Uber or Like Buses? Economic Feasibility Analysis of UAM for Airport Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00504)
*Shangqing Cao,Rishi Kumar Srinivasan,Raja Sengupta,Mark Hansen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段框架，用于分析城市空中交通（UAM）在机场接入场景中的经济可行性。第一阶段通过联合供需可变定价问题评估动态定价的影响，第二阶段优化车队调度与充电决策。以洛杉矶国际机场（LAX）为案例的研究表明，类似网约车（TNC）的动态定价策略可使运营利润提升超过100%，且较长航段具有规模经济效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机场接入是UAM有前景的早期应用场景，但一个核心开放问题是UAM应作为常规定时公交服务（如地铁、铁路）还是作为动态供需匹配的网约车（TNC）模式融入机场交通网络。理解UAM在机场的运营范式对于制定公平有效的监管和管理决策至关重要。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：第一阶段建立联合供需可变定价问题，采用二元Logit模型捕捉旅行时间优势与票价水平之间的权衡；第二阶段将确定的需求输入带非线性充电时间的电动UAM车辆路径问题（eUAMVRP-NL），优化车队调度与充电决策，推导运营收入与成本估算。

Result: 以洛杉矶国际机场（LAX）八辐条垂直起降场网络为案例，结果显示：UAM运营显著受益于类似TNC的管理模式；可变定价策略相比固定定价方案可使运营利润提升超过100%；较长的UAM航段存在规模经济效应。

Conclusion: 动态定价（TNC模式）对UAM运营的经济可行性具有显著优势，且长航段飞行具有成本效益优势。

Abstract: The airport access use case is a promising early-stage application for Urban Air Mobility (UAM). Understanding the operational paradigm of UAM at airports is crucial for making equitable and effective regulatory and management decisions. A central open question is whether UAM will be integrated into the airport transportation network as a conventional scheduled transit service, such as subways and rail, or as a Transportation Network Company (TNC) characterized by dynamic supply-demand matching. In this paper, we propose a two-stage framework for conducting an economic feasibility analysis of UAM networks. In the first stage, we introduce a joint-supply-demand variable pricing problem to evaluate the impact of dynamic pricing on UAM operations. This model uses a binary logit formulation to capture the trade-off between travel time advantages and fare levels. In the second stage, the determined demand is used as input for the Electric Urban Air Mobility Vehicle Routing Problem with Non-linear Charging Time (eUAMVRP-NL), which optimizes fleet scheduling and charging decisions to derive operating revenue and cost estimates. We apply this framework to a case study of the Los Angeles International Airport (LAX) access market with an eight-spoke vertiport network. Our results indicate that UAM operations benefit significantly from TNC-like management; a variable pricing policy can increase operating profits by more than 100\% compared to fixed-pricing schemes. Furthermore, we identify economies of stage length in longer UAM flights.

</details>


### [148] [Wire-Level Interrupt-to-Decision Latency of On-Sensor MLC versus Host Inference on the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano: A Pre-Registered Measurement Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00524)
*Akul Swami,Dnyaneshwar Sonawane*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过精确测量发现，STMicroelectronics LSM6DSOX IMU中嵌入式MLC的决策延迟主要来源于I2C读取协议（三笔事务），而非芯片本身的分类计算，且主机端决策树分类器在所有条件下延迟均低于MLC方案（空闲时2.1倍、I2C争用时2.3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LSM6DSOX的MLC被广泛宣传为低延迟替代方案，但实际线缆级别的决策交付延迟从未被精确测量过。

Method: 使用Saleae Logic Pro 8逻辑分析仪在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上测量三种流水线（主机端决策树、标准MLC bank-switch读取协议、MLC二进制快速变体）在空闲、I2C总线争用和CPU压力三种条件下的中断到决策延迟。实验方案预先注册，包含12次时间戳Zenodo修正，确认性数据收集共4,860次试验，有效4,770次（98.15%）。

Result: 主机流水线在所有条件下中位延迟均低于MLC：空闲时321.7 vs 681.5 μs（快2.1倍），I2C争用时574.5 vs 1,325.4 μs（快2.3倍）。MLC的决策延迟主要来自三笔事务的I2C读取协议，而非硅片分类计算。此外还发现MLC存在可复现的706.5 ms决策节律。

Conclusion: MLC的低延迟优势被I2C读取协议的开销所抵消，在真实系统中主机端推理反而延迟更低；MLC的706.5 ms决策节律对实时应用构成潜在限制。

Abstract: The Machine Learning Core (MLC) embedded in the STMicroelectronics LSM6DSOX IMU is widely cited as a low-latency alternative to host-side inference, yet wire-level decision-delivery latency is rarely measured. Using a Saleae Logic Pro 8 logic analyzer on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, we measured interrupt-to-decision latency (sensor INT1 edge to host decision GPIO) for three pipelines (a host-side decision-tree classifier, the standard MLC bank-switch read protocol, and an MLC binary-fast variant) under idle, I2C bus contention, and CPU stress. The protocol was pre-registered with 12 externally-timestamped Zenodo amendments before confirmatory data collection (4,770 of 4,860 trials included, 98.15%, across nine cells). The host pipeline exhibits lower median latency than the MLC pipeline under all conditions: 321.7 vs 681.5 us at idle (2.1x faster) and 574.5 vs 1,325.4 us under I2C contention (2.3x faster). The three-transaction I2C read protocol, not the silicon's classification, is the dominant latency contributor. We additionally characterize a reproducible 706.5 ms MLC decision cadence that bounds full stimulus-to-decision latency. Code, data, and pre-registration: github.com/akulswami/sensor-mlc-latency.

</details>


### [149] [Handling Control System Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00632)
*Hao Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文阐述了控制理论作为控制科学方法论核心的重要性，强调其兼具"知其所以然"与"知其然"的魅力，并指出控制理论与实际应用的融合是彰显这种魅力的关键。文章重点介绍了面向实际应用的先进控制理论中"处理控制系统最优性"这一基础方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制理论虽在数学意义上至关重要，但其更深层的魅力源于实际应用。本文旨在阐明控制理论（尤其是先进控制理论）面向实际应用时所涉及的基础方面，并聚焦于"处理控制系统最优性"这一核心主题。

Method: 本文为综述性文章，通过梳理控制科学的本质——方法论层面的数学考量，引出控制理论的核心地位。文章以"处理控制系统最优性"为切入点，讨论先进控制理论在实际应用中的基础性问题。

Result: 文章指出控制理论包含"知其所以然"（know-why）和"知其然"（know-how）两个维度的魅力，二者在面向实际应用时得以融合凸显。文章明确将"处理控制系统最优性"列为先进控制理论面向实际应用的若干基础方面之一。

Conclusion: 控制理论是控制科学中最本质的方法论内容，其魅力源于理论与实践的融合。面向实际应用的先进控制理论涉及多个基础方面，其中控制系统最优性问题是一个关键切入点和核心议题。

Abstract: Control science is a core representative of the third industrial revolution and is so important to modern civilization. Control systems are the main subject of control science and may involve many aspects of consideration, such as hardware consideration, software consideration, operation consideration, maintenance consideration, economy consideration, society consideration. However, besides all such aspects of consideration, one aspect that is most essential to the control system is methodology consideration in mathematical sense, knowledge on which is what we refer to as control theory. Besides its importance from the mathematical perspective, control theory is even more charming as it is deeply rooted in practical applications. Charms of control theory consist in both know-why and know-how and it is the fusion of control theory and practical applications that highlights such charms. Control theory for practical applications, especially when somewhat with so-called ``advanced'' flavour, involves several fundamental aspects. This article introduces the Handling Control System Optimality aspect of Advanced Control Theory for Practical Applications [1,2].

</details>


### [150] [Recursive Identification of EIV-ARX Models for Time Varying SISO Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00652)
*Deepanjhan Das,Shankar Narasimhan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种递归算法rARX-DIPCA，用于在线辨识时变EIV-ARX模型，能同时跟踪模型参数、噪声方差、过程阶次和时间延迟，无需存储历史数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EIV-ARX辨识方法多为批处理方式，无法适应时变系统和传感器退化等在线场景，需要能够实时跟踪参数变化且内存高效的递归算法。

Method: 在递归迭代PCA方法基础上，提出rARX-DIPCA算法，通过在线协方差更新递归更新模型参数和噪声方差，仅需指定滞后窗口内的数据，无需存储完整历史数据。

Result: 在基准系统的仿真研究中验证了该方法有效的跟踪性能和实际适用性。

Conclusion: rARX-DIPCA能够在传感器退化和模型系数变化场景下实现实时自适应，同时保持计算效率。

Abstract: This paper proposes a recursive algorithm, rARX-DIPCA, for identifying errors-in-variables autoregressive models with exogenous input (EIV-ARX), for tracking time-varying SISO processes. Building on a recently developed recursive iterative PCA method, the proposed algorithm recursively updates model parameters and noise variances as new measurements arrive, without storing historical data beyond a specified lag window. The method enables real-time adaptation to sensor degradation, and changes in model coefficients. The algorithm simultaneously identifies process order, time delay, and noise variances while maintaining computational efficiency through online covariance updates. Simulation studies on benchmark systems demonstrate effective tracking performance and practical applicability.

</details>


### [151] [State-Space Modelling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00699)
*Hao Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了面向实际应用的先进控制理论中的状态空间建模与分析方面，强调了控制理论在数学方法论和实践应用中的双重魅力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制科学是第三次工业革命的核心代表，对现代文明至关重要。控制系统中方法论（即控制理论）是最本质的考量方面，而控制理论既具有数学视角的重要性，又深深植根于实际应用，其魅力在于知因（know-why）与知法（know-how）的融合。

Method: 本文聚焦于面向实际应用的先进控制理论中的状态空间建模与分析方面，参考文献[1,2]。

Result: 阐明了状态空间建模与分析是面向实际应用的先进控制理论的基本方面之一，强调了控制理论在实际应用中（尤其是具有所谓"先进"风格时）涉及的多个基本方面。

Conclusion: 控制理论的价值在于数学严谨性与实践应用的深度融合，状态空间建模与分析是这一融合的关键组成部分。

Abstract: Control science is a core representative of the third industrial revolution and is so important to modern civilization. Control systems are the main subject of control science and may involve many aspects of consideration, such as hardware consideration, software consideration, operation consideration, maintenance consideration, economy consideration, society consideration. However, besides all such aspects of consideration, one aspect that is most essential to the control system is methodology consideration in mathematical sense, knowledge on which is what we refer to as control theory. Besides its importance from the mathematical perspective, control theory is even more charming as it is deeply rooted in practical applications. Charms of control theory consist in both know-why and know-how and it is the fusion of control theory and practical applications that highlights such charms. Control theory for practical applications, especially when somewhat with so-called ``advanced'' flavour, involves several fundamental aspects. This article introduces the State-Space Modelling and Analysis aspect of Advanced Control Theory for Practical Applications [1,2].

</details>


### [152] [Control of Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00796)
*Eugene Lavretsky*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了大气飞行器（如飞机等飞行平台）飞行控制中的数学基础、前沿技术、技术挑战与开放问题，重点讨论了面向控制应用的降阶建模与飞行仿真关键特征，并从理论与工程角度强调构建并实现具有闭环稳定性、鲁棒性与性能保证的制导与飞行控制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行控制系统的设计需要在理论与工程实践中取得平衡，然而当前在保证闭环稳定性、鲁棒性和性能方面仍面临诸多挑战。本文旨在系统梳理这些问题的数学基础、技术现状与开放难题，推动理论成果向工程实践的转化。

Method: 采用综述性分析方法，围绕飞行控制的数学基础展开，结合降阶建模与飞行仿真技术，从理论和工程两个层面探讨制导与飞行控制系统的设计方法。

Result: 指出了飞行控制领域当前的技术挑战与开放问题，明确了降阶建模与仿真在控制应用中的关键作用，强调了从理论到实践过渡中保证闭环稳定性、鲁棒性和性能的必要性。

Conclusion: 飞行控制系统的成功构建需要综合数学基础、降阶建模、仿真验证以及工程实现，未来的研究方向应聚焦于解决现有开放问题，以实现具有严格保证的飞行控制系统。

Abstract: The main focus of this talk is to present mathematical fundamentals, state-of-the-art, technical challenges and open problems in control of flight for atmospheric vehicles, such as aircraft and other aerial platforms. Reduced order modeling and flight simulation key features for control applications will be discussed. The emphasis is on the theoretical and engineering aspects of creating and transitioning to practice guidance and flight control systems with guarantees of closed-loop stability, robustness and performance.

</details>


### [153] [A Framework for Motion Planning with Temporal Logic Precedence Specifications via Augmented Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00842)
*Shilin You,Gael Luna,Tyler H. Summers*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于增强凸集图（augmented GCS）的轨迹规划框架，通过在自由空间、钥匙和门区域的精确凸划分中编码钥匙-门逻辑约束，同时优化钥匙收集序列和连续轨迹，实现了对STL片段约束下的避障与逻辑先决条件的精确求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹规划方法难以同时处理障碍物避碰和具有逻辑先决条件（如钥匙开门）的复杂任务，需要一种能够联合优化离散逻辑选择与连续轨迹的精确求解方法。

Method: 对自由空间进行精确凸划分，构建包含自由空间、钥匙区和门区连接关系的增强凸集图（GCS），其层次化结构精确编码钥匙-门的先决逻辑；在该图中求解最短路径，同时选择最优钥匙收集序列并计算最优连续轨迹，采用有限Bézier曲线参数化。

Result: 该方法能够精确求解满足避障和钥匙-门逻辑约束的最优轨迹，且解在有限Bézier曲线参数化下是精确的。

Conclusion: 提出的增强GCS框架有效融合了离散逻辑约束与连续空间规划，为含逻辑先决条件的避障轨迹规划提供了精确求解方案。

Abstract: We present a framework for planning trajectories that avoid obstacles and satisfy logical precedence constraints expressed with a fragment of signal temporal logic (STL). Our approach models environments containing obstacles, keys, and doors, where collecting a key unlocks its associated door and potentially opens shorter paths to a goal. Based on an exact convex partitioning of the free space that encodes connectivity among convex free space, key, and door regions, we construct an augmented graph of convex sets (GCS) whose layered structure exactly encodes the key-door precedence logic. A shortest path in the augmented GCS simultaneously selects an optimal key collection sequence and computes an optimal continuous trajectory, providing an exact solution up to a finite Bézier curve parameterization.

</details>


### [154] [Lipschitz-Enforced Machine Learning Framework for Accelerating Transient Stability Analysis of Networked Grid-Interactive Inverters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00883)
*Zhong Liu,Jialin Zheng,Xiaonan Lu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Lipschitz约束的机器学习框架，用于解决现代电力系统中并网逆变器的暂态稳定性分析问题。该框架通过Lipschitz连续性重构稳定认证机制，用确定性代数检查替代计算密集型验证，在网格构网逆变器系统上验证实现5倍以上训练加速，并相比传统方法捕获大30%的吸引域，突破了保守性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暂态稳定性分析方法在处理网络化逆变器资源时面临精度与可扩展性之间的固有权衡，传统解析方法（如LMI和SOS）存在严重的保守性瓶颈，亟需一种兼具可扩展性和理论严谨性的解决方案。

Method: 提出Lipschitz约束机器学习框架：利用Lipschitz连续性重构暂态稳定认证机制，将计算密集的验证过程替换为确定性的高效代数检查，从而绕过传统解析近似的复杂性限制。

Result: 在网络化构网逆变器系统上验证：训练速度提升5倍以上；相比LMI和SOS等传统方法，捕获的吸引域（ROA）扩大高达30%，显著突破传统方法的保守性瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该方法为现代电网中网络化逆变器资源的暂态稳定性分析提供了一种可扩展且理论严谨的解决方案，有效解决了精度与可扩展性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: The growing penetration of grid-connected inverters renders Transient Stability Analysis (TSA) increasingly challenging in modern power systems. Existing TSA methodologies encounter an intrinsic trade-off between accuracy and scalability when dealing with these networked inverter-based resources (IBRs). To bridge this gap, this paper proposes a Lipschitz-enforced machine learning framework that leverages Lipschitz continuity to restructure the transient stability certification mechanism. By replacing computationally intensive verification procedures with a deterministic and efficient algebraic check, the proposed method enables rigorous stability guarantees for complex multi-inverter systems, effectively bypassing the complexity limits of traditional analytical approximations. Validated on networked Grid-Forming (GFM) inverter systems, the proposed framework accelerates the training process by over 5 times compared to existing methods. Notably, the proposed framework substantially outperforms traditional transient stability analysis approaches (e.g., Linear Matrix Inequality and Sum-of-Squares methods) by capturing up to 30\% larger Regions of Attraction (ROA), effectively shattering the conservativeness bottleneck that has long constrained traditional analytical tools. This advancement provides a scalable and theoretically rigorous solution for the TSA of networked IBRs in modern power grids.

</details>


### [155] [Power Grid Infrastructure for AI Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00941)
*Amir Sajadi,Muhy Eddin Za'ter,Maria Vabson,Kyri Baker,Bri-Mathias Hodge*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能快速发展引发科技巨头竞相建设大型数据中心的现象，并分析了这些数据中心对电网规划与运行的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能领域的快速发展促使科技公司大规模建设数据中心，这些设施作为高耗能用户对电力系统产生重大影响，理解这一影响对于电网的可靠规划与稳定运行至关重要。

Method: 本文采用综述性分析方法，梳理了大型数据中心发展现状，并从电网规划与运行两个维度系统性地分析了其带来的技术挑战与影响。

Result: 研究揭示了大型数据中心对电网规划（如负荷预测、容量扩展）和运行（如供需平衡、频率调节、电压稳定性）带来的多方面影响。

Conclusion: 大型数据中心的快速发展对电力系统提出了新的挑战，需要在电网规划中充分考虑其高能耗和快速增长特性，并在运行中应对其对系统稳定性的影响。

Abstract: This article addresses recent advances in artificial intelligence, which have set off an astounding race among technology frontiers to build large data centers. It provides insights into impacts of large data centers on the planning and operation of the power grid.

</details>


### [156] [Data-Driven Min-Max MPC with Integral Quadratic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01194)
*Yifan Xie,Julian Berberich,Frank Allgöwer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于积分二次约束(IQC)的数据驱动min-max模型预测控制(MPC)综合方法，用于存在非线性不确定性的未知系统。利用输入-状态数据和IQC知识对未知系统矩阵进行集合隶属度表征，通过求解两个半定规划(SDP)最小化最坏情况成本上界，迭代获得状态反馈控制律，并证明了闭环系统的指数稳定性和约束满足性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的非线性系统控制缺乏严格的保证，而IQC框架为建模非线性提供了强大工具，因此需要将两者结合以实现具有理论保证的数据驱动控制。

Method: 采用集合隶属度表征未知系统矩阵，结合输入-状态数据与IQC知识；推导两个半定规划(SDP)用于最小化最坏情况成本上界；通过迭代求解SDP获得状态反馈控制律。

Result: 证明了闭环系统指数稳定且满足输入和状态约束；数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动min-max MPC方法能够在存在非线性不确定性时，为未知系统提供具有严格稳定性保证的控制律。

Abstract: Data-driven control of nonlinear systems with rigorous guarantees is a challenging control problem. Integral quadratic constraints (IQCs) provide a powerful framework for modeling nonlinearities. This paper presents a data-driven min-max model predictive control (MPC) synthesis method for unknown systems subject to (nonlinear) uncertainties using the IQC framework. The unknown system matrices are characterized by a set-membership representation using the input-state data and the knowledge of the IQCs. We derive two semidefinite programs (SDPs) that minimize an upper bound on the worst-case cost over all possible system dynamics and uncertainties. By iteratively solving these SDPs, the proposed state-feedback control law is obtained. We further prove that the resulting closed-loop system is exponentially stable and satisfies the input and state constraints. A numerical example demonstrates the validity of the proposed method.

</details>


### [157] [Efficient Numerical Modeling of Near-Field Diffraction in ORIS-Assisted Free-Space Optical Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01195)
*Tuan A. Hoang,Phuc V. Trinh,Shinya Sugiura,Chuyen T. Nguyen,Susumu Ishihara,Thanh V. Pham*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数值框架，用于分析光学可重构智能表面（ORIS）辅助的自由空间光（FSO）通信系统中的近场传播，通过将光场计算重构为空间-频域卷积，采用快速傅里叶变换（FFT）方法在保持精度的同时显著降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ORIS辅助FSO通信系统中的近场传播涉及复杂的衍射效应，难以进行闭式解析分析，且传统的黎曼求和方法因需在光学波长尺度上进行精细空间离散化而导致计算复杂度过高。

Method: 提出一种数值框架，包含两种方法：1）直接黎曼求和方法，提供精确场估计但计算复杂度高；2）将光场计算重构为空间-频域卷积，利用FFT实现高效的近场传播分析。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的FFT方法在保持与黎曼求和方法相当的精度的同时，大幅降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: FFT-based方法可有效解决ORIS辅助FSO近场传播分析中的计算效率瓶颈，为系统设计与性能评估提供了可行的数值工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates near-field propagation in optical reconfigurable intelligent surface (ORIS)-assisted free-space optical (FSO) communication systems. Unlike conventional far-field scenarios, near-field propagation involves complex diffraction effects that hinder tractable closed-form analysis. To address this issue, a numerical framework for evaluating the optical field distribution of ORIS-assisted FSO links is proposed. Specifically, two numerical approaches are considered: direct Riemann-sum evaluation and a fast Fourier transform (FFT)-based method. Although the Riemann sum approach provides accurate field estimation, it incurs extremely high computational complexity due to the fine spatial discretization of the ORIS surface required at optical wavelengths. To improve computational efficiency, the optical-field calculation is reformulated as a convolution in the spatial-frequency domain, enabling efficient FFT-based propagation analysis. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed FFT-based method achieves accuracy comparable to that of the Riemann-sum approach while significantly reducing computational complexity.

</details>


### [158] [Regulating EV Charging Markets for Fairness: Incentives for Pricing and Capacity Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01266)
*Ruiting Wang,Kita Hu,Yitong Yu,Scott Moura*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过博弈论框架研究电动汽车充电站定价与容量的公平性问题，发现无监管竞争会加剧不同人群间的充电设施接入差异，而精心设计的激励措施能在不显著损失效率的前提下减少不公平现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动出行转型需要既高效又社会公平的充电基础设施，但现有研究多关注效率而忽视公平性，因此本文旨在从博弈论视角分析充电市场中的公平问题。

Method: 构建非合作博弈市场模型，充电服务提供商竞争设定价格和容量，用户基于广义成本选择充电站，达到市场均衡；然后将分散市场结果与理想化规划者方案对比，并设计针对性激励措施引导运营商实现更公平的充电桩布局。

Result: 无监管竞争会加剧不同人口群体间充电设施接入的差异；精心校准的激励措施可以在效率损失较小的前提下显著减少不公平现象。

Conclusion: 该框架为政策制定者提供了如何在自由市场动态与更广泛的社会公平目标之间取得平衡的见解。

Abstract: The transition to electric mobility calls for charging infrastructure that is both efficient and socially equitable. This paper examines fairness in electric vehicle (EV) charging station pricing and capacity through a game-theoretic perspective. We model a non-cooperative market in which competing charging service providers set prices and capacities while customers choose stations based on generalized cost, leading to a market equilibrium. We then benchmark this decentralized outcome against an idealized planner solution that jointly optimizes efficiency and equity. To align market outcomes with socially desirable goals, we design targeted incentives that guide operators toward more fair charger placement. Case studies demonstrate that unregulated competition tends to exacerbate disparities in charger access across demographic groups, whereas carefully calibrated incentives can reduce inequities without significant efficiency loss. The framework provides insights for policymakers on reconciling free-market dynamics with the broader societal goals of fairness in electrified mobility systems.

</details>


### [159] [A Koopman Set-Membership Approach for Nonlinear Data-Driven Control with Stability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01378)
*Yifan Xie,Zuxun Xiong,Julian Berberich,Antonis Papachristodoulou,Frank Allgöwer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对未知非线性系统，提出了一种基于Koopman双线性实现的数据驱动控制器设计方法。通过Koopman算子理论将非线性系统表示为带残差项的双线性离散时间系统，利用收集的输入-状态数据对，基于集合隶属描述刻画未知系统矩阵，进而设计有理状态反馈控制器，通过求解平方和(SOS)规划保证系统稳定性，数值算例验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动控制方法在处理未知非线性系统时面临模型复杂、稳定性难以保证等挑战，亟需一种既能有效表征非线性动力学又能提供稳定性保障的数据驱动控制框架。

Method: 1) 基于Koopman算子理论将非线性系统表示为带残差项的双线性离散时间系统；2) 假设残差界已知，利用输入-状态数据对通过集合隶属描述刻画未知系统矩阵；3) 设计有理状态反馈控制器，通过求解平方和(SOS)规划确保对集合内所有双线性系统及残差的稳定性。

Result: 所提方法能够有效稳定未知非线性系统，数值示例验证了控制器的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种结合Koopman理论与集合隶属辨识的数据驱动控制方案，通过SOS规划求解有理反馈控制器，在存在残差的情况下仍能保证稳定性，为未知非线性系统的控制提供了理论保证。

Abstract: This paper proposes a data-driven controller design method for unknown nonlinear systems based on a Koopman bilinear realization. Using Koopman operator theory, the nonlinear system can be represented as a bilinear discrete-time system with a residual error term. The residual error is proportionally bounded by the norm of the lifted state and input, while the system matrices of the bilinear model are unknown. Assuming that bounds on the residual error are available, the unknown system matrices are characterized via a set-membership representation using the collected input-state data pairs of the nonlinear system. A data-driven controller design method is proposed to ensure stability for all bilinear systems within this set-membership description and for all admissible residual errors. More specifically, we design a rational state-feedback controller that stabilizes the bilinear model with residual error and, consequently, the original nonlinear system, by solving a sum-of-squares (SOS) program. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through numerical examples.

</details>


### [160] [Hosting Capacity Assessment and Enhancement for Edge Data Centers in Active Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01407)
*Linhan Fang,Xingpeng Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了配电网对中小型边缘数据中心的接纳能力，识别了三种关键限制机制（电流约束、电压约束和混合约束），并评估了BESS、DDG和STATCOM等灵活资源对提升接纳能力的互补效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘计算和AI驱动工作负载需求的增长，中小型边缘数据中心集成到配电网中变得日益重要，亟需研究配电网对数据中心的接纳能力及其限制机制。

Method: 通过基线分析识别不同候选母线的数据中心接纳能力差异，归纳三种主导限制机制（电流约束、电压约束、混合约束），并评估BESS、DDG和STATCOM三种灵活资源的提升效果。

Result: 数据中心接纳能力因网络拓扑和电气距离在不同母线上差异显著；灵活资源通过有功支持、持续本地发电和无功补偿提供互补效益，有效扩大了数据中心接纳容量。

Conclusion: BESS、DDG和STATCOM等灵活资源可通过互补方式提升配电网对数据中心的接纳能力，为高密度边缘计算负载的集成提供技术支撑。

Abstract: With the increasing demand for edge computing and AI-driven workloads, integrating small and medium-sized edge data centers into distribution networks has become increasingly important. This paper investigates the hosting capacity of distribution networks for data center integration and identifies the key physical mechanisms that limit the maximum allowable data center load. The baseline analysis shows that data center hosting capacity varies significantly across candidate buses due to network topology and electrical distance. Three dominant limiting mechanisms are identified: current-constrained locations, voltage-constrained locations, and mixed-constrained locations where both current loading and voltage deviation jointly affect hosting capacity. To increase the hosting capacity, this study evaluates multiple flexible resources, including battery energy storage systems (BESS), dispatchable distributed generators (DDG), and static synchronous compensators (STATCOM). Numerical results demonstrate that these resources provide complementary benefits through active power support, sustained local generation, and reactive power compensation, effectively expanding data center hosting capacity in distribution systems.

</details>


### [161] [Making Aggregations Reliable: Realizability Guarantees for Battery Fleets with Heterogeneous Power and Energy Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01562)
*Mazen Elsaadany,Mads R. Almassalkhi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可实现的复合电池模型，用于参数异构的聚合BESS集群，通过简单的线性条件保证个体层面的可行性，同时保持计算可处理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚合电池模型常使用凸松弛，但忽略了元件互补性约束，导致调度方案在个体层面不可行；同时，独立调度每个元件在大规模场景下计算成本过高。

Method: 推导了简单的线性条件，使得在基于优先级的控制器下，聚合充放电轨迹能在满足个体功率、能量约束及互补性约束的前提下安全解聚合；将该条件嵌入机组组合优化框架。

Result: 所提模型产生可行调度解；求解时间与系统规模基本无关，优于微观模型的混合整数规划；随着控制粒度的细化，解收敛到最优基准；对功率能量比异构等假设偏离具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提可实现的复合电池模型在保证个体可行性的同时保持了计算可扩展性，为大规模异构BESS集群的优化调度提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Aggregated battery energy storage systems (BESS) enable large fleets of heterogeneous battery elements to participate in system-level optimization and electricity markets. Scheduling each element independently is computationally impractical at scale. While many aggregate battery models rely on convex relaxations, they often ignore element complementarity constraints, leading to dispatch solutions that may be infeasible when implemented on individual battery elements. This paper develops a realizable composite battery model for parameter-heterogeneous BESS fleets that guarantees feasibility at the element-level while preserving computational tractability. We derive simple linear conditions under which aggregate charging and discharging trajectories can be safely disaggregated while respecting individual power limits, energy limits, and complementarity constraints under a priority-based controller. Numerical experiments in a unit-commitment setting demonstrate that the proposed realizable composite battery formulation produces feasible dispatch solutions. Solve times are effectively independent of system size, unlike micro-model mixed-integer formulations. Solutions obtained from the proposed formulation converge to the optimal benchmark as control granularity is refined. Additional studies illustrate the robustness of the framework to moderate violations of key modeling assumptions, including heterogeneous power-to-energy ratios.

</details>


### [162] [Secure RSMA-based Visible Light Networks under Spatial Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01941)
*Hung K. Hoang,Chuyen T. Nguyen,Thang K. Nguyen,Thanh V. Pham,Anh T. Pham*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究基于速率分割多址接入（RSMA）的可见光通信（VLC）系统在内部窃听场景下的安全速率和（SSR）最大化问题，提出基于CCCP和SDR的两种优化算法，并通过信道相似度降低（CSR）聚类策略有效克服高空间相关性导致的安全性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在VLC系统中，内部窃听导致合法用户可能截获他人私密数据，且高空间相关性制约了系统安全性能，现有研究尚未充分解决该问题。

Method: 1) 构建非凸SSR最大化优化问题；2) 采用CCCP和SDR两种方法求解参数化非凸问题；3) 提出信道相似度（CS）作为空间相关性度量指标；4) 提出CSR聚类策略主动降低CS以恢复系统自由度（DoF）。

Result: 两种算法在不同CS水平下均有效；所提CSR聚类策略显著优于现有基线方法，成功克服了高空间相关性导致的安全性能天花板。

Conclusion: CSR聚类策略能够有效提升RSMA-VLC系统在内部窃听场景下的安全速率和性能，突破高空间相关性的性能限制。

Abstract: This paper investigates the secrecy sum rate (SSR) of rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA)-based visible light communication (VLC) systems considering internal eavesdropping, where legitimate users may intercept private data intended for others. We formulate an optimization problem to maximize the SSR of the system, which is inherently non-convex due to the complex coupling of the objective function and constraints. To this end, two different approaches based on the convex-concave procedure (CCCP) and semidefinite relaxation (SDR) are leveraged to solve the non-convex parameterized problem. A central focus of this work is the investigation of channel similarity (CS), which serves as a metric for quantifying spatial correlation, and its impact on SSR performance. To mitigate the performance degradation caused by high spatial correlation, we propose a channel similarity reduction (CSR) clustering strategy that proactively minimizes CS to restore the system's degrees of freedom (DoF). Numerical results are provided to demonstrate the performance of the two proposed algorithms under various levels of CS. More importantly, the findings reveal that our proposed CSR-clustering strategy significantly outperforms existing baselines, effectively overcoming the secrecy performance ceiling caused by high spatial correlation.

</details>


### [163] [Anti-Windup in PID Control: Review, Analysis, and New Tuning Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01959)
*Malena Caparroz,Kristina Soltesz,Tore Hägglund,José Luis Guzmán*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对PI控制FOPDT过程的抗积分饱和策略进行了系统比较，提出了一种结合条件积分与动态反算的新型混合抗饱和方法，并开发了针对负载扰动抑制的回算时间常数系统化整定规则，通过优化研究给出了简单有效的实用公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行器饱和引发的积分器饱和严重退化PID控制系统性能，现有抗饱和策略在实际调参中大多依赖启发式方法且次优，缺乏系统化的整定指导。

Method: 进行了涵盖动态/瞬时反算、条件积分及适配方案的综合比较研究；提出条件积分与动态反算相结合的混合抗饱和策略；基于大量优化研究建立回算跟踪时间常数的系统化整定规则，考虑饱和比、控制器激进程度和扰动特性。

Result: 所提方法在扰动抑制场景中显著优于常用启发式规则，所导出的整定公式简单有效且接近最优性能。

Conclusion: 本文为工业应用中选择和调参抗饱和策略提供了清晰实用的建议，所开发的系统化整定规则无需复杂计算即可实现近优性能。

Abstract: Actuator saturation is a fundamental nonlinearity that significantly degrades the performance of PID-controlled systems by inducing integrator windup, leading to overshoot, slow recovery, and even instability. Although numerous anti-windup strategies have been proposed, their practical tuning remains largely heuristic and suboptimal in many industrial scenarios. This paper presents a comprehensive comparative study of classical and advanced anti-windup techniques for PI-controlled first-order-plus-dead-time (FOPDT) processes under a wide range of operating conditions. The analysis includes dynamic and instantaneous back-calculation, conditional integration, and adapted schemes. In addition, a novel hybrid anti-windup strategy is proposed, combining conditional integration with dynamic back-calculation to improve responsiveness during saturation, whilst preserving smooth recovery dynamics. Moreover, a key contribution of this work is the development of systematic tuning rules for the tracking time constant in back-calculation schemes, specifically optimised for load-disturbance rejection. These rules are derived from an extensive optimisation study that considers the saturation ratio, controller aggressiveness, and disturbance characteristics. The resulting guidelines provide simple yet effective formulas that achieve near-optimal performance without requiring complex computations. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed methods significantly outperform commonly used heuristic rules, particularly in disturbance rejection scenarios, and provide clear, practical recommendations for selecting and tuning anti-windup strategies in industrial applications.

</details>


### [164] [AI-Based KPI Prediction Methods in Future 6G Networks: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01972)
*Niloofar Mehrnia,Gourav Prateek Sharma,Samie Mostafavi,Andreas Johnsson,Sinem Coleri,Carlo Fischione,James Gross*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该综述首次系统性地分类总结了面向未来6G网络的KPI预测方法，涵盖从传统统计模型到深度学习/强化学习的各类技术，并探讨了数据收集、部署挑战及开放研究方向，为预测性网络自动化提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G向5G-Advanced及6G演进要求前所未有的网络性能（容量、时延、覆盖、可靠性等），传统反应式网络管理已无法满足需求，亟需基于数据驱动的预测性方法。

Method: 提出多维分类法，按KPI类型、数据来源、协议栈层级、预测时间跨度、模型族和预测目标对现有方法进行系统分类与综述。

Result: 覆盖从经典统计模型到深度学习、强化学习等多种代表性方法，分析了数据收集与学习架构等使能系统方面，并指出了数据可用性、可扩展性、隐私、可持续性等部署挑战。

Conclusion: 为研究人员和从业者提供了KPI预测领域的结构化理解，并给出了未来6G系统中预测性网络自动化的研究路线图，强调新KPI定义、概率性预测和可解释预测等开放方向。

Abstract: The evolution from 5G to 5G-Advanced and the vision of 6G demand unprecedented levels of network performance, in which meeting stringent network Key Performance Indicators (KPIs), including capacity, latency, coverage, and reliability, is critical to supporting emerging applications such as autonomous driving, industrial automation, and immersive communications. Traditional reactive network management is insufficient in this context, driving the need for predictive, data-driven approaches. Machine Learning (ML) has emerged as a key enabler, enabling the forecasting of KPI trends from diverse data sources and thereby enabling proactive, AI-native automation in mobile networks. This survey provides the first comprehensive and systematic review of data-driven KPI prediction methods for future 6G networks. We introduce a multi-dimensional taxonomy that classifies prediction approaches by KPI type, data source, the network protocol stack at which the KPI is predicted, prediction horizon, model family, and prediction objective. Using this taxonomy, we analyze the state of the art across various KPIs, highlighting representative methods ranging from classical statistical models to deep learning and reinforcement learning. We further discuss enabling system aspects, including data collection and learning architectures, and examine deployment challenges, including data availability, scalability, privacy, and sustainability. Finally, we outline open research directions spanning new KPI definitions, probabilistic and explainable predictions. This survey aims to provide researchers and practitioners with a structured understanding of the KPI prediction landscape and a roadmap toward predictive network automation in future 6G systems.

</details>


### [165] [Detecting Cyber Attacks in Power System AGC Using a Drifted Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02075)
*Mingqiu Du,Xiaozhe Wang,Qinglai Guo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于漂移多元Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程最大似然估计的鲁棒虚假数据注入攻击检测方法，无需负荷可观性假设，在多种攻击场景下实现快速准确检测，优于传统未知输入观测器和LSTM自编码器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动发电控制(AGC)系统依赖通信网络实时测量，易受隐蔽的虚假数据注入攻击(FDIA)威胁，可能导致设备损坏和经济损失。

Method: 利用漂移多元Ornstein-Uhlenbeck(OU)过程的最大似然估计(MLE)进行FDIA检测，不依赖负荷可观性假设。

Result: 在多种网络攻击场景下，所提方法能够准确快速检测复杂FDIA，优于未知输入观测器(UIO)方法（漏检）和LSTM自编码器方法（检测时间过长）。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于OU过程MLE的检测方法能够有效克服现有方法的不足，实现对AGC系统中隐蔽FDIA的快速准确检测。

Abstract: The Automatic Generation Control (AGC) system, reliant on real-time measurements over communication networks, is susceptible to stealthy false data injection attacks (FDIAs), risking equipment damage and economic losses. We propose a robust FDIA detection method using maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) of a drifted multivariate Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) process. Independent of load observability, in various cyberattack scenarios, the proposed FDIA detection method delivers accurate and rapid detection of sophisticated FDIAs, outperforming traditional unknown input observer (UIO) methods, which miss detections, and Long Short-Term Memory Autoencoder (LSTM-AE) approaches, which suffer from prolonged detection times.

</details>


### [166] [Physics-Guided Recurrent State-Space Neural Networks for Multi-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02278)
*Ruiyuan Li,Ajay Seth,Manon Kok*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出PG-RSSNN（物理引导循环状态空间神经网络），通过引入循环结构解决现有物理引导模型在多步预测中的梯度消失和数值发散问题，在多种物理系统上相比纯黑盒模型和纯物理模型表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统状态空间模型基于物理知识但多步预测精度差，而纯黑盒深度学习方法依赖大数据且忽略物理知识，现有物理引导的循环结构存在梯度消失和数值发散风险。

Method: 提出PG-RSSNN，该网络采用循环结构（recurrent structures）使得非饱和激活函数能在多步预测中正常使用，从而缓解梯度消失并消除状态估计反馈回路中的数值发散风险。

Result: 在线性状态空间模型（含高斯噪声）、机械臂、级联水箱系统等多个物理模型不完美场景下，PG-RSSNN在训练稳定性和多步预测精度上均优于纯黑盒神经网络和纯物理模型，尤其在训练数据有限和物理模型部分已知时效果显著。

Conclusion: PG-RSSNN成功融合物理知识与数据驱动方法，在保持训练稳定性的同时提升多步预测性能，对低数据场景和模型不完备场景具有强鲁棒性。

Abstract: State-space models are traditionally based on physical knowledge, but multi-step predictions from these physical models can be poor due to model inaccuracy. Black-box deep learning has shown promise as an alternative. However, these methods rely on the availability of large datasets and potentially available physical knowledge is neglected. We propose the PG-RSSNN, a physics-guided recurrent state-space neural network that incorporates recurrent structures to enable the use of non-saturating activation functions in multi-step prediction. It mitigates the vanishing gradients and eliminates the risk of numerical divergence in training seen in existing structures that feed back state estimates. Results across multiple systems with various physical model imperfections, from linear state-space models with Gaussian noise to a robotic arm and a cascaded water tank system, show that the proposed PG-RSSNN maintains stable training behavior, and improves multi-step predictions, as compared with black-box neural networks and physics-only models, even with limited training data and when physical models are only partially known.

</details>
